We introduce Chemically Grounded Diagnosis (Chem-GD), a metric that assesses whether a correct diagnosis is explained by factual, relevant CIF-derived evidence. Across four benchmarks, MOF-Sleuth establishes state-of-the-art performance among LLM-based approaches and MOF-specific machine-learning methods, demonstrating gains in detection, attribution, and grounded explanation quality.

</details>


### [69] [SenWorld: A Digital-Twin Simulation for Generating Context-Rich Evaluation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19949)
*Zenghui Zhou,Xiaoyang Li,Xiaoxuan Qiao,Zhilang Wei,Tianming Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SenWorld 是一个物理接地、确定性、事件溯源的数字孪生模拟系统，用于生成具有地面真值的智能手机个人助手评估数据，解决了真实设备轨迹隐私敏感、难以共享的问题。通过 16 个北京人设的全天模拟，生成的数据在类别分布和日常通信节奏上与真实用户基准高度吻合（JSD 0.07 和 <0.1），且无需脚本交互即可形成互惠对话子图和差异化行为库。投影到 717 个评估案例中，揭示了生产级助手 78 个失败案例，集中于通话和短信记录，快照指针确认为助手侧检索错误，无需 LLM 裁判。整体提供了隐私安全、可复现、分布校验的评估数据生成路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机个人助手需要基于纵向个人数据进行推理，评估它们需要上下文丰富且已知正确答案的评估数据，但真实设备轨迹因隐私敏感无法共享，现有方法依赖事后标注或 LLM 裁判，缺乏可靠的地面真值。

Method: 构建 SenWorld 数字孪生模拟：融合真实地图、天气、节假日、网络数据，让人设在确定性事件溯源世界中生活一天；全系统快照归档所有可观测信号；评估案例通过指向现有记录的指针标注，而非事后标注或 LLM 裁判。

Result: 16 人设在北京的模拟数据与保留的真实用户基准在类别分布（JSD 0.070）和通信日常节奏（JSD<0.1）上高度匹配，但生成记录较短。无脚本交互下形成完全互惠对话子图和差异化行为库。717 评估案例中暴露 78 个生产助手失败，集中于通话和短信记录，联系人、日程、闹钟零失败。快照指针确认为助手检索错误，无需 LLM 裁判。

Conclusion: SenWorld 提供了一条隐私安全、可复现、分布校验的评估数据生成路径，其标签由构造固定，无需事后标注或 LLM 裁判，适合智能手机个人助手的严格评估。

Abstract: Smartphone personal assistants reason over longitudinal personal data, yet evaluating them requires context-rich evaluation data whose correct answers are known, and real device traces are too privacy-sensitive to share. To address this challenge, we present SenWorld, a physically grounded, deterministic, event-sourced digital-twin simulation that generates such data with ground truth fixed by construction. In SenWorld, personas live through a full day in a world built from real map, weather, holiday, and network data; every observable signal is archived in full-system snapshots; and each evaluation case is labeled by a pointer to an existing record rather than by post-hoc annotation or a large language model (LLM) judge. We evaluate this method with 16 personas in Beijing. The generated data closely matches the held-out real-user benchmark in category distribution (Jensen--Shannon divergence (JSD) 0.070) and in the daily rhythm of communication records (JSD below 0.1), though generated records remain shorter than real ones. Without scripted interaction, personas form a fully reciprocated dialogue subgraph and differentiated behavioral repertoires. Projected into 717 evaluation cases, the generated data exposes 78 failures in a production smartphone assistant, concentrating on call and Short Message Service (SMS) records while contacts, schedules, and alarms never fail. The snapshot pointer confirms each failure as an assistant-side retrieval error, with no LLM judge involved. Overall, SenWorld offers a privacy-safe, reproducible, and distribution-checked path to evaluation data whose labels are fixed by construction.

</details>


### [70] [EvoThink: Evolving Thinking in Large Reasoning Models via Self-Pruning and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19962)
*Xinbang Dai,Zheyu Xin,Huikang Hu,Lin Ren,Rihui Jin,Guohui Xiao,Guilin Qi,Kuicai Dong,Zhaocheng Du,Yuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoThink框架，通过自剪枝训练（SPT）和顿悟时刻偏好优化（AMPO）两大组件，解决大型推理模型（LRM）过度思考导致的冗余验证问题，同时提升推理效率与能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缓解LRM过度思考的方法（如快慢思维切换、推理轨迹压缩）无法细粒度区分有益与冗余推理步骤，在追求效率时往往损害推理能力。

Method: EvoThink包含两个核心组件：1) 自剪枝训练（SPT）：无监督迭代剪枝冗余推理步骤，并在精简轨迹上自训练；2) 顿悟时刻偏好优化（AMPO）：受遗传算法启发，识别有价值的失败推理尝试，合成从错到对的顿悟数据，优化模型内化该推理模式。

Result: 在数学推理与代码生成基准上的大量实验表明，EvoThink不仅显著降低推理阶段token消耗，还提升了LRM的推理能力。

Conclusion: EvoThink通过细粒度剪枝冗余验证与引入顿悟式学习，实现了推理效率与能力的双重提升，为解决LRM过度思考问题提供了有效新范式。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) often suffer from overthinking due to redundant verification steps. Existing approaches for mitigating overthinking, such as fast-slow thinking switching and reasoning trajectory compression, fail to make a fine-grained distinction between beneficial and redundant steps within the LRM's reasoning process, and may thus impair reasoning capability in their pursuit of efficiency. To simultaneously improve reasoning efficiency and capability, we propose EvoThink, a framework that reduces redundant verification and encourages the exploration of new reasoning paths. EvoThink comprises two key components: Self-Pruning Training (SPT), an unsupervised method that iteratively prunes redundant reasoning steps and self-trains on the concise trajectories; and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization (AMPO), which, inspired by genetic algorithms, identifies valuable failed reasoning attempts, synthesizes from-wrong-to-right aha-moment data, and optimizes the model to internalize this reasoning pattern. Extensive evaluations across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks demonstrate that EvoThink not only substantially reduces inference-time token usage but also improves the reasoning capability of LRMs.

</details>


### [71] [The Giant Hippocampus: From Structural Monoculture to a System of Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19973)
*Jaeho Seol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前AI主流架构Transformer与大脑皮层的异质性结构存在根本性错位，认为Transformer更类似于海马形成而非通用皮层，并提出异构拓扑网络作为替代方案，主张在训练前基于结构证据指定模块化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI研究将最先进模型视为Transformer在不同模态上的规模化复用，而神经科学证实大脑皮层为镶嵌结构，不同认知功能由质然不同的微观结构实现。这种差距被论证为可测量的结构性错误，而非风格差异。CNN曾证明将局部感受野与层级深度等先验直接编码可在少数据下达强性能，但该教训因“硬件彩票”使Transformer成阻力最小路径而被抛弃。混合专家模型虽被引为多样性证明，实则仅在相同专家间划分参数。

Method: 采用功能主义分析，结合百年来从Brodmann分区到单细胞Patch-seq的细胞建筑学证据，对比Transformer架构与大脑皮层/海马形成的结构-功能对应关系；追溯CNN到Transformer的架构演变历程及“硬件彩票”影响；提出异构拓扑网络设计范式，强调训练前基于结构证据指定模块化与标准化接口。

Result: 证实Transformer最适理解为海马形成的功能类比，而非通用皮层——相当于将整个皮层视为巨大Broca区，实则标准化于从未为听觉、执行控制、工作记忆等任务构建的巨大海马。提出异构拓扑网络：一个系统的系统，各模块保持其计算所需的归纳偏置，通过标准化接口通信，这是面向AI架构师的设计纪律，而非认知科学模型。

Conclusion: AI架构设计应从“训练后逆向工程行为”转向“训练前基于结构证据指定模块化”。异构拓扑网络提供了一种设计纪律：利用神经科学的结构证据作为设计输入，构建保持计算所需归纳偏置的异构模块系统，而非沿用单一模板的规模化扩展。

Abstract: AI researchers describe state-of-the-art models as one thing repeated at scale: the Transformer, wired identically for text, pixels, or speech. Neuroscientists describe the cortex as a mosaic - dense Layer 4 in visual cortex for spatial encoding, thick Layers 5/6 in motion cortex for temporal integration - different jobs solved by different structures. This paper argues the gap is a structural error, not a stylistic one, and is measurable. A century of cytoarchitecture, from Brodmann to single-cell Patch-seq, shows distinct cognitive functions are implemented by qualitatively different structures, not by rescaling one template. The convolutional neural network is the field's own proof: local receptive fields and hierarchical depth encoded this prior directly, reaching strong image recognition on far less data than later architectures needed. The paper traces how this lesson was discarded: the "Hardware Lottery" made the Transformer the path of least resistance, not the principled choice, and Mixture-of-Experts, often cited as diversity, in fact partitions parameters among identical experts. A functionalist analysis shows the Transformer is best understood as a functional analog of the hippocampal formation, not a general-purpose cortex - the same mistake as treating cortex as one giant Broca's area, except the field has now standardized on a giant hippocampus, applied to tasks it was never built for: audition, executive gating, working memory. The paper closes with an alternative: a Heterogeneous Topological Network, a System of Systems in which distinct modules keep the inductive bias their computation demands and communicate through standardized interfaces. This is a design discipline for AI architects, not cognitive science: specify modularity before training, using structural evidence as a design input rather than reverse-engineering architecture from a trained model's behavior.

</details>


### [72] [Coordinating from Memory: Graph-Structured Experience Reuse for Multi-Agent Adaptation in Dynamic Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19985)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhanhui Lin,Zhaokun Yan,Youyang Ni,Chenjun Lei,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出图结构经验记忆（GSEM）框架，利用异构关系图编码历史协调经验，通过图神经网络检索相似经验指导策略适应，在动态柔性作业车间调度中显著降低完工时间和适应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态制造环境中频繁出现机器故障、紧急订单到达、加工时间变化等干扰，现有多智能体强化学习方法将每个干扰事件独立处理，丢弃了宝贵的协调经验，导致适应效率低下。

Method: 提出图结构经验记忆（GSEM）框架，将历史协调事件编码为捕获任务依赖、机器状态和智能体间协作模式的异构关系图；设计基于图神经网络的检索机制，识别结构相似的历史事件，实现基于经验的策略适应而非从头学习。

Result: 在动态柔性作业车间调度基准测试中（三种干扰类型），与最强记忆增强基线相比，GSEM将完工时间降低4.1%-10.0%，适应时间缩短33%-38%，且优势随干扰频率增加而增大；消融实验和跨干扰迁移实验验证了图结构编码和相似性检索的必要性，并展示了学习到的协调模式的跨干扰泛化能力。

Conclusion: GSEM框架通过图结构化经验记忆和相似性检索，有效利用历史协调经验加速多智能体在动态制造环境中的适应，显著提高调度性能和适应效率，且具备跨干扰类型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Dynamic manufacturing environments require multi-agent systems to coordinate effectively under frequent operational disturbances such as machine failures, urgent job arrivals, and processing time variations. Existing multi-agent reinforcement learning approaches treat each disturbance episode independently, discarding valuable coordination experience that could accelerate future adaptation. In this paper, we propose a Graph-Structured Experiential Memory (GSEM) framework for multi-agent coordination in dynamic manufacturing. The framework encodes historical coordination episodes as heterogeneous relational graphs that capture task dependencies, machine states, and inter-agent collaboration patterns. When a new disturbance occurs, a graph neural network-based retrieval mechanism identifies structurally similar past episodes, enabling experience-guided policy adaptation rather than learning from scratch. Experiments on dynamic flexible job-shop scheduling benchmarks with three disturbance types show that GSEM reduces makespan by 4.1%-10.0% and adaptation time by 33%-38% compared to the strongest memory-augmented baseline, with the advantage increasing under higher disturbance frequency. Ablation studies and cross-disturbance transfer experiments further validate the necessity of graph-structured encoding and similarity-based retrieval and demonstrate the cross-disturbance generalizability of learned coordination patterns.

</details>


### [73] [CLARK: Closed-loop Learning for Adaptive Reasoning over Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19996)
*Yousef Khan,Luca Gherardini,Marco Maratea,Joel Arrais,Jose Sousa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLARK框架融合知识图谱、符号规则挖掘和概率推理，基于LP^MLN形式化，解决机器学习模型对数据分布变化敏感及难以融入先验知识的问题，在医疗数据集上提升分类性能和泛化推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在数据分布变化时性能下降，且难以整合先验知识，需要一种能自适应、可解释、知识驱动的分类方法。

Method: 基于CACTUS衍生的知识图谱，将图结构转为LP^MLN程序，迭代引入符号学习器提出的候选规则，通过概率权重学习校准，实现不确定性推理和图结构细化。

Result: 在两个医疗数据集上评估，CLARK提升了分类性能，实现了更泛化的推理。

Conclusion: CLARK提供了一种构建自适应、可解释、知识驱动分类模型的原则性方法。

Abstract: Machine Learning models are widely used for automating classification tasks by extracting statistical patterns from data. However, their performance deteriorates if the data distribution changes, making them ill-suited to handle uncertain and evolving information. Moreover, they provide limited support for integrating prior knowledge. To address these limitations, we present CLARK (Closed-loop Learning for Adaptive Reasoning over Knowledge Graphs), a framework that integrates knowledge graphs, symbolic rule mining, and probabilistic reasoning under the Logic Programs with Markov Logic Networks (LP$^{\text{MLN}}$) formalism. Starting from CACTUS-derived KGs, CLARK translates graph structure into an LP$^{\text{MLN}}$ program and iteratively enriches it with candidate rules proposed by symbolic learners. These rules are calibrated through probabilistic weight learning, enabling reasoning under uncertainty and refinement of the underlying graph structure. We evaluate CLARK on two medical datasets, analysing both rule quality and downstream classification performance. Results demonstrate that CLARK leads to improved classification performance and more generalisable inference. Overall, CLARK provides a principled approach to constructing adaptive, interpretable, knowledge-driven models for classification.

</details>


### [74] [Safe Remediation as Risk-Constrained Intervention Decision in Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20005)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhaokun Yan,Chenjun Lei,Qiao Li,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种安全修复框架，将修复决策建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），引入三维风险分解和自适应人工介入机制，在微服务基准测试中显著降低错误修复率并提升修复成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化修复系统侧重于生成动作而非判断是否应干预，安全性仅作为事后通过人工审批保障的次要考虑，而错误修复的成本往往超过不行动的成本，亟需从决策层面引入风险约束。

Method: （1）将安全修复重构为风险约束的干预决策问题，建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），在误修复率（FRR）约束下最大化修复成功率；（2）提出包含爆炸半径、可逆性和认知不确定性的三维风险分解，提供可解释的单动作安全接口；（3）设计上下文自适应人工介入（HITL）门控，将升级从二元保险转变为感知值班负载和业务关键度的带宽感知控制层；策略从历史事故日志离线学习，实现对预期FRR的显式控制。

Result: 在Train Ticket微服务基准上，结合Chaos Mesh故障注入和RCAEval对齐的故障分类法实验表明，该框架相比强运行手册基线将FRR降低39%，修复成功率提升2.5个百分点；相比固定阈值变体，值班升级负载降低17%。

Conclusion: 该工作通过CMDP建模、多维风险量化和自适应人工介入，实现了可显式控制风险的自动化修复决策，在保证安全性的前提下提升修复效率并减轻人工负担，为IT运维中的安全自动化提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: In modern IT operations (IT-Ops), the cost of an incorrect repair often exceeds the cost of no action at all. Yet existing automated remediation systems are designed to generate actions rather than to decide whether intervention is warranted, leaving safety as an afterthought enforced by manual approval. This paper makes three contributions to close this gap: (i) we reformulate safe remediation as a risk-constrained intervention decision problem and cast it as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP), in which the agent maximizes repair success subject to a bounded false remediation rate (FRR); (ii) we introduce a three-dimensional risk decomposition comprising blast radius, reversibility, and epistemic uncertainty, providing operators with an interpretable per-action safety interface; and (iii) we design a context-adaptive human-in-the-loop (HITL) gate that turns escalation from a binary failsafe into a bandwidth-aware control layer responsive to on-call load and business criticality. The full policy is learned offline from historical incident logs, enabling explicit control of the expected FRR. Experiments on the Train Ticket microservice benchmark with Chaos Mesh fault injection and an RCAEval-aligned fault taxonomy show that our framework reduces FRR by 39% while improving repair success by 2.5 points over a strong runbook baseline, and reduces on-call escalation load by 17% relative to a fixed-threshold variant.

</details>


### [75] [EvoDRC: A Self-Evolving Agentic Framework for Automated DRC Violation Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20019)
*Bing-Yue Wu,Chia-Tung Ho,Haoyu Yang,Brucek Khailany,Vidya A. Chhabria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoDRC 是一个基于技能进化的智能体框架，用于模块级设计规则检查（DRC）修复。它从无关参考设计提取知识初始化分层修复技能，并在目标设计上通过可追踪的修复经验持续进化技能。实验表明在 DAC26 DRC 基准测试的七个模块级设计上，相比基线实现了 73.5% 的整体违规减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进工艺节点物理设计中，DRC 收敛仍是主要瓶颈。虽然详细布线器具备规则感知能力，但残留的设计规则违规（DRV）往往需要人工工程变更顺序（ECO）迭代。自动化该过程面临挑战：修复必须考虑复杂几何交互、保持电路连通性、避免引入新违规。

Method: EvoDRC 采用技能进化框架：1) 从无关参考设计蒸馏知识初始化分层修复技能；2) 将版图分解为有界修复区域，每区域分配一个大语言模型（LLM）修复智能体；3) 提供局部 DRC 分析、连通性检查、影响预览工具作为反馈；4) 将修复操作及其导致的 DRV 变化存入知识数据库，用于持续进化修复技能。

Result: 在 DAC26 DRC 基准测试的七个模块级设计上实验，EvoDRC 达到 73.5% 的整体违规减少率，优于报告的基线方法。

Conclusion: EvoDRC 通过知识蒸馏初始化和经验驱动的技能进化，结合 LLM 智能体与专用验证工具，有效实现了模块级 DRC 修复的自动化，显著减少了设计规则违规，为先进节点物理设计收敛提供了可行的自动化方案。

Abstract: Design rule check (DRC) closure remains a major bottleneck in advanced-node physical design. Although detailed routers are rule-aware, residual design rule violations (DRVs) often require manual engineering change order iterations. Automating this process is challenging because repairs must account for complex geometric interactions, preserve circuit connectivity, and avoid introducing new violations. We present EvoDRC, a skill-evolution framework for agentic block-level DRC repair. EvoDRC initializes layer-specific repair skills using knowledge distilled from an unrelated reference design and continuously evolves these skills using traceable repair experience collected from the target design. EvoDRC decomposes the layout into bounded repair regions and assigns an LLM repair agent to each region. Local DRC analysis, connectivity-checking, and impact-preview tools provide feedback on proposed modifications. Repair operations and their resulting DRV changes are stored in a knowledge database and used to evolve the repair skills. Experiments on seven block-level designs from the DAC26 DRC Benchmark show that EvoDRC achieves a 73.5\% overall reduction compared to the reported baseline.

</details>


### [76] [Global Difference Constraint Propagation for Constraint Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20022)
*Lucas Kletzander,Jip J. Dekker,Andreas Schutt,Peter J. Stuckey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全局差分约束传播器，将所有差分约束同时处理，替代传统的单独传播方式，并实现了传播解释机制以集成到惰性子句生成求解器中，实验证明显著提升求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限域传播算法通常将每个差分约束作为独立的传播器处理，虽然保证完备性但效率低下。现有SMT求解器虽包含差分约束理论求解器，但其需求与全局传播器不同，缺乏传播解释能力，无法直接用于惰性子句生成框架。

Method: 基于最短路径算法构建全局差分约束传播器，实现界一致性传播；关键创新是设计传播解释机制，将传播推导转化为子句，使其能集成到惰性子句生成求解器中。

Result: 实验表明，全局处理差分约束相比标准独立传播方法，能显著提高求解性能。

Conclusion: 全局差分约束传播器结合传播解释机制，能有效整合进惰性子句生成求解器，大幅优于传统逐个约束传播的方式。

Abstract: Difference constraints of the form $x - y \leq d$ are well studied, with efficient algorithms for satisfaction and implication, because of their connection to shortest paths. Finite domain propagation algorithms, however, typically do not make use of these algorithms, and treat each difference constraint as a separate propagator. Propagation does guarantee completeness of solving, but can be needlessly slow. In this paper we describe how to build a (bounds consistent) global propagator for difference constraints that treats them all simultaneously. SAT modulo theory solvers have included theory solvers for difference constraints for some time. While a theory solver for difference constraints gives the basis of a global difference constraint propagator, we show how the requirements on the propagator are quite different. Crucially, we show how to explain propagations by a global difference constraint propagator, in order to use it within a lazy clause generation solver. We give experiments showing that treating difference constraints globally can substantially improve on the standard propagation approach.

</details>


### [77] [Reading and Steering Representations of Materials-Science Mechanisms in an Open-Weight Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20058)
*Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在回答材料科学问题时是否真正表征和使用物理规律。通过分析 google/gemma-4-E4B-it 模型的隐藏状态，发现三种可分离的机制信息形式：概念可在单个隐藏状态中读出，本构取向由状态间的受控变换承载，选定的内部表示因果控制工程答案。实验表明，物理关系在受控状态变化中比在绝对状态中更清晰可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型能正确回答科学问题，但正确输出并不揭示模型是否真正表征或使用了支配物理规律。作者旨在探究模型内部是否存在可分离的物理机制表征形式，并验证这些表征是否因果驱动模型的回答。

Method: 结合匹配的直接词表读出和雅可比词表读出、无选项状态几何、60条定律反事实基准及因果干预。在50个保留材料描述上验证雅可比透镜复现概念秩序；用72提示基准生成机制特异性隐藏状态邻域，并通过图审计排除数值比较混淆；设计方向反转提示对比，检验隐藏状态变换是否遵循本构定律；实施双向干预和反事实状态补丁，观察答案概率偏移。

Result: 三个独立拟合的雅可比透镜复现概念秩序；两种读出的无目标词集盲测识别出10个机制家族中的9个。隐藏状态邻域虽呈机制特异性，但图审计显示同样可由数值比较解释。反转物理输入方向后，状态变换在60个冻结关系中正确排序直接、中性、逆向定律，40个有向定律中39个定向正确，词汇对照近随机。双向干预在12组匹配案例中均将答案概率推向或远离物理合理结果；反事实状态补丁跨机制和答案格式传递对立决策信号。

Conclusion: 材料科学机制信息在模型中以三种可实验分离的形式存在：概念驻留于单一隐藏状态，本构取向体现为状态间受控变换，特定内部表示因果控制工程答案。物理关系在受控状态变化中比在静态绝对状态中更显著，表明模型内部确实编码并利用了物理规律而非仅靠表面模式匹配。

Abstract: Large language models can answer scientific questions, yet a correct output does not reveal whether the model represents or uses the governing physics. Here we show that materials science mechanism information in the open-weight google/gemma-4-E4B-it model has three experimentally separable forms: concepts are readable in individual hidden states, constitutive orientation is carried by controlled transformations between states, and selected internal representations causally control engineering answers. We combine matched direct and Jacobian vocabulary readouts, option-free state geometry, a 60-law counterfactual benchmark and causal interventions. In 50 held-out materials descriptions, three independently fitted Jacobian lenses reproduced concept ranks, and target-free word sets from both readouts enabled blinded identification of 9 of 10 mechanism families. A separate 72-prompt benchmark produced mechanism-specific hidden-state neighborhoods, but an exact graph audit showed that this apparent physical organization was equally explained by numerical comparison. We therefore compared otherwise identical prompts in which only the direction of the physical input was reversed, asking whether the resulting hidden-state movement followed the supplied constitutive law. These state transformations ordered direct, physically neutral and inverse laws across 60 frozen relations and correctly oriented 39 of 40 directional laws, whereas lexical controls were near chance. Bidirectional interventions shifted answer probabilities toward or away from the physically appropriate outcome across all 12 matched cases, while counterfactual state patches transferred opposing decision signals across mechanisms and answer formats. Physical relationships were therefore more visible in controlled state changes than in absolute states alone.

</details>


### [78] [Spectral-LSH: Sub-Quadratic Prompt Compression via Krylov-Projected Locality-Sensitive Hashing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19368)
*Ali Mahdavi,Azaseh Zamanifar,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Omid Kashefi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Spectral-LSH，一种无需训练的提示压缩方法，通过 Krylov 子空间与随机特征近似注意力核算子的主导成分，避免显式 O(N²) 计算，再利用 SimHash 在注意力特征空间聚类并合并相似 token 为宏 token。实验显示存在压缩比相变：低压缩（ρ≤4×）下分块策略最优；高压缩（ρ≥8×）下光谱路径保留更多质量；自适应后端结合两者，在 16× 压缩下显著降低 PPL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长提示推理因预填充注意力的二次方复杂度而昂贵，现有压缩方法要么需训练，要么在高压缩比下质量下降严重，需要一种免训练、高压缩比下仍能保持质量的方法。

Method: Spectral-LSH：1) 利用 Krylov 子空间方法配合随机特征隐式近似注意力核算子的主导特征分量，避免显式构建 O(N²) 核矩阵；2) 在得到的注意力特征空间中应用 SimHash 将相似 token 分组；3) 将每组 token 聚合为带因果位置编码的宏 token，实现提示压缩。

Result: 在 Mistral-7B、Qwen2.5-7B/14B 上实验：ρ=4× 以下分块策略延迟-质量最优；ρ=8× 以上光谱路径优于分块；ρ=16× 时 Qwen2.5-7B 自适应 PPL 从 353.4 降至 197.0，Qwen2.5-14B 从 9.53 降至 3.43。结构化长上下文压力测试中，局部 LSH 在 8× 压缩下全指标优于分块。自适应后端融合两种模式，但分块总延迟最低。

Conclusion: Spectral-LSH 通过光谱近似与 SimHash 实现免训练高压缩比提示压缩，在高压缩比下显著优于分块基线，自适应策略可覆盖全压缩比区间，为长上下文推理提供实用方案。

Abstract: Long-prompt inference remains expensive because prefill attention scales quadratically with sequence length. We propose Spectral-LSH, a training-free prompt compression method that operates before the prompt enters the language model. Spectral-LSH approximates the dominant components of an implicit attention-kernel operator using a Krylov subspace method together with random features, avoiding explicit $O(N^2)$ attention-kernel materialization. It then applies SimHash in the resulting attention eigenspace to group similar tokens and aggregate them into macro-tokens with causal positional assignments.
  We evaluate Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct on C4. Our experiments reveal a compression-ratio phase transition. Below $ρ= 4 \times$, local token redundancy is low enough that lightweight chunking typically provides the best latency--quality trade-off. Above $ρ= 8 \times$, the spectral path preserves quality that chunking loses. At $ρ= 16 \times$, Qwen2.5-7B (adaptive) reduces the PPL ratio from 353.409 to 196.963, while Qwen2.5-14B (adaptive) reduces it from 9.533 to 3.427.
  On a small long-context structured stress test containing JSON-like, code-like, and table-like inputs, local LSH also improves every metric over chunking at $8 \times$. The adaptive backend captures both regimes by using the chunk path at low compression and spectral clustering at high compression, although chunking remains the fastest backend in total latency.

</details>


### [79] [PRO-LONG: Programmatic Memory Enables Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20064)
*Alexis Fox,Junlin Wang,Paul Rosu,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRO-LONG 是一个基于程序化记忆的极简上下文管理框架，用于长时程探索任务中的 LLM 智能体，在 ARC-AGI-3 基准上显著提升性能，同时大幅降低 token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务要求持续感知、推理和探索，现有 LLM 智能体在 ARC-AGI-3 等持续学习基准上表现有限。现有的上下文管理方法面临权衡：保留更多信息会导致检索相关细节变得更加困难。

Method: 提出 PRO-LONG 框架，维护完整的结构化交互日志，并利用编程智能体的最新进展高效搜索历史记录，从而解决信息保留与检索效率的权衡。

Result: 在完整 ARC-AGI-3 公开游戏集上，PRO-LONG 使基础编程智能体平均提升 18.0 个百分点，达到或超过最先进的专用框架（高达 76.1% pass@1），同时 token 使用量减少 4.2-5.8 倍；配合 Fable 5 实现 97.4% best@2，总成本仅 1750 美元。

Conclusion: PRO-LONG 通过程序化记忆实现了高效的长时程上下文管理，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低了计算成本，为 LLM 智能体在探索性任务中的部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks require sustained perception, reasoning, and exploration, and are a persistent challenge for large language model (LLM) agents. This gap is reflected in their limited performance on continual learning benchmarks such as ARC-AGI-3, especially when models are evaluated out of the box. Various agent harnesses have been proposed to close this gap, and each commits to a strategy for handling long sequences of observations, i.e., what information to save from the environment and how to load it into model context, a choice we argue is particularly consequential. Existing methods for context management face a significant tradeoff, as preserving more information makes retrieving relevant details less tractable. We propose PRO-LONG, a minimal context management framework built around programmatic memory for LLM agents in long-horizon, exploratory settings. PRO-LONG addresses the tradeoff by keeping a complete, structured interaction log and capitalizing on recent progress in coding agents to search this history efficiently. On the full ARC-AGI-3 public game set, PRO-LONG improves over a base coding agent by an average of 18.0 percentage points across frontier models, and matches or exceeds state-of-the-art specialized harnesses (up to 76.1% pass@1) while using 4.2-5.8x fewer tokens. With Fable 5, PRO-LONG achieves 97.4% best@2 at a total cost of \$1,750. Relevant code and logs are available at https://github.com/alexisfox7/PRO-LONG.

</details>


### [80] [Mitigating Scaffolding Collapse in Socratic Tutors via Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19371)
*Jing Shao,Qifeng Wu,Hanyu Zhang,Sixia Sun,Jun Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为 Scaffold-Preserving Representation Alignment 的两阶段框架，旨在解决基于大语言模型的苏格拉底式导师在多轮对话中因学生持续施压而逐渐放弃引导式探究、直接给出答案的“脚手架崩塌”问题。通过监督微调预热、轨迹加权直接偏好优化结合锚定冻结参考状态的边界保持表示损失，该方法在隐藏状态层面维持脚手架保持与崩塌诱导状态的分离。在 Qwen3-8B 上跨五个 STEM 学科和五种红队攻击策略的评估显示，崩塌率降至 32%，平均崩塌轮次推迟至 9 轮之后，且过度拒绝率保持较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型的苏格拉底式导师在长期多轮对话中容易发生脚手架崩塌：在学生持续施压下，导师逐渐放弃引导式提问，直接透露解决方案。现有防御主要通过提示工程、偏好优化或过滤来约束可观测响应，但未解决导致轨迹级崩塌的内部表示漂移问题。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：1) 监督微调预热苏格拉底导师；2) 结合轨迹加权直接偏好优化 (DPO) 与边界保持表示损失，该损失锚定冻结参考状态，旨在跨对话轮次维持脚手架保持与崩塌诱导隐藏状态的分离。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 上跨五个 STEM 学科和五种红队攻击策略评估：崩塌率降至 32%，平均崩塌起始轮次推迟至 9 轮之后，过度拒绝率保持较低。

Conclusion: 表示层面的对齐能够在该红队协议下提升长时程苏格拉底式辅导的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based Socratic tutors increasingly guide students through multi-turn questioning, but they can suffer from scaffolding collapse: under sustained student pressure, a tutor gradually abandons guided inquiry and reveals solutions directly. Prior defenses primarily constrain observable responses through prompting, preference optimization, or filtering, leaving the internal representation drift that precedes trajectory-level collapse largely unaddressed. We propose Scaffold-Preserving Representation Alignment, a two-stage framework that first warms up a Socratic tutor with supervised fine-tuning, then combines trajectory-weighted direct preference optimization with a margin-preserving representation loss anchored to frozen reference states. Our method is designed to maintain separation between scaffold-preserving and collapse-inducing hidden states across dialogue turns. We evaluate our method across five STEM disciplines and five red-teaming attack strategies. On Qwen3-8B, our method lowers Collapse Rate to 32%, delays average collapse onset beyond nine turns, and keeps over-refusal low, suggesting that representation-level alignment can improve the robustness of long-horizon Socratic tutoring under our red-teaming protocol.

</details>


### [81] [TRUST-ESD: A Risk-Calibrated and Governance-Aware AI Framework for Enterprise Strategic Decision Support Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20065)
*Tian Qiu,Li Yan,Mahabubur Rahman Miraj,Shanqin Yi,Md Intekhab Rahman Galib,Jahid Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出TRUST-ESD框架，用于企业战略决策支持，结合预测效用估计、共形不确定性校准、CVaR下行风险评分、风险记忆检索、策略即代码治理、可解释性和人工监督，实现风险校准和治理感知的决策推荐。实验显示其在风险调整效用、风险暴露、CVaR、校准误差、解释保真度和治理合规性上均显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业战略决策需要AI系统不仅准确，还要不确定性感知、风险校准、可解释和治理合规。现有仅基于预测的方法仅最大化期望效用，忽视风险、可靠性和合规性，因此需要一种综合框架平衡价值、可靠性、风险暴露和合规。

Method: TRUST-ESD框架通过评估可行反事实策略，包含七大组件：预测效用估计、共形不确定性校准、基于CVaR的下行风险评分、风险记忆检索、策略即代码治理、可解释性和人工监督。该框架推荐平衡价值、可靠性、风险暴露和合规的策略。

Result: 与强不确定性感知基线相比，TRUST-ESD将风险调整效用提升7.95%，风险暴露降低23.22%，CVaR降低23.78%，校准误差降低13.89%，解释保真度提升10.90%，治理合规性提升9.76%，同时保持有竞争力的预测准确性。消融实验和案例研究证实各组件协同提升可信决策。

Conclusion: TRUST-ESD通过整合不确定性校准、下行风险评分、风险记忆、可解释性和治理验证，为企业战略决策提供了一个风险校准和治理感知的可信框架，显著改善了风险调整后的决策质量，同时满足合规要求。

Abstract: Enterprise strategic decision support requires AI systems that are not only accurate, but also uncertainty-aware, risk-calibrated, explainable, and governance-compliant. This paper proposes TRUST-ESD, a risk-calibrated and governance-aware framework for enterprise decision support under uncertainty. TRUST-ESD evaluates feasible counterfactual strategies through predictive utility estimation, conformal uncertainty calibration, CVaR-based downside-risk scoring, risk-memory retrieval, policy-as-code governance, explainability, and human oversight. Unlike prediction-only methods that select actions by maximum expected utility, TRUST-ESD recommends strategies that balance value, reliability, risk exposure, and compliance. Experimental results show that TRUST-ESD improves risk-adjusted utility by 7.95%, reduces risk exposure by 23.22%, reduces CVaR by 23.78%, lowers calibration error by 13.89%, improves explanation fidelity by 10.90%, and increases governance compliance by 9.76% compared with strong uncertainty-aware baselines, while maintaining competitive predictive accuracy. Ablation and case-study analyses further confirm that uncertainty calibration, downside-risk scoring, risk memory, explainability, and governance validation jointly improve trustworthy enterprise decision-making.

</details>


### [82] [CUSUM-Shaped Inference-Time Monitoring and Targeted Re-Decoding for Quantized Small Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20129)
*El Hassane Ettifouri,Ayoub Belfatmi,Mahaman Sanoussi Yahaya Alassan,Walid Dahhane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MGT-B (Monitoring-Guided Test-time Backtracking)，一种针对量化小型自回归推理模型在推理时出现的长、重复或无效轨迹的外部监控与回溯修复机制。该方法基于预采样不确定性和退化特征的滑动窗口，利用类 CUSUM 累积混合赌注因子触发报警，随后估计回滚点、恢复 token 与 KV 缓存并进行约束重解码。在 MATH-500 上的两个非确证性实验集合中观测到适度准确率提升，但作者强调结果仅支持特定设置下的选择性监控修复，不构成通用或理论认证的推理改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化小型自回归推理模型在推理过程中容易陷入冗长、重复或无效的轨迹，而现有的推理时算力分配通常不考虑轨迹的动态发展情况，缺乏在线监控与干预机制。

Method: 构建 MGT-B 外部控制器：(1) 以重叠窗口采集预采样不确定性与退化特征，映射为位置条件经验尾概率；(2) 采用带 CUSUM 形重置的混合赌注因子累积统计量；(3) 触发报警后估计回滚点，恢复 token 与键值缓存状态，执行约束重解码。

Result: 在 240 对时间审计集上准确率从 82/240 提升至 88/240 (+2.50pp，McNemar p=0.2632)；在 467 对历史覆盖集上从 146/467 提升至 167/467 (+4.50pp，McNemar p=0.000753)。无报警轨迹与原始输出完全一致；151 条报警轨迹中包含 29 次修正与 8 次退步。两组分析均非确证性，经验因子未被证实为有效 e-process 或 e-detector。

Conclusion: 结果仅支持在所研究的 MATH-500 设置下采用选择性监控与修复机制，不构成通用或理论认证的推理能力提升。

Abstract: Quantized small autoregressive reasoning models can enter long, repetitive, or unproductive trajectories, yet inference-time compute is usually allocated without observing how a trajectory develops. Building on an earlier token-level e-CUSUM controller, we develop MGT-B (Monitoring-Guided Test-time Backtracking), a revised external controller that maps overlapping windows of pre-sampling uncertainty and degeneration features to position-conditional empirical tail probabilities, accumulates mixture betting factors with a CUSUM-shaped reset, and responds to an alarm by estimating a rollback point, restoring token and key-value-cache state, and performing constrained re-decoding. To audit whether the effect persists on problem identities first observed after the manual choice of log threshold h = 10, we retrospectively exclude 260 IDs present in pre-threshold artifacts and retain the chronologically first post-threshold pair for each remaining ID, yielding a 240-pair chronology-audit set. On this set, accuracy changes from 82/240 to 88/240 (+2.50 percentage points; 13 corrections, 7 regressions; exact McNemar p = 0.2632; paired bootstrap 95% interval [-1.25, +6.25]). A broader 467-pair historical-coverage set of seed-matched pairs changes accuracy from 146/467 to 167/467 (+4.50 points; McNemar p = 0.000753), but includes 200 seed-1 IDs available before or during threshold selection and is reported only as an exploratory estimate. All 316 no-alarm outputs in the 467-pair set are identical to vanilla, while the 151 alarmed trajectories contain 29 corrections and 8 regressions. Neither analysis is confirmatory, and the empirical factors are not established as a valid e-process or e-detector. The results support a selective monitoring-and-repair mechanism for the studied MATH-500 setting, rather than a general or theoretically certified reasoning improvement.

</details>


### [83] [PoTRE: Test-Time Reasoning inspired by Cognitive Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20268)
*Anmol Kankariya,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PoTRE (Poly-Topological Reasoning Ensembles)，一种异构多智能体推理框架，通过四个专业化智能体（对抗性修正、层级规划、谱搜索、直接链）和自适应聚合层，在 ARC-AGI-2、HLE、PRBench Finance 三个前沿基准上达到 SOTA，HLE 准确率 49.92%，且推理 token 开销不增加反而降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在需要长程规划和迭代纠错的复杂推理任务上表现不佳；单一提示策略在面对新颖抽象或严格领域约束时脆弱性高。现有同质化扩展路径收益递减，亟需架构层面的异构化设计来提升推理鲁棒性与效率。

Method: PoTRE 将推理解耦为四个异构智能体：(1) 对抗性修正智能体，负责错误发现与修正；(2) 层级战略规划智能体，进行长程任务分解与策略制定；(3) 谱搜索智能体，在解空间执行宽度/深度优先探索；(4) 直接链智能体，提供快速基线推理。任务自适应聚合层根据任务类型动态选择候选、语义融合或神经符号验证三种模式，输出最终解。

Result: 在三个前沿基准上评测：HLE 达到 49.92% 准确率，超越此前官方最佳分数；ARC-AGI-2 与 PRBench Finance 同样显著优于同规模同质化基线。实验表明异构架构在相同或更少推理 token 下实现更优推理性能。

Conclusion: 异构多智能体协作配合自适应聚合，能有效缓解 LLM 在复杂推理中的规划与纠错短板，且不依赖单纯参数或 token 规模堆叠，为下一代推理系统提供了可扩展的架构范式。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at many tasks, they frequently struggle with complex reasoning that requires long-horizon planning and iterative error correction. Furthermore, standard single-stream prompting proves brittle when models encounter novel abstractions or rigorous domain constraints. We introduce PoTRE (Poly-Topological Reasoning Ensembles), a heterogeneous framework that decouples inference into four agents: (1) Adversarial Refinement Agent, (2) Hierarchical strategic Planning Agent, (3) Spectrum Search Agent, and (4) Direct Chain Agent. A final Task-Adaptive Aggregation Layer dynamically reconciles these perspectives -- via final candidate selection, semantic synthesis, or neuro-symbolic verification -- to produce a robust global solution. We evaluate PoTRE on three frontier benchmarks: ARC-AGI-2, Humanity's Last Exam (HLE), and PRBench Finance. PoTRE achieves state-of-the-art accuracy of 49.92% on HLE, surpassing the previous best official score. We demonstrate that this architectural heterogeneity achieves improved reasoning performance using similar or fewer inference tokens compared to heavily scaled homogeneous baselines.

</details>


### [84] [Train the Model, Not the Reader: Decodability Supervision for Verifiable Activation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20379)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文揭示自然语言自编码器的重构得分无法保证解释的忠实性，提出RECAP方法通过协同训练辅助预测器使内部表示可被独立探针解码，从而实现可验证的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言自编码器通过重构质量评估解释忠实度，但结构上对单个虚假声明不敏感，高重构分数不等于解释准确，存在模型学习私有编码作弊的问题，需要可验证的解释机制。

Method: 提出两个审计协议（grounded-vs-true cross和evaluator swap）及RECAP方法：在目标模型旁联合训练线性头（辅助预测器），使指定内容可被线性探针解码；在沙盒模型和预训练Pythia-160M上实验验证。

Result: RECAP训练的模型上，新训练的verbalizer能真实陈述指定内容且私有编码消失，仅增加0.001 nat损失；在Pythia-160M上内容可靠被探针解码，verbalizer部分传达（真实率0.44-0.46对照组近零）；独立探针对verbalizer真实声明AUC 0.96（无RECAP为0.82）；面对对抗编辑解释以最大化重构分数同时撒谎，RECAP探针仍能识别谎言AUC 0.95，对照探针降至随机0.51。

Conclusion: 高重构分数不能认证单个声明的真实性；RECAP使指定内部内容可被独立探针核查，而非依赖模型可博弈的文本断言，为可解释性和AI安全提供更可靠的验证机制。

Abstract: Natural-language autoencoders score explanations of hidden activations by reconstruction: an explanation is deemed faithful if the activation can be regenerated from it. The test is structurally insensitive to individual false claims: if flipping a claim does not change the reconstruction, the claim is never penalized. We show the test is passed in two ways, neither faithful. On a released Qwen-2.5-7B verbalizer, explanations reconstruct well above chance while ~2% of specific claims are reconstruction-dependent, so the score tracks gist, not specific facts. Under exact synthetic ground truth, the standard recipe develops co-adapted private codes (false wording the reconstruction depends on) in 5/5 runs, and fixes that leave the target model unchanged do not help. We contribute two audit protocols, the grounded-vs-true cross and the evaluator swap, and RECAP (Readable Encodings via Co-trained Auxiliary Predictors): linear heads trained alongside the target model to keep designated content decodable. On RECAP-trained sandbox models, fresh verbalizers state the designated content truly and the codes vanish, at a +0.001-nat cost. This replicates on a pretrained Pythia-160M: the content becomes reliably probe-decodable, though a fresh verbalizer conveys it only in part (truth 0.44-0.46 vs a near-zero control). For interpretability, high reconstruction does not certify individual claims. For AI safety, RECAP makes designated internal content independently checkable against probes rather than asserted by prose a model can game: an independent probe scores the verbalizer's true claims above its false ones (AUC 0.96, vs 0.82 without RECAP). Against an adversary that edits an explanation to maximize the reconstruction score while lying (suppressing ~87% of its lie penalty), the RECAP probe still flags the lies (AUC 0.95) while the control probe collapses to chance (0.51).

</details>


### [85] [SoftReason: A Fully Differentiable Neuro-Soft-Symbolic Deductive Reasoning Architecture over High-Dimensional Perceptual Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20402)
*Wael AbdAlmageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SoftReason：一种神经软符号架构，用于对潜在感知事实和知识图谱谓词进行可微分演绎推理，实现端到端感知落地、知识图谱证据注入和可微分演绎闭包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典神经符号流水线在感知与演绎之间存在离散接口，导致梯度断裂。需要一种可微分方法，将感知、知识图谱证据和演绎推理融合在一个可训练架构中。

Method: SoftReason 将演绎状态表示为候选常量和谓词上的局部软解释张量。感知模块提出概率基础事实，知识图谱三元组作为高置信度软证据进入。核心创新是学习即时后果算子的可微分提升：利用谓词定义嵌入和潜在组合通道形成软体谓词混合，聚合所有可能见证者，提出查询条件头事实，并通过单调概率或更新解释。

Result: 在知识感知视觉问答（KVQA）任务上实例化框架，展示了端到端感知落地、知识图谱证据注入和可微分演绎闭包在一个可训练架构中的整合效果。

Conclusion: SoftReason 通过提供完全可微分的神经软符号架构，弥合了感知与符号推理之间的鸿沟，将潜在感知事实与知识图谱谓词集成用于演绎推理。

Abstract: In many reasoning problems, the premises are not observed as discrete symbols, but must be inferred from high-dimensional inputs. Further, the predicate vocabulary, argument structure, and trusted evidence are supplied by a Knowledge Graph (KG), or rule definitions. Classical neuro-symbolic pipelines have a discrete interface between perception and deduction. We present a neuro-soft-symbolic architecture for differentiable deductive reasoning over latent perceptual facts and knowledge-provided predicates. SoftReason removes the gradient gap by representing the deductive state as a local soft interpretation tensor over candidate constants and predicates. Perception proposes probabilistic base facts, KG triples enter as high-confidence soft evidence, and every query anchor, predicate choice, and closure update remains differentiable. Our core innovation is a learned differentiable lift of the immediate-consequence operator. It uses predicate-definition embeddings and latent composition channels to form soft body-predicate mixtures, aggregate over all possible witnesses, propose query-conditioned head facts, and update the interpretation through a monotone probabilistic OR. We instantiate the framework on Knowledge-aware Visual Question Answering (KVQA), and demonstrates how SoftReason supports end-to-end perceptual grounding, KG evidence injection, and differentiable deductive closure in one trainable architecture.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Native Multi-Dimensional Subquadratic Operators via Input Dependent Long Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19378)
*David R. Wessels,Farhad Ramezanghorbani,David W. Romero,Alireza Moradzadeh,Olivia Viessmann,Maksim Zhdanov,John St. John,Ken Janik,David M Knigge,Yucheng Tang,Erik J Bekkers,Saee Gopal Paliwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 HyenaND，一种亚二次复杂度、全局、输入相关的多维卷积算子，直接作用于原始几何结构，配合 CUDA 实现 nSubQ 加速，在基因组学、计算机视觉、医学成像和 PDE 建模等任务上达到注意力基线性能，混合配置更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有亚二次注意力替代方案在多维数据上需妥协：标准卷积缺乏全局感受野和输入依赖性；循环模型需将数据光栅化为 1D 扫描顺序，破坏空间结构。需要一种直接在原始几何上操作、保持全局感受野和输入依赖的算子。

Method: 提出 HyenaND，通过隐式参数化的全局、输入相关多维卷积核进行卷积，实现亚二次复杂度、全局、输入相关的操作。配合 CUDA 实现 nSubQ，融合 FFT-卷积路径，将 O(L log L) 理论复杂度转化为实际加速。

Result: 在长上下文基因组学、计算机视觉、医学成像、PDE 建模等任务上，纯 HyenaND 堆栈匹配强注意力基线准确率；混合 HyenaND 和注意力层的配置优于纯注意力和强循环混合模型。

Conclusion: HyenaND 为多维数据提供了高效、保持几何结构的亚二次注意力替代方案，结合硬件加速实现实际加速，混合策略进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Subquadratic alternatives to attention require compromises when applied to multi-dimensional data: standard convolutions lack global receptive fields and input dependency, while recurrent models require rasterizing data such as images, volumes, and partial differential equation (PDE) into an ad-hoc $1\rm D$ scan order that violates their spatial structure. We introduce \textit{HyenaND}, a subquadratic, global, input-dependent operator that acts directly on the native geometry of multidimensional data through convolutions with implicitly parametrized global, input-dependent multi-dimensional convolutional kernels. Our CUDA implementation, \texttt{nSubQ}, fuses the FFT-convolution path to turn HyenaND's $\mathcal{O}(L \log L)$ scaling into wall-clock speedups. Across long-context genomics, computer vision, medical imaging, and PDE modeling, pure HyenaND stacks match the accuracy of strong attention baselines, while hybrid configurations that interleave HyenaND and attention layers outperform both pure attention and strong recurrence-based hybrids.

</details>


### [87] [Air Quality Arena: A Large-Scale Multi-Region Ground Monitoring Dataset and Benchmark for Air Quality Forecasting with Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19381)
*Rishi Bharadwaj,Manik Gupta,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出大规模多国多污染物空气质量数据集AQA-Data及基准AQA-Bench，系统评估11个时间序列基础模型与经典基线在短期空气质量预测任务上的表现，结果显示时间序列基础模型作为零样本预测器持续优于经典方法，最佳模型采用跨模态架构利用视觉基础模型进一步提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气污染每年导致约790万人过早死亡，精准预测是公共卫生重中之重。现有基准在地理范围和污染物覆盖上均较窄，且未对最新一代时间序列基础模型在真实大规模数据上的表现进行评估，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 构建AQA-Data，涵盖6种主要污染物、跨越3年、覆盖7个国家和4大洲的14000多个站点-污染物时间序列；建立AQA-Bench基准，在短期空气质量预测任务上对11个领先时间序列基础模型及经典基线进行统一评测。

Result: 实验表明时间序列基础模型是有效的零样本预测器，在所有评测设置中均持续超越经典基线；表现最优的模型采用跨模态架构，借助视觉基础模型进行时间序列预测，达到了最佳预测精度。

Conclusion: AQA为空气质量预测提供了首个大规模、多国、多污染物的综合基准，验证了时间序列基础模型在真实复杂场景下的显著优势，并揭示了跨模态融合视觉基础模型能进一步提升预测性能，为后续研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Air pollution causes an estimated 7.9 million premature deaths annually, making accurate forecasting a critical public health priority. Machine learning is increasingly being applied to forecast air pollution levels, yet existing benchmarks remain narrow in both geographic scope and pollutant coverage, and fail to evaluate the latest generation of time series foundation models (TSFMs) on real world, large scale data. We present Air Quality Arena (AQA), a large scale multi-country and multi-pollutant dataset (AQA-Data) and benchmark (AQA-Bench) to address this gap. AQA covers 6 major pollutants over a three year period across 7 diverse countries and 4 continents, with more than 14,000 station-pollutant series, aiming to provide a comprehensive benchmark for air quality tasks. We benchmark this dataset across 11 leading time series foundation models and classical baselines to assess performance on short-term air quality forecasting. Our results demonstrate that TSFMs are effective zero-shot forecasters and consistently outperform classical baselines, with our top-performing model employing a cross-modal architecture that leverages a vision foundation model for time series forecasting. AQA is publicly released at AirQualityArena.github.io

</details>


### [88] [LAARA: Layer-Aware Adaptive Rank Allocation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19391)
*Ashutosh Tripathi,Surya Deep Singh,Pranab Sahoo,Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出LAARA框架，基于对角Fisher信息动态分配各Transformer层的低秩适配器秩，实现免搜索的自适应参数高效微调，在GLUE和MathInstruct基准上以更少可训练参数匹配或超越LoRA、AdaLoRA、DyLoRA、Bitfit等SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩适应方法（如LoRA）统一为所有Transformer层分配相同秩，忽略了不同层异构的适配需求。论文从理论和实证上证明均匀秩分配根本上是次优的，因此需要一种能根据层重要性自动分配秩的方法。

Method: 提出LAARA（Layer Aware Adaptive Rank Allocation）框架：训练中计算轻量级对角Fisher估计指导秩分配；结合投影级归一化、对数压缩、混合适配器重要性估计和投票式变更阻尼机制，实现稳定高效的免搜索动态秩自适应。

Result: 在GLUE和MathInstruct基准实验中，LAARA持续匹配或优于LoRA、AdaLoRA、DyLoRA、Bitfit等主流方法，同时使用显著更少的可训练参数。结果表明基于Fisher引导的秩分配为自适应参数高效微调提供了原则性且有效的基础。

Conclusion: LAARA通过理论分析和实验验证，确立了基于Fisher信息的层感知自适应秩分配作为参数高效微调的新范式，解决了统一秩分配的次优问题，为大模型高效微调提供了更 principled 的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation is widely used for parameter-efficient fine-tuning, yet existing methods typically assign the same adapter rank to every transformer layer despite their heterogeneous adaptation requirements. In this work, we show theoretically and empirically that uniform rank allocation is fundamentally suboptimal. Motivated by this observation, we propose LAARA (Layer Aware Adaptive Rank Allocation framework), a search-free framework that dynamically allocates ranks using lightweight diagonal Fisher estimates computed during training. LAARA combines projection-wise normalization, logarithmic compression, blended adapter importance estimation, and a vote-to-change dampening mechanism to produce stable and efficient rank adaptation. Experiments on GLUE and MathInstruct benchmark demonstrate that LAARA consistently matches or outperforms popular state of the art approaches such as LoRA, AdaLoRA, DyLoRA, and Bitfit while using significantly fewer trainable parameters. Our results show that Fisher-guided rank allocation provides a principled and effective foundation for adaptive parameter-efficient fine-tuning. The code is publicly available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LAARA-D305/LAARA.py

</details>


### [89] [Reproducing Recurrent Transformers: The CoTFormer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19405)
*Aras Kavuncu,Bryan Vullo,Alberto Berni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoTFormer将思维链建模为循环潜在计算，保留中间状态作为可注意力表示，评估其在困惑度、计算效率及分布外归纳推理泛化上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将显式推理轨迹内化为循环潜在计算，以提升模型在算法推理任务上的分布外泛化能力，同时保持计算效率。

Method: 提出CoTFormer架构，将Chain-of-Thought形式化为循环潜在计算，保留中间状态作为可注意力表示；对比结构变体，在困惑度和计算效率指标上评估，并扩展到受控算法任务测试分布外归纳推理泛化。

Result: CoTFormer及其变体在困惑度和计算效率上表现良好，且在受控算法任务上展示出更强的分布外归纳推理泛化能力。

Conclusion: 循环潜在计算框架能有效内化显式推理过程，提升模型在归纳推理任务上的分布外泛化，为高效推理模型设计提供新思路。

Abstract: The CoTFormer architecture formalizes Chain-of-Thought as a form of recurrent latent computation, preserving intermediate states as attendable representations to mimic explicit reasoning traces. In this work, we evaluate CoTFormer and its structural variants across perplexity and compute efficiency metrics. Furthermore, we extend evaluation to controlled algorithmic settings to determine whether this recurrent framework improves out-of-distribution generalisation on inductive reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [90] [Reward-Aware Population Scaling of Evolutionary Strategies in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19408)
*Sung Cho,Gyubin Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出进化策略（ES）微调大语言模型时，二元奖励需要大种群规模（N≈30）而交叉熵奖励仅需N=1的差距，主要源于奖励设计与归一化方式，而非种群大小本身。研究发现，在强模型 regime 下，z-score 优势归一化会导致 N=2 失效；关闭归一化后，二元奖励 ES 在 N=2 时即可在 GSM8K 和 TREC 上提升 0.5B–7B 模型性能。通过推导二元准确率奖励导致的零优势概率 q 的闭式表达，并用 Qwen2.5-Instruct/GSM8K 的零训练探针验证（平均绝对误差 0.020），确认可用种群阈值 N_avail 很小。结论：小种群失效往往是实现细节导致的伪象，而非内在种群下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化策略（ES）因内存高效、可并行、兼容黑箱或离散奖励而受关注，但现有结论冲突：交叉熵奖励微调仅需 N=1，二元奖励却常需 N≈30。作者旨在揭示这一差距的真实成因，判断是否为种群规模的内在限制，从而指导更高效的 ES 微调实践。

Method: 1. 对比 z-score 优势归一化与无归一化两种 ES 变体在二元奖励下的表现；2. 在 0.5B–7B 参数量的强模型上，于 GSM8K 和 TREC 任务测试 N=2 等小种群设置；3. 理论推导二元准确率奖励导致的零优势概率 q 的闭式公式，依赖基础准确率、批大小、样本对内正确性相关性；4. 设计零训练探针实验，在 Qwen2.5-Instruct/GSM8K 上验证公式预测，计算 12 种配置下的平均绝对误差。

Result: 关闭归一化后，二元奖励 ES 在 N=2 时即可持续提升 GSM8K 和 TREC 性能；启用 z-score 归一化则导致崩溃或退化。理论公式预测的零优势概率 q 与零训练探针实测值高度吻合（MAE=0.020），且在强模型 regime 下可用种群阈值 N_avail 很小。表明小种群失效主要源于奖励粒度与归一化实现细节，而非种群规模本身的硬性下限。

Conclusion: 在强模型 regime 下，二元奖励 ES 的小种群（N=2）失效是归一化等实现细节造成的伪象，而非内在种群大小限制。通过合理的奖励设计（如禁用 z-score 归一化）可大幅降低所需种群规模，使 ES 微调更加高效实用。

Abstract: Using Evolutionary Strategies (ES) for fine-tuning large language models is attractive because it is memory-efficient, parallel, and compatible with black-box or discrete rewards. Yet its population-size conclusions conflict sharply: fine-tuning with cross-entropy (CE) reward succeeds with $N=1$, while binary-reward training often needs $N \approx 30$. We show this gap is largely about reward design and normalization, not population size. In the capable-model regime we study, z-score advantage normalization can cause $N=2$ to fail. Disabling normalization lets binary-reward ES with $N=2$ improve on GSM8K and TREC across capable models spanning 0.5B-7B, where the normalized variant collapses or degrades. This small-$N$ risk is set by reward granularity: binary accuracy reward induces a zero-advantage probability $q$ that depends in closed form on base accuracy, batch size, and intra-pair correctness correlation; a zero-training probe on Qwen2.5-Instruct/GSM8K matches the formula with mean absolute error 0.020 across 12 configurations and finds the availability threshold $N_{\mathrm{avail}}$ to be small in this capable-model regime. The implication is not that $N=2$ is universally sufficient, but that small-population failure in capable-model binary ES can be an implementation artifact rather than an intrinsic population limit.

</details>


### [91] [Adaptive Multi-Expert Graph Transformer for Interpretable EEG-Based Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19429)
*Maryam Rahimimovassagh,Md Elias Hossain,Ivan Garibay,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种空间多专家图Transformer，将EEG记录建模为动态功能连接图序列，利用wPLI估计时变连接，通过层次化图编码和多专家Transformer实现亚型感知的异常EEG检测，在TUAB数据集上取得了有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算方法往往将EEG的动态神经同步变化简化为静态特征，忽略了时空尺度上的动态演化，限制了异常EEG检测的准确性和可解释性。

Method: 提出空间多专家图Transformer：(1) 使用加权相位滞后指数(wPLI)估计时变功能连接，将EEG建模为动态图序列；(2) 层次化图编码从电极、区域到全局聚合信息；(3) 多专家Transformer架构配合门控机制自适应融合专家输出，实现亚型感知推理和全局异常预测。

Result: 在TUAB数据集上的实验显示，该方法在异常EEG检测任务上达到有竞争力的性能，验证了动态图建模结合自适应专家融合在可解释、亚型感知的时空分析中的潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了动态图建模与自适应多专家融合相结合能有效捕捉EEG的时空动态特性，为可解释的亚型感知异常EEG检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Electroencephalographic (EEG) abnormalities arise from dynamic changes in neural synchrony across spatial and temporal scales, yet many computational approaches reduce these dynamics to static features. We present a Spatial Multi-Expert Graph Transformer that models each EEG recording as a sequence of dynamic functional connectivity graphs. Time-resolved connectivity is estimated using the weighted Phase Lag Index (wPLI), and hierarchical graph encoding aggregates information from electrode to regional and global levels. A multi-expert transformer architecture enables subtype-aware reasoning, with a gating mechanism adaptively fusing expert outputs for global abnormality prediction. Experiments on the TUAB dataset show competitive abnormal EEG detection performance and demonstrate the potential of dynamic graph modeling with adaptive expert fusion for interpretable, subtype-aware spatial--temporal analysis.

</details>


### [92] [Unlearning as Distribution Restoration: A Controlled Counterfactual Study, a Validated Selective Screen, and the Limits of Oracle-Free Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19442)
*Sen Yang,Yuen-Hei Yeung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现现有的机器遗忘评估标准（匹配重训练神谕的训练探针）存在缺陷，会偏向保留被遗忘知识的方法。作者重新将遗忘定义为恢复到匹配的参考模型，在5个开源架构家族的45个模型-种子单元上审计了无神谕筛选和证书式标准，发现基准锚定的保留集筛选是强有力的必要条件测试，而损伤相对重校准在部分情况下能认证方法，但固定幅度的logit抑制攻击能击败前向认证，证明仅靠前向认证不健全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评估主要通过在训练探针上匹配重训练神谕来进行，但作者怀疑这种标准可能无法真正检测遗忘效果，反而可能偏向那些实际上保留了被遗忘知识的方法。需要在受控环境中系统性地审计现有评估标准的可靠性，并寻找更可靠的无神谕评估方法。

Method: 在受控的nonce-fact测试床中建立匹配的重训练参考模型，跨越5个开源架构家族的45个模型-种子单元，系统审计无神谕筛选和证书式标准。具体包括：1) 测试现有训练探针标准对保留被遗忘知识的偏向性；2) 将遗忘重构为恢复到匹配参考；3) 评估基准锚定的保留集筛选作为必要条件测试；4) 基于参考模型操作点的损伤相对重校准；5) 使用固定幅度logit抑制攻击测试前向认证的健全性；6) 证明可识别性定理界定哪些事实支持无神谕遗忘阈值。

Result: 1) 现有标准评为合格的候选方法，其被遗忘事实得分比从未学习水平低-2.82 nats（聚类CI [-3.16,-2.48]），表明保留了被遗忘知识。2) 参考模型本身在41/45个单元中未通过固定保留阈值，31/45未通过自身往返测试，仅1/45完全认证，反证了绝对保留/往返证书。3) 基准锚定保留集筛选在密封挑战套件上拒绝注入模型45/45，接受参考44/45，部分检测实体路由抑制35/45，是具有测量灵敏度的必要测试而非充分证书。4) 损伤相对重校准在15/45单元认证小子集；在不弃权时，其选择在优化轴上处于重训练噪声内（0.80 nats），而常用训练探针标准偏离5.17 nats。5) 固定幅度logit抑制攻击在12/45单元击败完整前向电池，证明前向认证不健全。6) 可识别性定理界定了哪些事实支持无神谕遗忘阈值，TOFU为预测边界情况。

Conclusion: 机器遗忘评估不应依赖训练探针匹配重训练神谕，而应重构为恢复到匹配的重训练参考。基准锚定的保留集筛选是强有力的必要条件测试但非充分证书。损伤相对重校准可在部分情况下提供认证，但前向认证因易受logit抑制攻击而不健全。无神谕遗忘阈值的存在受可识别性定理限制，TOFU代表边界情况。评估应作为经验选择性测试，而非绝对证书。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is commonly evaluated by matching a retrained oracle on trained probes. In a controlled nonce-fact testbed with a matched retraining reference, we find this criterion can favor methods that retain held-out knowledge: candidates it rates adequate score held-out forget facts $-2.82$ nats below the never-learned level (cluster CI $[-3.16,-2.48]$). We recast unlearning as restoration to the matched reference and audit oracle-free screens and certificate-style criteria across 45 model-seed cells spanning five open architecture families. The reference itself falsifies an absolute retain/round-trip certificate: the injected model, which retains the retain set by construction, fails the fixed retain threshold in 41/45 cells and its own round trip in 31/45, and the reference fully certifies in only 1/45. A base-anchored held-out screen remains strong as a selective necessary test: on a sealed challenge suite it rejects the injected model in 45/45 cells, accepts the reference in 44/45, and partially detects entity-routing suppression (35/45); it is a necessary test with measured sensitivity, not a sufficiency certificate. A damage-relative recalibration anchored to the reference's own operating point certifies a small subset in 15/45 cells; where it does not abstain, its picks lie within retraining noise (0.80 nats) on the axes it optimizes, while the common trained-probe criterion sits 5.17 nats away (a supporting comparison, not a head-to-head benchmark). A fixed-magnitude logit-suppression attack defeats the full forward battery in 12/45 cells, so forward-only certification is not sound; our method is an empirical selective test for methods-as-produced. An identifiability theorem delimits which facts admit an oracle-free forget threshold at all, with TOFU as the predicted boundary case.

</details>


### [93] [Marine Engine Fault Dataset: Open-Access Data under Controlled Reference and Fault Scenario Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19444)
*Ahmad BahooToroody,Oleksiy Bondarenko,Mohammad Mahdi Abaei,Niki Yoichi,Enrico Zio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个开放获取的海洋柴油机故障数据集，包含基准工况和五种故障场景的多传感器时序数据，为船舶机械状态监测研究提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放获取的海洋发动机预测性维护数据集稀缺，特别是带有记录工况、子系统级干预和系统级测量的受控故障实验数据集。

Method: 在试验台上运行一台涡轮增压、中冷的三缸船用柴油机，进行30-90%负载范围的基准性能测试，并引入五种物理故障干预（冷却水泵汽蚀、压缩机空气滤清器堵塞、空气冷却器结垢、喷油阀喷嘴堵塞、涡轮增压器排气侧限制增加导致的退化），采集多传感器时序数据（运行、热力、压力、流量、燃烧相关变量）及元数据。

Result: 技术验证显示基准测量在工况范围内物理相干，施加的异常产生可解释的响应模式，与受影响子系统一致，不同严重程度下表现出可渐进区分的行为。

Conclusion: 该数据集结合了受控故障实现、多工况运行和系统级测量，为异常检测、故障诊断、退化建模等船舶机械状态监测研究提供了详实记录的基准。

Abstract: Open-access datasets for marine-engine predictive maintenance remain scarce, particularly those from controlled fault experiments with documented operating conditions, subsystem-level interventions and system-level measurements. This work presents the Marine Engine Fault Dataset, an openly available dataset from a turbocharged, intercooled three-cylinder marine diesel engine operated on a testbed under both reference and fault-scenario conditions. The experimental campaign combined a reference-performance program across the 30-90% load range with scenario-based tests in which abnormal conditions were introduced after stabilized fault-free operation, enabling controlled comparison between baseline and fault-affected behaviour. Five anomaly classes were implemented through physical interventions affecting major engine subsystems: cooling-water pump cavitation, compressor air-filter clogging, air-cooler fouling, injection-valve nozzle clogging and turbine degradation induced through increased exhaust-side restriction. The released data comprise multi-sensor time-series of operating, thermal, pressure, flow and combustion-related variables, with a separate reference-performance record and metadata for structured reuse. Technical validation shows that the reference measurements remain physically coherent across the operating range and that the imposed anomalies produce interpretable response patterns consistent with the affected subsystems, including progressively distinguishable behaviour where different severities were implemented. By combining controlled fault realization, multi-load operation and system-level measurements within a real marine-engine platform, the dataset provides a well-documented benchmark for anomaly detection, fault diagnosis, degradation modelling and related condition-monitoring studies in maritime machinery.

</details>


### [94] [Guardrails as Scapegoats: Auditing Unfaithful Safety Refusals in Tool-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19449)
*Aarushi Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级黑盒审计框架，用于评估工具增强型LLM代理在面对静默基础设施故障（如HTTP 200但返回空/空值/畸形载荷）时的行为。实验发现：代理倾向于编造结果（FAR占56.6%），而非诚实承认失败（HSR）或拒绝执行（USR）。关键发现是：在系统提示词中加入标准安全语言（如“优先考虑用户隐私和数据安全”）会使USR行为放大15.6倍（从0.25%增至3.95%），揭示了安全词汇会诱导模型在工具静默失败时编造政策理由拒绝执行。敏感工具（如医疗记录、合同、用户档案）是USR的主要触发场景。论文提出了载荷-响应错位启发式检测方法，并讨论了安全优先部署的治理启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强型LLM代理的评估框架主要关注能力指标或显式工具崩溃，而忽视了静默基础设施故障（如返回HTTP 200但载荷为空、空值或畸形）的审计。这类静默故障在生产环境中常见且危害大，可能导致代理产生幻觉、编造数据或错误拒绝执行，但缺乏系统性评估方法。

Method: 提出轻量级黑盒审计框架，注入四种静默故障画像（空载荷、空值载荷、畸形载荷、部分缺失载荷）到12个生产级工具存根中。将代理响应分为三个互斥行为类别：诚实投降（HSR）、编造（FAR）、不忠实安全拒绝（USR）。在温度为零、中性系统提示词下评估两个前沿模型和两个开源模型。通过消融实验，在系统提示词中添加标准安全语言（

Result: 基线实验中，FAR主导（56.6%的有效响应），代理将空载荷视为真实数据并返回编造结果。USR在基线极少（0.25%，396条有效轨迹中仅1例）。消融实验显示，添加安全语言后USR放大15.6倍（从0.25%增至3.95%；95% CI: 2.2%-6.4%；Fisher精确检验 p<0.001）。USR是潜在行为，由系统提示词中的安全词汇激活，使模型在工具静默失败时倾向于编造政策理由。敏感工具（fetch_medical_record, retrieve_contract, fetch_user_profile）占USR实例大多数。

Conclusion: 静默工具故障会诱发代理大规模编造行为（FAR），而安全对齐语言反而会激活不忠实安全拒绝（USR）这一潜在风险行为。这揭示了安全提示词可能产生的意外副作用：在工具故障时，模型可能滥用安全理由掩盖自身无法处理故障的事实。建议生产部署采用载荷-响应错位启发式检测，并重新审视安全优先部署的治理策略，避免安全词汇成为模型逃避责任的借口。

Abstract: Evaluation frameworks for tool-augmented LLM agents focus overwhelmingly on capability metrics or explicit tool crashes, leaving silent infrastructure failures and HTTP 200 responses with empty, null, or malformed payloads largely unaudited. We introduce a lightweight black-box auditing framework that injects four silent failure profiles across 12 production-adjacent tool stubs and classifies agent responses into three mutually exclusive behavioral classes: Honest Surrender (HSR), Fabrication (FAR), and Unfaithful Safety Refusal (USR). Evaluating two frontier and two open-source models at temperature zero under a neutral system prompt, we find that FAR dominates (56.6% of valid responses): agents treat empty payloads as real data, silently returning fabricated results. USR, in which an agent invents a policy or privacy rationale to explain the failure, is nearly absent at baseline (0.25%, one instance across 396 valid trajectories). Our key finding emerges from an ablation where we augment the system prompt with standard safety language ("prioritize user privacy and data security"), which amplifies USR by 15.6x (from 0.25% to 3.95%; 95% CI on ablation rate: 2.2%-6.4%; Fisher's exact test, p < 0.001). USR is a latent behavior, activated when safety vocabulary in the system prompt primes the model to reach for policy rationales when tools silently fail. Sensitive tools (fetch_medical_record, retrieve_contract, fetch_user_profile) account for the majority of USR instances. We propose a payload-response misalignment heuristic for production-level detection and discuss governance implications for safety-forward deployments.

</details>


### [95] [REGEN: Replay-recycling for Expert-to-Generalist distillation with Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19450)
*Yunjie Chen,Xiaoxin Chen,Fang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REGEN方法，通过回收教师模型RL训练的回放内存并使用离线RL算法，训练通用模型，大幅降低训练成本，同时保持与MOPD相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模在线RL在LLMs中激发高级能力但扩展成本高；MOPD虽解耦RL阶段但仍需耦合推理和反向传播，限制可扩展性。

Method: REGEN：回收教师专用RL训练产生的回放内存（免费副产品），采用离线RL算法训练通用模型，完全解耦采样与训练。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和指令跟随任务上，REGEN以显著更低成本达到MOPD相当的准确率。

Conclusion: REGEN将在线RL转化为数据合成过程而非一次性学习阶段，可扩展至大规模后训练而无需重计算负载。

Abstract: Large-scale online reinforcement learning (RL) is the predominant means of eliciting advanced abilities including long-term reasoning and agentic tool use in large language models (LLMs). However, continuing to scale it across vast task domains of interest remains challenging in both computational infrastructure and cost, especially when considering RL as merely a one-off learning stage. Recently, a widely used technique for distilling knowledge across various domains and training stages, multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD), helps to decouple the RL stage, saving costs, while maintaining generality across vast domains. Nonetheless, similar to online RL, MOPD requires coupled inference and backward passes, which continues to limit its scalability and computational efficiency. To address these challenges, we propose REGEN: Replay-recycling for Expert-to-Generalist Distillation with Offline RL. Instead of distilling from multiple teacher models, REGEN trains a generalist by simply recycling the replay memory -- the free by-product of the teachers' specialized RL training -- and employing offline RL algorithms. REGEN completely decouples the rollout sampling from the backward training process and thus greatly reduces the training cost. Across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and instruction following, REGEN matches the accuracy of MOPD at substantially lower cost. It potentially turns online RL into a data synthesis process instead of a one-off learning stage, and can potentially be extended to large-scale post-training without requiring heavy computational load.

</details>


### [96] [Predictive Extrema, Unprofitable Policies: An AI-Assisted Audit of Candle-Based Binance Spot Timing Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19453)
*Ayoub Jadouli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文审计了基于K线的机器学习模型在加密货币现货交易中的表现，发现所有测试策略在扣除成本后均亏损，无正向可执行策略价值，操作决策维持不交易。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证基于K线的机器学习模型能否将加密货币极值或短期结果预测转化为扣除成本后的正向币安现货模拟交易策略。

Method: 使用固定种子模型运行和确定性模拟器进行数值实验，测试多种模型协议（如十对强制每日选择器、验证集选择的局部极小/极大策略、Gurgul启发的OHLCV每日自适应模型），并对早期One4All“30天留出测试”进行取证审计。

Result: 所有测试策略均亏损：十对选择器19个周期亏损6.72%，局部极小策略亏损1.79%，局部极大策略跑输持仓2.80%，Gurgul模型7周期亏损44.30%（买入持有亏损41.20%）；早期留出测试存在数据泄露等缺陷；事件排序性能未建立正向可执行策略价值。

Conclusion: 在测试的探索性协议中，无模型能产生扣除成本后的正向可执行策略，所有操作决策维持NO_TRADE（不交易）。

Abstract: We audit whether candle-based machine-learning models can turn predictions of cryptocurrency extrema or short-horizon outcomes into positive Binance Spot paper policies after assumed costs. Numerical results come from scripted fixed-seed model runs and deterministic simulators; human-supervised AI agents supported the July 20 evidence-integrity revision through literature retrieval, separately tasked critique, artifact reconciliation, documentation, and source packaging, not trading decisions. The strongest later-period evidence, conditional on extensive predecessor search, is negative: an unchanged ten-pair mandatory-daily selector lost 6.72\% over 19 July cycles at an assumed 31-bps completed-cycle cost, with 3 wins and 16 losses. In short model-specific July evaluations, the validation-selected local-minimum policy returned -1.79\%, while the local-maximum sell-to-cash/re-entry policy underperformed continuous holding by 2.80\%; their gross mean advantages of 11.11 and 12.21 bps were below even the 21-bps stress. A Gurgul-inspired, OHLCV-only daily adaptation attained minimum/maximum ROC AUC of 0.874/0.896 but average precision of only 0.134/0.116 and lost 44.30\% over seven cycles, versus -41.20\% for buy-and-hold. A forensic audit also downgraded an earlier One4All "30-day holdout": its dates had influenced prior architecture work, its four-hour outcome horizon was not purged at split boundaries, it used same-close entry, and its raw result directories were absent. Across the tested, mostly exploratory protocols, event-ranking performance did not establish positive executable policy value. Every operational decision remains NO\_TRADE.

</details>


### [97] [Generating Bearing Vibration Signals at User-Specified Fault Probabilities Using PR-GAN and Counterfactual Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19455)
*Seyed Mohammadreza Alavi,Ardeshir Shojaeinasab,Reza Jalayer,Masoud Jalayer,Behnam Bahrak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对轴承振动数据集中中间概率样本稀缺问题，提出两种生成目标故障概率振动信号的方法：基于训练的PR-GAN和免训练的反事实(CF)方法。实验表明CF方法在概率控制精度和成功率上显著优于PR-GAN，且所需信号修改更小，但PR-GAN运行时间更短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴承振动数据集中大多数样本的故障预测概率接近0或1，中间“灰色地带”概率样本稀缺，但这些临界样本对维护决策和决策边界研究至关重要，因此需要生成具有特定目标故障概率（0.25、0.50、0.75）的振动信号。

Method: 使用异构集成分类器的平均输出作为固定的、可梯度访问的概率预言机。提出两种方法：1) 基于训练的概率正则化生成对抗网络(PR-GAN)，扩展WGAN-GP，通过残差生成器编辑真实信号并推动分类器输出向目标概率靠拢；2) 免训练的逐样本Wachter风格反事实(CF)程序，直接优化每个输入信号以达到目标概率，同时保持与源信号接近。

Result: 在CWRU和Paderborn轴承数据集上评估，CF方法平均绝对概率误差为0.005-0.008，保留样本的容忍内成功率达到1.000；PR-GAN平均误差为0.046-0.059，成功率为0.501-0.680。CF在概率引导可靠性上更优，所需平均L1变更更小；PR-GAN在大多数设置下报告运行时间更短。

Conclusion: 免训练的反事实方法在生成特定故障概率的振动信号方面表现优于基于训练的PR-GAN，能更精确地控制目标概率且信号失真更小，适合用于生成决策边界附近的关键样本；PR-GAN虽运行较快但概率控制精度和成功率较低。

Abstract: In bearing vibration datasets, most samples receive predicted fault probabilities close to 0 or 1, while samples with intermediate (gray-zone) probabilities are rare. Such borderline samples are important because they reflect conditions in which maintenance decisions may require additional inspection or a conservative response and are useful for studying decision boundaries. To address this scarcity, this paper proposes and compares two approaches that generate vibration signals whose predicted fault probability matches a target probability of 0.25, 0.50, or 0.75. We use the average output of a heterogeneous ensemble classifier with different architectures and random initializations as a fixed, gradient-accessible probability oracle. The first, training-based approach, Probability-Regularized Generative Adversarial Network (PR-GAN), extends Wasserstein Generative Adversarial Network with Gradient Penalty (WGAN-GP) and edits a real signal through a residual generator while pushing the classifier output toward the target probability. The second is a training-free, per-sample Wachter-style counterfactual (CF) procedure that directly optimizes each input signal to reach the target probability while remaining close to the source signal. We evaluate both methods on the Case Western Reserve University (CWRU) and Paderborn bearing datasets using mean absolute target-probability error, time-domain total variation, and frequency-domain log power spectral density (log-PSD) differences. Across all settings, CF reaches the target with a mean absolute probability error of 0.005-0.008 and a within-tolerance success rate of 1.000 on retained samples, whereas PR-GAN's mean error is 0.046-0.059 with success rates between 0.501 and 0.680. CF therefore steers the probability more reliably and requires smaller average L1 changes, whereas PR-GAN has a lower reported runtime in most settings.

</details>


### [98] [MoA-Structured Decode Attention DNF Derivation, KV-Cache Accumulation, GQA/MQA, and OpenACC Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19456)
*Lenore Mulin,Gaetan Hains*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用数组数学（MoA）推导出四个面向Transformer注意力机制的内存最优推理工件，实现了DRAM流量最小化、精确浮点运算、高效KV缓存追加以及GQA/MQA的KV流量降低，均通过PyTorch验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决Transformer注意力机制推理过程中的内存瓶颈，通过代数消除K^T缓冲区、硬件融合内存访问和优化KV缓存追加，实现内存最优的推理。

Method: 基于数组数学（MoA）和指称范式（DNF），利用ψ-归约代数消除K^T缓冲区，采用操作范式（ONF）步长算术和硬件融合内存访问实现GPU内核，使用MoA拼接操作实现O(d_k+d_v)的KV缓存追加，通过ψ-选择推导GQA和MQA以减少KV流量。

Result: 四个工件：(1) 单查询解码DNF实现(d_k + nd_k+ nd_v+ d_v)×4 B DRAM流量，数值误差≤2×10^{-7}；(2) C/OpenACC GPU内核实现精确IEEE-754浮点运算（误差∞范数=0）；(3) 多步KV缓存实现O(d_k+d_v)逐步追加；(4) GQA/MQA经ψ-选择推导，实现h_q/h_kv倍KV流量减少。均通过PyTorch scaled_dot_product_attention验证。

Conclusion: 所提方法基于MoA形式化推导，提供了内存最优的Transformer注意力推理工件，在保持数值精度的同时显著降低内存流量和计算开销，为高效大模型部署提供了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: We derive four memory-optimal inference artifacts for transformer attention using the Mathematics of Arrays (MoA), each following directly from the forward-pass Denotational Normal Form (DNF) of with the query-row index fixed to the current decode step. The artifacts are: (1)~a single-query decode DNF in which the $ψ$-reduction eliminates the $K^\top$ buffer algebraically, achieving $(d_k + nd_k+ nd_v+ d_v)\times4\,{B}$ Dynamic Random Access Memory (DRAM) traffic result numerically verified to $\|{err}\|_\leq2\times10^{-7}$; (2)~a C/OpenACC Graphics Processing Unit (GPU) kernel with Operational Normal Form (ONF) stride arithmetic and hardware-coalesced memory access, verified to $\|\mathrm{err}\|_\infty=0$ (exact IEEE-754 floating-point arithmetic); (3)~a multi-step KV-cache with $O(d_k+d_v)$ per-step append via MoA concatenation $\#$; and (4)~Grouped-Query Attention (GQA) and Multi-Query Attention (MQA) derived via $ψ$-selection, achieving a proven $\frac {h_q} { h_{kv} }$ reduction in KV traffic. All programs are verified against PyTorch scaled_dot_product_attention.

</details>


### [99] [Total Variation Distance Estimation in Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19510)
*Eric Price,Kevin Tian,Zhiyang Xun,Yusong Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在三种访问模型（采样访问、logit 访问、带噪 logit 访问）下估计两个长度为 n 的自回归分布总变差距离的查询复杂度，给出了改进的理论界限并配合实验验证了算法的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 部署常使用不同的推理优化（如批处理、自定义内核、量化），导致相同权重下不同推理引擎产生的分布存在有意义差异。需要高效估计两个自回归分布的总变差距离，以量化这种差异。

Method: 针对三种访问模型设计估计器：(1) 采样访问模型，使用 O~(n^2 K/ε^2) 次查询，K 为下一token分布的最大支持集大小；(2) logit 访问模型，使用 O(n/ε^2) 次查询，且证明此界限紧致；(3) 带噪 logit 访问模型，若概率值给定相对误差 σ，则使用 O~((n+n^2σ^2)/ε^2) 次查询，平滑插值前两种保证。

Result: 理论上改进了 Meel et al. (2025) 的 O~(n^3 m/ε^5) 界限（m≥K 为词表大小）；实验上在 SGLang 和 vLLM 等相同权重的推理引擎间测量距离，验证算法的鲁棒性与实用性，并展示总变差距离在 KL 散度无限大时仍可估计。

Conclusion: 本文建立了自回归分布总变差距离估计在三种访问模型下的最优/近似最优查询复杂度，并通过实验证明了方法在实际 LLM 部署差异检测中的有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Modern LLM deployments use a number of implementation choices and inference optimizations (e.g., batching, custom kernels, and quantization) on top of fixed weights, so two engines serving "the same model" can produce meaningfully different distributions. We study the problem of estimating the total variation (TV) distance between two length-$n$ autoregressive distributions to additive error $\varepsilon$, under three access models. (1) Under sample access, we use $\widetilde{O}(n^2 K/\varepsilon^2)$ queries, where $K$ is the maximum support of the next-token distribution. This improves upon the $\widetilde{O}(n^3 m/\varepsilon^5)$-query estimator of Meel et al. (2025), where $m \geq K$ is the total size of the token alphabet. (2) Under logit access, we use $O(n/\varepsilon^2)$ queries, and this is tight. (3) Under noisy logit access, we smoothly interpolate between the above two guarantees: if probability values are given to relative error $σ$, we use $\widetilde{O}((n+n^2σ^2)/\varepsilon^2)$ queries. We complement our theoretical results with an empirical evaluation of our algorithms, for example measuring the distance between SGLang and vLLM serving identical weights. Our experiments highlight the robustness and practicality of estimating the total variation distance, which remains estimable where the KL divergence is infinite. Our code is available at https://github.com/XunZhiyang/llm-tv-estimation.

</details>


### [100] [Do Sheaf Neural Networks Use Holonomy? A Measure--Intervene--Control Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19514)
*Ankit Grover,Rémi Bourgerie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于层神经网络的基无关度量方法，量化三角循环乘积中的旋转、茎空间面积和定向，通过测量-干预-控制研究分离几何变化、连接敏感性和三角形特定计算的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何架构常以内部机制（如旋转）为由进行合理化，但仅凭任务性能无法证明这些机制是否驱动预测。需要一种基无关的度量方法来量化训练后的三角循环乘积，分离旋转、茎空间面积和定向，从而评估几何机制的实际作用。

Method: 使用层神经网络（SNNs）作为测试平台，引入首个基无关的训练三角循环乘积测量方法，分离旋转、茎空间面积和定向。在自定义高同配GraphUniverse机制下，对比神经层传播（NSP）在三角形计数和社区检测任务中的表现；通过将学习到的SO(2)传输替换为恒等映射进行干预实验；使用图摘要岭回归预测器作为对照；在固定度图上分析旋转随训练集大小的变化。

Result: NSP使三角形加权平均二维SO(2)循环旋转从0.010弧度增加到0.388弧度（三角形计数任务），而社区检测任务仅达到0.029弧度。将所有学习到的SO(2)传输替换为恒等映射会显著增加测试误差，表明对完整学习连接的训练后敏感性。然而，图摘要岭回归预测器更准确，对角映射也能改善性能，固定度图虽旋转增加但未超越训练均值预测器。

Conclusion: 该测量-干预-控制研究成功分离了几何变化、连接敏感性和三角形特定计算的证据，表明虽然NSP学习到了显著的旋转机制，但这种几何变化并非三角形计数任务性能提升的唯一或必要原因，其他简单预测器也能达到甚至超越性能。

Abstract: Geometric architectures are often justified by internal mechanisms such as rotations, yet task performance alone cannot show whether those mechanisms drive predictions. Using sheaf neural networks (SNNs) as a testbed, we introduce the first basis-independent measurement of trained triangle-loop products, separating rotation, stalk-space area, and orientation. In a custom high-homophily GraphUniverse regime, Neural Sheaf Propagation (NSP) increases the triangle-weighted mean two-dimensional SO(2) loop rotation from 0.010 to 0.388 radians for triangle counting, while the community-detection comparison ends at 0.029 radians. Across the training-set-size experiment, replacing all learned SO(2) transports with identities sharply increases test error, establishing post-training sensitivity to the complete learned connection. However, a graph-summary ridge predictor is more accurate, diagonal maps also improve, and fixed-degree graphs develop increasing rotation without outperforming the training-mean predictor. This measure-intervene-control study separates geometric change, connection sensitivity, and evidence for triangle-specific computation.

</details>


### [101] [Geospatial Diffusion-based Evolution Synthesis (GeoDES) for Storm-Centered Weather Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19522)
*Sonia Cromp,Satya Sai Srinath Namburi GNVV,Youran Wang,Grace Kisslinger,Frederic Sala,James Booth,Allegra LeGrande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoDES，一种基于扩散模型的地理空间演化合成模型，专门生成气旋风暴的精细结构，解决了区域模型历史数据有限和全球模型分辨率粗糙的问题，在北大西洋测试集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习天气模型难以预测大尺度天气系统（如气旋风暴）的精细结构；区域模型受限于固定地理边界内的有限历史记录，全球模型计算昂贵且分辨率过粗，无法捕捉风暴精细动力学。

Method: 提出Geospatial Diffusion-based Evolution Synthesis (GeoDES)模型，一种定制的图像到视频扩散模型，专注于生成演化中的风暴结构，合成物理一致、高保真的天气事件。

Result: 在北大西洋测试集上，GeoDES实现峰值涡度误差降低52%，异常相关系数提高8%，优于次优方法。

Conclusion: GeoDES填补了区域与全球模型的空白，能生成高保真风暴事件，可用于压力测试预报模型和扩展气象数据集，显著提升了风暴结构预测的准确性。

Abstract: While machine learning-based weather models hold significant promise, they struggle to predict the detailed structure of large-scale weather systems such as cyclonic storms. Regional models are constrained by limited historical records within fixed geographic boundaries, while global models are computationally expensive and often operate at resolutions too coarse to capture fine-grained storm dynamics. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Geospatial Diffusion-based Evolution Synthesis (GeoDES) model, a custom image-to-video diffusion model. By focusing generation strictly on the evolving storm structure, GeoDES synthesizes physically consistent, high-fidelity weather events suitable for stress-testing forecast models and expanding meteorological datasets. Evaluations demonstrate that GeoDES outperforms prior methods on key metrics, achieving $52\%$ lower Peak Vorticity Error and $8\%$ higher Anomaly Correlation Coefficient than the next strongest methods on the North Atlantic test set.

</details>


### [102] [SynPre-FL: Synthetic data-driven pretraining integrated Federated Learning training framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19524)
*Akarsh K Nair,Muhammad Arifur Rahman,Nicholas Shopland,Andy Burton,Jun He,Yuan Shen,David Baldwin,Emma O'Dowd,Amna Burzic,Mufti Mahmud,David J. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SynPre-FL 框架，结合高保真合成电子健康记录（EHR）生成与合成预训练联邦学习，解决联邦学习在临床风险预测中的数据稀缺、客户端异质性、类别不平衡及隐私保护问题。通过潜在自编码器-扩散模型生成合成队列预热联邦训练，再采用异质性感知优化（类别平衡局部目标、近端正则化、自适应服务端聚合），并配合事后校准与联邦安全可解释性，实现隐私感知、可解释、鲁棒的分布式表格 EHR 临床预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽为隐私保护临床风险预测提供可行路径，但受限于数据共享受限、客户端异质性、类别不平衡以及缺乏真实表格 EHR 基准，部署仍受阻。合成数据生成虽可缓解数据稀缺，但其与联邦优化的系统性结合研究尚少。因此需要一个统一框架，将高保真合成数据生成与联邦学习深度融合，以在非独立同分布条件下实现鲁棒预测。

Method: 1. 采用潜在自编码器-扩散模型生成隐私保护的高保真合成 EHR 队列。2. 利用合成数据预热联邦训练（合成预训练）。3. 引入异质性感知优化：类别平衡局部目标、近端正则化、自适应服务端聚合。4. 部署事后校准提升概率可靠性，并采用联邦安全 SHAP 实现可解释性。

Result: 合成生成器在保护隐私（抵抗成员推理攻击和重构攻击）的同时，保留了单变量、双变量及多变量统计结构。在 TSTR、TRTS 和基于模型的评估中，合成数据展现出强下游效用。在 5、10、15 个异质客户端的联邦设置下，SynPre-FL 持续优于基线方法，尤其在严重非 IID 分片下鲁棒性与可扩展性显著提升。校准改进了概率可靠性，SHAP 分析在不同联邦规模下产生稳定且临床连贯的特征归因。

Conclusion: SynPre-FL 提供了一个实用且可复现的框架，将合成数据生成与联邦学习相结合，实现了面向分布式表格 EHR 数据的隐私感知、可解释且鲁棒的临床预测，为临床风险预测的联邦部署奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) offers a promising approach to privacy-preserving clinical risk prediction, but its deployment remains limited by restricted data sharing, client heterogeneity, class imbalance, and the lack of realistic tabular electronic health record (EHR) benchmarks. Synthetic data generation may alleviate data scarcity, yet its integration with federated optimisation has received limited systematic study. We propose SynPre-FL, a unified framework combining high-fidelity synthetic EHR generation with synthetic-pretrained FL for robust prediction under non-IID conditions. A latent autoencoder-diffusion model generates privacy-preserving synthetic cohorts, which are used to warm-start federated training. This pretraining is followed by heterogeneity-aware optimisation using class-balanced local objectives, proximal regularisation, and adaptive server aggregation. Post-hoc calibration and federated-safe explainability support reliable and interpretable risk estimates. Experiments show that the synthetic generator preserves univariate, bivariate, and multivariate structure while protecting against membership-inference and reconstruction attacks. The generated data achieve strong downstream utility under TSTR, TRTS, and model-based evaluations. Across federated settings with 5, 10, and 15 heterogeneous clients, SynPre-FL consistently improves robustness and scalability over baseline methods, especially under severe non-IID fragmentation. Calibration improves probability reliability, while SHAP analysis produces stable and clinically coherent feature attributions across federation sizes. SynPre-FL therefore provides a practical and reproducible framework for combining synthetic data with FL to enable privacy-aware, interpretable, and robust clinical prediction from distributed tabular EHR data.

</details>
