### [103] [The C-index illusion: discrimination without calibration in published survival models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19526)
*Rafael da Silva,Danilo Alvares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现了三个已发表的生存分析机器学习模型，在硬盘故障、P2P信贷违约、用户流失三个领域验证了仅依赖C指数评估模型的缺陷。实验显示：高C指数模型严重失校准(p=2.6e-136)且非由单一特征捷径导致；将提前还款视为非信息性删失而非竞争风险会使违约风险高估2-4个百分点；流失模型的概率估计随预测期限退化而整体C指数保持稳定。指标选择虽未直接导致模型偏好逆转（受限于模型数量），但会导致对所选模型产生误导性的过度自信。作者发布了预注册的可复用评估工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICML 2026 Spotlight论文《Stop Chasing the C-index》在合成数据上规范性论证了仅用C指数评估生存模型会忽略校准和时间依赖性准确性，从而产生系统性误导。但这一问题在真实、已发表的非临床模型中是否存在尚未被检验。本研究旨在填补这一空白，实测真实场景下仅关注判别力而忽视校准的后果。

Method: 复现三个领域的已发表生存ML模型（硬盘故障、P2P信贷违约、用户流失），将评估工具与锚定论文的合成实验对齐验证，预注册五个假设并在Holm校正的家族误差率下检验。评估维度包括判别力(C指数)、校准性检验、竞争风险建模对比、预测期限下的概率估计退化、以及指标选择是否翻转模型偏好。

Result: 五个预注册假设中三个被拒绝：(1) 复现模型C指数达0.9595(原文0.958)但校准检验极显著失败(p=2.6e-136)，特征消融未发现单一捷径特征；(2) 将提前还款视为非信息性删失导致违约风险高估约2个百分点，高风险段高达近4个百分点；(3) 流失模型概率估计随预测期限退化而全局C指数保持在预注册区间内。指标选择是否翻转模型偏好未拒绝原假设，但功效受限(每领域仅2-3模型)；核心失效模式是对所选模型产生误导性自信而非选错模型。

Conclusion: 仅依赖C指数评估生存模型在真实非临床场景中确实会导致严重的校准失效、风险估计偏差和概率退化被掩盖的问题。评估必须同时纳入校准检验、竞争风险建模和时间依赖性能分析。作者提供的预注册评估工具包可供社区复用，推动更可靠的生存模型评估实践。

Abstract: "Stop Chasing the C-index when Evaluating Survival Analysis Models" (ICML 2026, Spotlight) argued normatively, on synthetic data, that evaluating survival models by discrimination alone, i.e. the concordance index, produces systematically misleading model comparisons, because the metric ignores calibration and time-dependent accuracy. Whether this matters for real, published, non-clinical models has not been tested. We reproduce three published survival-ML models across three structurally distinct domains (hard-drive failure, peer-to-peer credit default, and user disengagement on digital platforms), validate our evaluation instrument against the anchor paper's own synthetic experiment, and test five pre-registered hypotheses under a Holm-corrected family-wise error rate. Three of five reject. A model that reproduces the published discrimination almost exactly (C = 0.9595 vs. 0.958 reported) fails a formal calibration test at p = 2.6e-136; a broad feature-ablation search finds no single attribute responsible for this discrimination, so the calibration failure is not an artifact of a trivial shortcut. A lender's estimated default risk is biased upward by roughly two percentage points, growing to nearly four points in the riskiest segment, when loan prepayment is treated as non-informative censoring rather than as a competing risk. A platform's churn model shows probability estimates that degrade with the prediction horizon even as its global discrimination stays within the pre-registered C-index band. A direct test of whether metric choice inverts which model is preferred does not reject, though with limited power given only two to three models per domain; the failure mode we document is better characterized as misplaced confidence in a chosen model than as choice of the wrong one. We release a reusable, pre-registered evaluation harness with full code and a single annotated notebook.

</details>


### [104] [Trustworthy Privacy-Preserving Multimodal Federated Learning for Personalised Breast Cancer Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19532)
*Ruth Amey,Muhammad Arifur Rahman,Taha Osman,Nicholas Shopland,Andy Burton,Mufti Mahmud,David J. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨联邦学习在乳腺癌肿瘤进展预测中的应用，利用多模态数据（临床、影像、生物标志物等）构建隐私保护模型，对比联邦学习与中心化学习性能，评估透明度、可扩展性、安全性、公平性四大部署支柱，旨在验证联邦学习是否能在保持数据本地化的前提下达到可比预测性能，为数字孪生等个性化治疗规划提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决敏感健康数据用于预测模型训练时的隐私担忧，特别是在个性化癌症护理中，需在保护数据隐私的同时开发鲁棒的肿瘤进展预测模型，并满足透明度、可扩展性、安全性、公平性等部署需求。

Method: 采用联邦学习框架，整合多模态数据（临床信息、肿瘤特征、生物标志物数据、患者人口统计学数据、MRI影像数据）建模肿瘤特征随时间变化；将联邦学习模型性能与中心化聚合数据训练的模型对比；探究安全模型更新策略、跨患者亚组性能维持、跨机构可扩展性支持等策略。

Result: 研究评估联邦学习能否在保持数据本地化的同时达到与中心化学习相当的预测性能，结果有助于理解隐私保护多模态预测建模的可行性。

Conclusion: 联邦学习在乳腺癌肿瘤进展预测中展现出可行性，能在保护隐私前提下实现多模态预测建模，为未来数字孪生辅助临床医生和患者制定个性化治疗方案提供支持。

Abstract: Federated learning has emerged as a potential solution to privacy concerns associated with using sensitive health data for training predictive models, particularly in personalised cancer care. This research investigates whether federated learning can support the development of robust models for predicting tumour progression in breast cancer patients while addressing four critical deployment pillars: transparency, scalability, security, and fairness. This study evaluates a federated learning framework using multimodal data, including clinical information, tumour characteristics, biomarker data, and patient demographics, alongside medical imaging data such as MRI scans, to model changes in tumour characteristics over time. The performance of the federated approach was compared with that of a centralised model trained on aggregated data. The report then further examines strategies to enhance secure model updates, maintain performance across patient subgroups, and support scalability across institutions. The findings assess whether federated learning can achieve predictive performance comparable to centralised learning while preserving data locality. These results contribute to understanding the feasibility of privacy-preserving, multimodal predictive modelling and support future applications such as digital twins to assist clinicians and patients in personalised treatment planning.

</details>


### [105] [Agent-Centric Animal Pose Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19548)
*Eyrun Eyjolfsdottir,Kristin Branson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于智能体中心的自回归生成模型框架，用于从姿态追踪数据建模动物行为（包括单个体和群体），并发布了一个通用库，展示了在求偶果蝇群体中的社会行为分布捕捉能力，且支持跨表示的系统比较和新领域适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解动物行为的算法层面——动物关注什么、如何形成内部模型和计划、以及如何映射到行动——仍是神经科学和行为学的核心挑战；数据驱动的生成模型为此提供了一条可行路径。

Method: 提出智能体中心的自回归模型框架：输入自我中心感觉观测，输出自我中心运动，镜像生物约束；社会行为由智能体独立感知和响应同类而涌现。发布通用库，处理多并行表示及离散化等机器学习特定变换，提供可组合的操作序列和定量拟合工具。

Result: 训练模型成功捕捉求偶果蝇群体的社会行为分布；库支持跨输入输出表示的系统比较，并能直接适应新领域。

Conclusion: 该框架和库为从姿态数据建模动物行为（特别是社会行为）提供了有效工具，能够捕捉行为分布，并具有良好的泛化性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Understanding animal behavior at an algorithmic level -- what animals attend to, how they form internal models and plans, and how this maps to action -- remains a central challenge in neuroscience and ethology. Data-driven generative models offer a path toward this understanding. We introduce a framework for training agent-centric autoregressive models of animal behavior from tracked pose, applicable to single animals and to groups in which each agent senses and responds to its conspecifics. Our models input egocentric sensory observations and output egocentric movements, mirroring the biological constraint that animals observe and act on the world from their own reference frame. Social behavior emerges from agents independently sensing and responding to one another. This agent-centric formulation requires managing many parallel representations of the same data, along with ML-specific transformations like discretization. We release a general-purpose library focused on the composable sequences of operations that translate between these representations. We show that trained models capture the distribution of social behavior in groups of courting Drosophila, and our library includes quantitative tools for measuring fit. We demonstrate how the library supports systematic comparison across input and output representations and that it adapts straightforwardly to a new domain.

</details>


### [106] [End-to-End Differential Privacy in Training Deep Neural Network Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19580)
*Huaiyuan Rao,Calvin Hawkins,Alexander Benvenuti,Matthew Hale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的差分隐私训练框架，仅对训练输入进行隐私保护而保持标签公开。利用 Dirichlet 机制对 Softmax 输出进行随机化，并结合 Rényi 差分隐私给出多轮训练的紧致隐私预算。在 CIFAR10、MNIST、MedMNIST、FashionMNIST、SVHN 等数据集上实现了新的最高准确率，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私机器学习工作通常同时对输入和标签进行隐私保护，但在标签为公开或可安全公开的场景下，这种保护过于保守。因此需要一种仅保护训练输入而保持标签公开的框架，以提高模型效用。

Method: 针对带 Softmax 输出层的神经网络，将训练输入到 Softmax 输出的映射视为到单位单纯形的映射。在训练过程中对 Softmax 输出应用 Dirichlet 机制以实现输入的差分隐私保护。由于训练数据在多个轮次中重复使用，采用 Rényi 差分隐私来推导 Dirichlet 机制在重复使用下的紧致隐私界。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上从头训练均达到最先进准确率。在 CIFAR10 上，ε=4 时准确率从 78.37% 提升至 88.17%；ε=1 时仍达 82.96%，大幅超越先前工作。

Conclusion: 提出的端到端标签差分隐私框架通过仅保护输入、公开标签，并利用 Dirichlet 机制与 Rényi 差分隐私，显著提升了隐私预算相同条件下的模型准确率，为标签可公开场景提供了更实用的隐私保护训练方案。

Abstract: Differentially private machine learning enables model training on sensitive data while ensuring that individual data is unlikely to be recoverable from the parameters of the resulting model. However, existing work often privatizes both training inputs and their labels, and these protections may be conservative when labels are public or can be safely made public. Therefore, in this work we propose a novel private training framework that instead privatizes training inputs while keeping labels public. We consider neural networks with softmax output layers, and thus the mapping from training inputs to the output of the softmax layer is a mapping onto the unit simplex. We randomize softmax outputs during training by applying the Dirichlet mechanism to enforce differential privacy for the training inputs, hence the ``end-to-end'' label. Because training data is reused across multiple training epochs, we use the notion of \Renyi differential privacy to formulate tight bounds on the strength of privacy provided by the Dirichlet mechanism across repeated uses. We show empirically that we attain new state-of-the-art accuracy when training from scratch on CIFAR10, MNIST, MedMNIST, FashionMNIST, and SVHN across all privacy budgets evaluated. Notably, when implementing $(ε, δ)$-differential privacy with $δ=10^{-5}$, we improve the prior state-of-the-art accuracy from $78.37\%$ to $88.17\%$ at $ε=4$ on CIFAR10, and our approach has $82.96\%$ accuracy even for $ε=1$, which significantly outperforms prior work.

</details>


### [107] [The Mechanism Matters: When Knowledge Graphs Help Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19616)
*Mohammed Sameer Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验系统研究知识图谱（KG）在强化学习（RL）中的作用，发现：结构化 KG 能显著提升组合稀疏奖励任务的样本效率与求解可靠性，且收益源于图结构而非泛化正则化；KG 价值随任务相关知识量而增；注入机制决定安全性：软性、保最优注入受益于正确知识且无视错误知识，而硬性动作屏蔽脆弱，会在 KG 不完整或损坏时禁止关键动作，导致错误 KG 比无 KG 更差。临床败血症管理离线 RL 案例为阴性结果，印证收益需任务结构与机制匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献多为单域正面结果方法论文，缺乏系统性认识：KG 结构何时帮助智能体、何时中性、何时有害。

Method: 设计受控实验，独立变化 RL 任务、注入机制（状态特征、动作屏蔽、基于势函数的奖励塑形）和 KG 质量；使用合成、完全可控的 KG 覆盖 MiniGrid 环境；并辅以 UMLS 衍生的临床败血症管理离线 RL 案例研究。

Result: 三大发现：1) 在组合稀疏奖励任务上，结构化 KG 指导将样本效率与求解可靠性从 70% 提升至 97%，打乱边的洗牌控制使收益崩塌至基线（屏蔽 p=0.0001；塑形 p=0.006），证明收益源于结构而非泛化正则化。2) KG 价值随任务相关知识量增大而增加。3) 安全性取决于机制：软性、保最优注入受益于正确知识且无害忽略错误知识；硬性屏蔽脆弱，在 KG 不完整或损坏时禁止必要动作，使错误 KG 比无 KG 更差。临床案例为阴性，强调收益需要任务结构与所选机制可利用的结构匹配。

Conclusion: 为实践者提供具体指导：如何以及在多大程度上信任 KG 以指导 RL，需根据任务结构选择合适的注入机制，避免使用脆弱的硬性约束。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) are widely used to inject prior knowledge into reinforcement learning (RL), yet the literature is dominated by single-domain, positive-result method papers, so we lack a systematic account of when KG structure helps an agent, when it is neutral, and when it hurts. We conduct a controlled study that independently varies the RL task, the injection mechanism (state features, action masking, or potential-based reward shaping), and KG quality. Using a synthetic, fully controllable KG over MiniGrid environments, we report three findings. First, on compositional sparse-reward tasks structured KG guidance improves sample efficiency and solve reliability (70% to 97% of seeds), and a shuffle control that permutes the KG's edges while preserving their count collapses the benefit toward baseline (masking p=0.0001; shaping p=0.006), so the gain is structural rather than generic regularization. Second, KG value scales with the amount of task-relevant knowledge the graph contains. Third, and most consequential, safety depends on the mechanism: soft, optimality-preserving injection benefits from correct knowledge and harmlessly ignores incorrect knowledge, whereas hard masking is brittle, forbidding essential actions when the KG is incomplete or corrupted and making a wrong KG worse than none. A UMLS-derived clinical case study on sepsis management under offline RL is a careful null, underscoring that benefits require task structure the chosen mechanism can exploit. Our results give practitioners concrete guidance on how, and how much, to trust a KG when using it to guide RL.

</details>


### [108] [SCPP: A Unified Python Library for Soft Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19620)
*Kiyan Rezaee,Morteza Ziabakhsh,Artin Bahrampour,Seyed Mohammad Ghoreishi,Asal Khaje,Ali Sajedifar,Manny Chalak,Ava Zerafatangiz,Sadegh Eskandari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCPP（软聚类Python包），一个开源的、兼容scikit-learn的软聚类统一框架，集成了40种代表性算法（模糊、概率、图、矩阵分解、深度学习等），提供标准化的训练、预测、隶属度表示、评估和基准测试流程，支持可复现实验与算法扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软聚类方法种类繁多且异构，缺乏统一的编程接口、标准化的评估基准和易用的开源工具，导致算法对比、复现和部署困难，亟需一个通用框架来规范流程并整合现有算法。

Method: 设计并实现SCPP框架，遵循scikit-learn Estimator接口规范，统一模型训练、预测、隶属度矩阵输出、聚类质量指标计算、运行时间/内存/可扩展性基准测试；集成40种经典与前沿软聚类算法；提供完整文档、示例、自动化测试及与科学Python生态的无缝集成。

Result: 发布了开源Python包（GitHub: soft-clustering/soft-clustering），包含算法库、基准测试套件（数据集、指标、标准化性能评估）、文档和示例，实现了跨算法的可复现实验与便捷扩展。

Conclusion: SCPP填补了软聚类领域统一工具链的空白，为研究者和从业者提供了标准化、可扩展、开箱即用的平台，有望推动软聚类方法的系统对比、复现与实际应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we present SCPP (Soft Clustering Python Package), an open-source Python framework for soft clustering. SCPP establishes a canonical, scikit-learn-compatible estimator interface that standardizes model training, prediction, membership representation, evaluation, and benchmarking across heterogeneous soft clustering methods, including fuzzy, probabilistic, graph-based, matrix factorization, and deep learning methods. The framework currently integrates 40 representative algorithms together with a comprehensive benchmarking comprising datasets, clustering quality metrics, and standardized runtime, memory, and scalability evaluation. SCPP further provides extensive documentation, practical examples, automated testing, and seamless integration with the scientific Python ecosystem, enabling reproducible experimentation and straightforward extension with new algorithms. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/soft-clustering/soft-clustering.

</details>


### [109] [HypEMBER: Hypernetwork-based Ensemble for Robust Policy Learning of Parametrized Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19628)
*Nicolò Botteghi,Gabriele Pascali,Urban Fasel,Andrea Manzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HypEMBER框架，结合超网络与集成学习，解决参数化动力学系统中高维状态空间、模型不确定性和参数不确定性导致的标准RL方法计算不可行、鲁棒性差、泛化能力弱的问题，实现跨参数变化的泛化控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对参数化动力学系统中高维状态空间、昂贵数值求解器、部分已知控制方程、物理参数不确定或难以精确估计等挑战，标准RL方法存在计算不可行、缺乏鲁棒性、跨参数变化泛化能力差，且在噪声或不完整测量下性能进一步下降。

Method: HypEMBER框架：1) 使用超网络表示策略和价值函数，根据系统物理参数生成底层模型权重，实现跨不同动力学模式的参数化泛化；2) 引入策略和价值函数近似器的集成学习，量化认知不确定性，改进探索策略并增强训练期间及之后的鲁棒性。

Result: 在一维Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程和二维时变涡旋流粒子导航任务上验证，HypEMBER持续提升训练稳定性和样本效率，在系统动力学不确定性和观测噪声/参数误设下表现出优于最先进RL方法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HypEMBER通过超网络实现参数化泛化，通过集成学习量化不确定性，有效解决了参数化动力学系统的鲁棒控制问题，在测量噪声和参数误设条件下显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: In this work we investigate reinforcement learning (RL) as a framework for the robust control of parametrized dynamical systems in presence of measurements and model uncertainties. High-dimensional state spaces, expensive numerical solvers, the partial knowledge of the governing equations, and the dependence on physical parameters that may be uncertain or difficult to estimate accurately, make the use of standard RL approaches computationally unfeasible. Indeed, lack of robustness and poor generalization across parameter variations are further amplified in presence of noisy or incomplete measurements, ultimately hampering control performance. To address these challenges, we introduce HypEMBER, a novel RL framework based on the combination of hypernetworks and ensemble learning. In the proposed approach, both the policy and value functions are represented through hypernetworks that generate the weights of the underlying models conditioned on the physical parameters of the system, thereby enabling parametric generalization across different dynamical regimes. In addition, an ensemble of policy and value approximators is employed to quantify epistemic uncertainty, leading to improved exploration strategies and enhanced robustness during and after training. The performance of the proposed framework is assessed on two representative parametrized control problems: (i) the one-dimensional Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation and (ii) a particle-navigation task in a two-dimensional time-dependent gyre flow, focusing on robustness with respect to measurement noise and parameter misspecification. Numerical results demonstrate that HypEMBER consistently improves training stability and sample efficiency, while achieving superior robustness to uncertainties affecting both the system dynamics and the available observations, in comparison with state-of-the-art RL methods.

</details>


### [110] [Anatomy of a Sound Neural Reasoner: One-Shot Amortization, First-Pass Poisoning, and Search Inertness in Clue-Rich Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19635)
*Aleksey Komissarov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析了 Lattice Deduction Transformer (LDT) 在数独求解中的表现，发现其在线索丰富的数独中表现为一次性预测器而非迭代搜索器，准确性主要取决于校准和对称性，搜索主要减少计算浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解神经求解器（如 LDT）在约束满足问题中的实际行为，特别是是否真正学习了搜索过程，还是仅作为一次性预测器。

Method: 通过实验分析 LDT 在不同规模数独上的表现，引入 CoLT 组件（学习分支、MRV、回溯、值排除、共享 nogoods），对比约束图注意力与位置表，提出数字排列增强和测试时对称变换并集两种干预措施，并在图着色任务上验证泛化性。

Result: 发现 LDT 在线索丰富数独中首次前向传播即填满大部分格子，硬实例失败源于“首轮毒化”；CoLT 不改变可解实例但减少 1497 倍无效推导；约束图注意力在冻结预算下匹配完整 CoLT 准确率；数字排列增强将 9x9 准确率从<1% 提升至 96.5%；测试时对称并集使硬切片检查点从 72.8-78.9% 达到 100%；图着色任务中单次行为消失，搜索影响准确率。

Conclusion: 在线索丰富补全任务中，LDT 类系统是一次性摊销预测器而非学习搜索过程；准确性由校准和对称性决定，搜索主要消除计算浪费。

Abstract: Neural solvers are built to deduce, branch, and revise intermediate states. The Lattice Deduction Transformer (LDT) appears to do exactly that. In clue-rich Sudoku, it does not: one forward pass commits essentially the entire grid (every blank cell on standard 6x6, 94-96% on augmented 9x9), turning the iterative solver into a one-shot predictor wrapped in an exact verifier. All hard-slice failures are decided before search begins, when the first pass confidently deletes a value required by the true solution. We call this first-pass poisoning. Adding learned branching, MRV, backtracking, value exclusion, and shared nogoods (CoLT) does not change which Sudoku instances are solved; it cuts repeated invalid derivations 1,497-fold. At the frozen training budget, constraint-graph attention alone matches full-CoLT accuracy, while positional tables recover only under substantially longer training, indicating an optimization and sample-efficiency advantage rather than an absolute capacity difference. The diagnosis predicts two effective interventions. Digit-permutation augmentation raises 9x9 accuracy from below 1% to 96.5 +/- 0.3 across three training seeds on a symmetry-disjoint split. Test-time union over symmetry-transformed passes raises all three hard-slice checkpoints from 72.8-78.9% to 100% without retraining. On from-scratch graph coloring, one-shot behavior disappears and search changes accuracy. In clue-rich completion, LDT-like systems are one-shot amortized predictors rather than learned search procedures: accuracy is determined by calibration and symmetry, while search primarily removes computational waste.

</details>


### [111] [Expert-Guided Forecast Editing for Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19659)
*Hung Le,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Manh Nguyen,Huu Hiep Nguyen,Dai Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DEFT框架，用于在冻结的时间序列基础模型生成的预测轨迹上进行专家引导的编辑。DEFT在趋势-季节分解空间中平衡利用基础模型的预测分布与结构化探索，通过仅查询完整轨迹并复用组件级评分，在有限专家查询预算下持续优于直接搜索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型可跨异构域零样本预测，但其输出固定，难以直接融入任务特定的专家反馈。现有策略要么纯粹利用模型分布（best-of-N），要么将预测视为无结构高维向量进行优化探索，两者在查询预算紧张时均次优。需要一种能平衡先验利用与结构化探索的方法。

Method: DEFT将基础模型采样的轨迹分解为趋势和季节分量，先在该分解空间中利用模型预测分布，再对各分量进行逐分量细化探索。专家仅对完整重组轨迹打分，框架将评分反传至出现过的趋势和季节分量，实现冻结模型下的组件级结构化反馈复用。

Result: 在包含78个数据集、3个基础模型、4种反馈类型、7个查询预算的两个预测基准上，DEFT始终显著优于best-of-N、交叉熵法和贝叶斯优化等直接搜索方法。分子动力学案例研究表明该原理可推广至物理约束反馈。

Conclusion: 稀疏测试时指导应平衡先验利用与结构化探索；在分解空间中复用组件级评分是实现这一平衡的有效途径，可显著提升专家引导预测编辑的样本效率。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models can forecast across heterogeneous domains without task-specific training, but their forecasts are fixed once produced and cannot directly incorporate task-specific expert feedback. We study expert-guided forecast editing: a frozen foundation model generates candidate future trajectories, and an expensive expert evaluator scores them to guide forecast revision. Under a tight query budget, two natural strategies sit at opposite ends: best-of-$N$ purely exploits the foundation model's predictive distribution, while optimization approaches mostly explore the forecast horizon as an unstructured high-dimensional vector. Each extreme is individually sub-optimal. We introduce \textbf{DEFT}, an expert-guided forecast editing framework that balances the two by first exploiting the foundation model's predictive samples in a decomposed trend--seasonal space, then exploring around them via component-wise refinement. DEFT queries the expert only on complete trajectories, then reuses scores for the trend and seasonal components that appeared in the queried recombinations. This lets each expert query provide structured component-level feedback while keeping the foundation model frozen. We compare DEFT against direct search approaches, including best-of-$N$, cross-entropy methods, and Bayesian optimization, under matched expert-query budgets. Across two forecasting benchmarks consisting of 78 datasets, three time-series foundation models, four feedback types, and seven query budgets, DEFT consistently improves the effectiveness of expert guidance. A molecular-dynamics case study further suggests that the same principle extends to more physically grounded feedback, supporting the hypothesis that sparse test-time guidance should be spent balancing prior exploitation with structured exploration.

</details>


### [112] [Efficient Clustering with Provable Guardrails for LLM Inference at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19704)
*Longshaokan Wang,Wai Tsang Keung,Punit Ghodasara,Roman Wang,Ali Dashti,Francesc Moreno-Noguer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种两阶段聚类算法，用于在大规模LLM应用中通过聚类输入并仅对代表样本调用LLM来降低推理成本和延迟，同时提供每样本的相似度和属性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类方法无法在大规模下同时保证簇内最小相似度、分类属性精确匹配和可扩展性，限制了LLM应用向数百万用户扩展。

Method: 两阶段算法：首先使用Mini-batch K-Means生成初始聚类，然后在每个初始聚类中贪婪选择代表样本，等价于嵌入空间中alpha-ball上的Set Cover的Johnson-Chvatal启发式。算法通过构造强制执行相似度和属性护栏，时间复杂度O(nd + n^2 d/K)，空间复杂度O(nd + n^2/K^2)，当K随n线性增长时呈线性。

Result: 在内部和公开数据集上对比常见聚类方法，该方法不仅提供每样本护栏，还快10-1000倍，并能扩展到标准方法难以处理的数据规模。在3800万客户的用户画像推荐系统部署中，将下游成本和延迟降低50倍，同时保持个性化，解除生产发布阻碍。

Conclusion: 该算法有效解决了大规模LLM应用的推理成本和延迟瓶颈，提供严格的每样本质量控制，并展示了实际生产部署的显著效益。

Abstract: Scaling LLM-based applications to millions of users is bottlenecked by the inference cost and latency of modern foundation models. A natural fix is to cluster the inputs and call the LLM only on cluster representatives, letting other members inherit the output -- but this is only safe if each member is measurably close to its representative. Existing clustering methods do not offer such per-sample quality control at scale: none jointly guarantee a minimal within-cluster similarity, exact matching of categorical attributes, and scalability to tens of millions of samples. We propose a two-stage algorithm that generates initial clusters with Mini-batch K-Means, then greedily selects representatives within each initial cluster -- a step equivalent to the Johnson-Chvatal heuristic for Set Cover over alpha-balls in embedding space. The algorithm enforces the similarity and attribute guardrails exactly by construction, and runs in $O(nd + n^2 d/K)$ time and $O(nd + n^2/K^2)$ memory for $n$ samples, feature dimension $d$, and $K$ initial clusters -- linear in $n$ when $K$ grows proportionally with $n$. We provide benchmarks against common clustering methods on internal and public datasets: our method not only delivers per-sample guardrails but also runs 10-1000x faster and scales to data sizes where most standard methods become intractable. Deployed on 38 million customers for a persona-based recommender, the clustering method cut downstream cost and latency by 50-fold while preserving personalization and unblocked the production launch.

</details>


### [113] [How Fast Can Reward Models Score? A Systems Study of C++ and PyTorch Inference Runtimes for RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19712)
*Venkata Naga Sai Vishnu Rohit Pulipaka,Anish Katta,Deva Rohit Reddy Peddireddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文构建了基于ONNX Runtime的C++推理引擎，对比PyTorch eager、torch.compile和FastAPI在CPU/GPU上的奖励评分性能。CPU上自研引擎全面胜出，GPU上torch.compile最快。加速主要归因于ONNX Runtime而非C++语言，批处理策略比语言或运行时选择影响更大。需多次独立运行才能获得可靠基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF流程中奖励评分阻塞策略更新，评分慢会拖垮整个循环。现有方案多默认用PyTorch eager或torch.compile，未验证是否最优。评分虽小但与Rollout生成争抢CPU/GPU资源，加速评分引擎能释放算力供生成使用。

Method: 基于ONNX Runtime开发原生C++推理引擎。先验证正确性：输出与PyTorch基准在CPU上匹配至5.7e-6，GPU上4.2e-3。再在CPU/GPU上对比PyTorch eager、torch.compile、FastAPI，采用多次独立运行确保可靠性。

Result: CPU上：自研引擎击败所有基线，置信区间无重叠。GPU上：击败PyTorch和FastAPI，但torch.compile更快。加速源于ONNX Runtime而非C++语言。批处理策略对性能影响最大，超出预期。

Conclusion: ONNX Runtime在CPU推理上显著优于PyTorch生态；GPU上torch.compile仍具优势。批处理策略是关键优化点，比语言或运行时选择更重要。基准测试必须用多次独立运行，单次运行不可信。

Abstract: In RLHF pipelines, reward scoring blocks policy updates. Slow scoring bottlenecks the entire loop, since no update runs until every rollout gets a score. And yet most setups just default to PyTorch eager mode or torch.compile, no one checks if that's actually fastest. Scoring itself is small. Rollout generation eats far more of a typical RLHF step. But scoring and generation fight over the same CPU and GPU resources, so a faster scoring engine doesn't shrink step time on its own. It mainly frees up capacity generation can use instead. We built a native C++ inference engine on ONNX Runtime. First step: confirm correctness. Output matched the PyTorch reference to 5.7 x 10^-6 on CPU and 4.2 x 10^-3 on GPU, close enough to trust. Then we tested it against PyTorch eager mode, torch.compile, and FastAPI, on both CPU and GPU. CPU was decisive. Our engine beat every baseline, confidence intervals didn't even overlap. GPU gave a different view: we beat PyTorch and FastAPI, but torch.compile came out ahead. Further testing traced the speedup to ONNX Runtime itself, not C++ as a language. And batching strategy mattered more than either the language or the runtime choice, more than we expected. The results are from repeated, independent runs, since single runs just aren't reliable enough to trust.

</details>


### [114] [Building Fast, Evaluating Slow: Pipeline Choices Dominate Autointerpretability Score Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19386)
*Sinie van der Ben,Neele Roch,Anna Hedström,Mennatallah El-Assady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过系统实验揭示了稀疏自编码器（SAE）可解释性自动评分的不稳定性，发现方法学差异导致的方差超过架构差异，指标间稳定性各异，Top-k排序不一致，跨论文比较可能反映流程差异而非架构差异，并提出了方差分解、稳定性检查和最小报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前跨论文比较SAE可解释性依赖自动解释性评分，假设评分反映特征稳定属性而非评估管道的混淆因素，但该假设未经验证，需系统研究评分稳定性以确保比较的有效性。

Method: 在四个指标（模拟、检测、模糊、纯度）、两个模型（Pythia-160M、Apertus-8B）和四个方法学变化轴上进行系统实验，分析方差分解、指标稳定性概况及Top-k排序一致性。

Result: 1) 方法学方差整体超过架构方差；2) 各指标稳定性不同，检测最稳定，模糊最不可靠；3) Top-k特征排序在语料库和抽样条件下不一致，掩盖了单特征不稳定性，仅监控解释相似性无法发现。

Conclusion: 跨论文比较可能反映管道差异而非架构差异，不可靠的评估阻碍可解释性研究进展；贡献包括方差分解方法、稳定性检查和最小报告清单以支持评估。

Abstract: Cross-paper comparison of sparse autoencoder (SAE) interpretability often relies on autointerpretability scores. In this evaluation pipeline, a language model (LM) explains each feature, and another LM scores the explanation. For these comparisons to be meaningful, scores must reflect stable properties of the features rather than confounding aspects of the evaluation pipeline. Through systematic experiments across four metrics (simulation, detection, fuzzing, purity), two models (Pythia-160M, Apertus-8B), and four axes of methodological variation, we show that this assumption does not hold. Specifically, we find that R1) methodological variance collectively exceeds architectural variance across all metrics and tested models; R2) each metric exhibits a distinct instability profile, with detection being the most stable and fuzzing unreliable across all conditions; R3) top-k feature rankings do not stay consistent across corpus and draw conditions, masking per-feature instability behind stable mean scores; a failure that cannot be detected by monitoring explanation similarity alone. These findings suggest that cross-paper comparisons based on autointerpretability scores may reflect pipeline differences rather than architectural differences, with implications for the ongoing debate on SAE utility. More broadly, unreliable evaluation slows progress in interpretability research at a time when reliable tools for understanding AI systems are needed. To support evaluation, we contribute a variance decomposition approach, a Stability Check, and a Minimum Reporting Checklist.

</details>


### [115] [Koopman Dreamer: Spectrally Constrained Latent Dynamics for Stable World-Model Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19719)
*Jiaqi Li,Xinglong Zhang,Haibin Xie,Yixing Lan,Wei Pan,Xin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Koopman Dreamer 是一种基于 Dreamer 的世界模型，引入谱约束的确定性潜在动力学核心，利用二维旋转缩放块表示阻尼、旋转和准周期模式，结合线性和低秩双线性动作项以及随机状态调制，改进长时程潜在轨迹想象的稳定性，并在连续控制任务中取得更强的闭环性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的潜在世界模型在连续控制中通过想象潜在轨迹优化策略，提高了样本效率，但常见的神经网络转移模型对模态持久性和长轨迹滚动中的误差累积缺乏直接控制，导致长时程想象不稳定，影响闭环控制性能。

Method: 提出 Koopman Dreamer：1) 谱约束的确定性潜在动力学核心，采用 Koopman 启发的二维旋转缩放块（有界半径）显式建模阻尼、旋转和准周期模式；2) 线性和低秩双线性动作项分别捕捉全局和状态相关的控制效应；3) 随机状态调制提供局部修正信息；4) 结合后验条件 EMA 教师目标、单步一致性、多步滚动和开环观测预测目标，缩小后验训练与先验想象的分布差距；5) 推导多步滚动误差界，分离谱主干和双线性交互的放大效应与随机状态失配和建模残差的加性效应，明确误差衰减与长期信息保持的权衡。

Result: 在 DeepMind Control Suite 本体感知连续控制任务和 UAV-LiDAR 自主导航任务上，Koopman Dreamer 显著提升了长时程潜在滚动的稳定性，并在依赖高质量多步想象的任务上实现了更强的闭环控制性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入谱约束的 Koopman 动力学核心和多目标训练框架，Koopman Dreamer 有效解决了潜在世界模型在长时程想象中的误差累积和模态持久性控制问题，为基于模型的强化学习提供了更稳定、更可解释的潜在动力学建模范式。

Abstract: Latent world models improve sample efficiency in continuous control by optimizing policies over imagined latent trajectories, but common neural transitions offer limited direct control over modal persistence and error accumulation in long rollouts. We propose Koopman Dreamer, a Dreamer-style world model with a spectrally constrained deterministic latent dynamics core. Its Koopman-inspired backbone uses two-dimensional rotation--scaling blocks with bounded radii to represent damping, rotation, and near-periodic modes. Linear and low-rank bilinear action terms capture global and state-dependent control effects, while stochastic-state modulation supplies local correction information. To reduce the mismatch between posterior-conditioned training and prior-only imagination, the model combines posterior-conditioned EMA teacher targets with one-step consistency, multi-step rollout, and open-loop observation-prediction objectives. We further derive a multi-step rollout-error bound that separates amplification by the spectral backbone and bilinear interaction from the additive effects of stochastic-state mismatch and modeling residuals, clarifying the trade-off between error attenuation and long-term information retention. Experimental results on proprioceptive continuous-control tasks from the DeepMind Control Suite and UAV-LiDAR autonomous navigation demonstrate that Koopman Dreamer improves the stability of long-horizon latent rollouts and achieves stronger closed-loop control performance on tasks that rely on high-quality multi-step imagination.

</details>


### [116] [Decodable but Not Detectable: A Leakage Fingerprint for Near-OOD Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19393)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现并修复了OOD检测基准中的数据泄露问题，开发了泄露诊断工具，验证其有效性，并证明扰动型OOD信号仅可被监督解码而不可被无监督检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文档基准上审计扰动型OOD检测器时发现AUROC仅为0.326（远低于0.5随机水平），原因是指定的'OOD'类别其实是模型训练时见过的类，导致检测器因正确识别熟悉样本而被惩罚。

Method: 删除污染类别并跨两个域重训练35个模型；提出'泄露指纹'诊断：监督解码近乎完美（AUROC≈1）但无监督检测崩塌至0.65以下；在ResNet-50和ViT-B/16的CIFAR-10/100上验证52个设置（20泄露、32干净）；实地审计24个标准近/远OOD基准对。

Result: 修正后AUROC升至0.911。泄露诊断在嵌入空间达灵敏度18/20、特异性31/32，匹配的拟合集排除对照完美20/20。实地审计仅触发CIFAR-100对CIFAR-10这一内在困难对，远OOD对均未触发。修正协议下扰动信号可被监督解码（AUROC 0.87-1.00）但无监督检测器均失败，扰动法不优于普通马氏距离。

Conclusion: 贡献在于修正的评估协议和经过验证的泄露诊断工具，而非新的OOD方法。提供了理论解释并撤回了早期的循环相关性分析。

Abstract: While auditing a perturbation-based OOD detector on a document benchmark, we recorded an AUROC of 0.326 -- well below the 0.5 chance level. The cause is a benchmark leak: the designated "OOD" class is one the model was trained on, so its examples sit inside the in-distribution fit set and the detector is penalized for correctly ranking them as familiar. Deleting the class and retraining 35 models across two domains raises the score to 0.911. We distill the contamination into a leak fingerprint -- near-perfect supervised decodability (AUROC approximately 1) coupled with unsupervised detection collapsed below 0.65 -- and validate it on a controlled battery of 52 settings (20 leaked, 32 clean) across ResNet-50 and ViT-B/16 on CIFAR-10/100, achieving sensitivity 18/20 and specificity 31/32 in embedding space; the matched fit-set-exclusion controls are perfect at 20/20. An in-the-wild audit of 24 standard near/far OOD benchmark pairs fires on exactly one (the intrinsically hard CIFAR-100 vs CIFAR-10 pair) and on no far-OOD pair, confirming specificity and that standard cross-dataset construction is clean. Under the corrected protocol, perturbation signals are decodable but not detectable: a supervised reader recovers the OOD signal (AUROC 0.87-1.00) while no unsupervised detector does, and the perturbation method does not improve on plain Mahalanobis distance. We provide a theoretical account of why and, for transparency, retract an earlier circular correlation. The contributions are a corrected protocol and a validated leak diagnostic, not a new OOD method.

</details>


### [117] [Analytic Distribution of Classifier-Free Guidance for Schedule Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19725)
*Enze Jiang,Zheng Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过概率流 ODE 对无分类器引导（CFG）进行理论分析，推导出常数和时变引导下的精确路径积分分布表示，揭示了 CFG 通过指数路径积分修正修改初始分布，并据此提出分布引导 CFG（DG-CFG）调度，在 Stable Diffusion 1.5 上显著提升生成质量、改善多样性-保真度权衡并降低采样步数成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFG 是扩散模型条件生成的默认机制，但其确定性引导动力学采样的分布无法用常规乘积分布启发式 $p_0^ω q_0^{1-ω}$ 捕获，缺乏对引导诱导分布的精确理论刻画，难以解释分数差异在采样轨迹上的累积效应。

Method: 利用概率流 ODE 分析 CFG，推导常数和时变引导下的解析路径积分表示，证明 CFG 通过指数路径积分修正项修改 $p_{t_0}$，时变调度通过权重 $ω(t)-1$ 进入修正；基于此设计 DG-CFG 调度，平衡各时间步贡献并考虑信号强度与低噪声分数误差放大。

Result: 解析分数的玩具模型验证了预测分布；在 Stable Diffusion 1.5 上，DG-CFG 提升生成质量，实现更强的多样性-保真度权衡，尤其在强引导导致饱和和质量下降时优势明显；在不同 NFE 预算下，DG-CFG 以更少采样步数达到固定图像质量目标，降低采样计算成本。

Conclusion: DG-CFG 基于路径积分理论提供了有原则的时变引导调度，有效解决了 CFG 分布表征不准和强引导下质量退化问题，同时提高了采样效率。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is the default mechanism for conditional generation in diffusion models, but the distribution sampled by its deterministic guided dynamics is not captured by the usual product-distribution heuristic $p_0^ωq_0^{1-ω}$. We analyze CFG through the probability flow ODE and derive exact analytic path-integral representations of the induced distributions for both constant and time-dependent guidance. The resulting formulas show that CFG modifies $p_{t_0}$ by an exponential path-integral correction, and that a time-dependent schedule enters this correction through the weight $ω(t)-1$. This characterization explains how score discrepancies accumulate along sampling trajectories and motivates Distribution-Guided CFG (DG-CFG), a schedule that balances timestep contributions while accounting for signal strength and low-noise score-error amplification. A toy model with analytic scores closely verifies the predicted distributions. On Stable Diffusion~1.5, DG-CFG improves generation and yields a stronger diversity--fidelity trade-off across guidance strengths, with especially clear gains when strong guidance causes saturation and quality degradation in constant and heuristic schedules. Across NFE budgets, DG-CFG reaches fixed image-quality targets with fewer sampling steps, reducing the sampling cost needed to achieve target metrics.

</details>


### [118] [The World Model Remembers, the Actor Forgets: Dream Rehearsal for Continual Model-Based RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19749)
*Gurp Nijjer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DreamerV3 在持续强化学习中灾难性遗忘源于演员而非世界模型；通过分级梦境排练（监督自我模仿）可恢复技能并实现无任务标签的持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究基于模型的强化学习智能体（DreamerV3）在任务序列训练中灾难性遗忘的具体组件，澄清是记忆问题还是通道问题。

Method: 使用预注册的组件级探针测量世界模型和演员的保留情况；冻结世界模型，对比想象中强化学习与监督自我模仿在分级梦境上的恢复效果；提出分级梦境排练方法并离线评估评分失效模式。

Result: 世界模型保留旧任务几乎所有可测量知识（保留率~1.0），演员行为崩溃；RL在想象中无法恢复技能（0/3种子），分级梦境自我模仿全恢复（3/3种子）；分级梦境排练使4任务链3/3保留（纯回放0/3），8任务链3/3保留，优于真实回合克隆（配对差异+0.13，95% CI [0.07,0.24]）。

Conclusion: 持续RL中的遗忘是演员通道问题而非世界模型记忆问题；分级梦境排练提供无任务标签、参数恒定的持续学习方案，且评分规则对性能至关重要。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement-learning agents of the DreamerV3 family forget catastrophically when trained on task sequences, even when an unbounded replay buffer preserves every earlier experience. We ask a question the continual-RL literature has assumed an answer to but never measured: which component forgets? Under never-clear replay, pre-registered component-level probes (n=3 seeds throughout) show that the world model retains essentially everything measurable about old tasks -- reward discrimination (retention ratio ~1.0), value estimates, and termination structure -- while the actor's behavior collapses. Forgetting in this regime is a channel problem, not a memory problem. We demonstrate this by intervention: with the world model frozen and identical imagined rollouts, reinforcement learning in imagination fails to recover a lost skill (0/3 seeds), while supervised self-imitation on the world model's own graded dreams recovers it on 3/3 seeds with zero environment interaction. Interleaved during training, this graded dream rehearsal yields a task-label-free, parameter-constant continual learner: 3/3 four-task chains retained where plain replay passes 0/3, 3/3 eight-task chains, and consistent gains over matched real-episode cloning (paired difference +0.13, bootstrap 95% CI [0.07, 0.24], complete seed separation). The dream-grading step is load-bearing: we characterize two scoring failure modes, provide an offline selection gauge that caught both before they contaminated results, and give a realized-first grading rule that closes them. All experiments were pre-registered with committed protocols; every refuted hypothesis is reported.

</details>


### [119] [Convergence-Latency-Aware Adaptive Modulation and Resource Allocation in RIS-Assisted Wireless Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19759)
*Liwei Wang,Wen Chen,Jun Li,Qingqing Wu,Ming Ding,Xusheng Zhu,Qiong Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究RIS辅助的无线联邦学习系统，针对阻塞传播环境下的调制依赖传输误差，提出自适应调制与子信道分配的联合收敛-时延优化方案，通过符号误码率对梯度上传的影响建模，推导收敛上界，构建混合整数非线性规划问题并用低复杂度交替优化求解，在多个数据集上验证了更快收敛和更高测试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线联邦学习在不可靠无线传输下面临训练时延大和收敛性能下降的问题，特别是在阻塞传播环境中。现有研究很少刻画调制依赖的传输误差下学习收敛与通信时延的权衡，RIS虽能提高可靠性但缺乏联合收敛-时延感知的通信设计。

Method: 1. 建模RIS辅助阻塞链路下的无线联邦学习系统；2. 刻画符号误差对上传本地梯度的影响，推导揭示符号误码率(SER)对FL损失衰减影响的收敛相关上界；3. 基于该上界构建联合收敛-时延优化问题，形式化为混合整数非线性规划(MINLP)；4. 提出低复杂度混合交替优化框架求解自适应调制与子信道分配。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和Speech Commands数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方案比现有自适应通信方案持续实现更快收敛和更高测试准确率，尤其在复杂任务和具有挑战性的无线场景中优势明显。

Conclusion: 本文首次在RIS辅助阻塞链路无线联邦学习中联合考虑收敛性能与通信时延，通过符号误码率建模梯度上传误差影响，提出基于交替优化的低复杂度自适应调制与资源分配算法，有效解决了调制依赖传输误差下的收敛-时延权衡问题，为复杂无线环境下的联邦学习提供了实用的通信设计框架。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) over wireless networks suffers from significant training latency and degraded convergence due to unreliable wireless transmission, especially under blocked propagation environments. Although reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs) can improve communication reliability, existing wireless FL studies rarely characterize the trade-off between learning convergence and communication delay under modulation-dependent transmission errors. In this paper, we consider a wireless FL system operating under RIS-assisted blocked-link propagation scenarios, and focus on adaptive modulation and sub-channel allocation for convergence-latency aware communication design. By characterizing the effect of symbol errors on uploaded local gradients, we derive a convergence-related upper bound that reveals the impact of symbol error rate (SER) on FL loss decay. Based on this result, we formulate a joint convergence-latency optimization problem, which is cast as a mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem, and solve it using a low-complexity hybrid alternating optimization framework. Extensive experiments on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Speech Commands show that the proposed scheme consistently achieves faster convergence and higher test accuracy than existing adaptive communication schemes, especially in complex tasks and challenging wireless scenarios.

</details>


### [120] [AlphaRoute: Large Language Models as Semantic Optimizers for Multi-Objective Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19768)
*Kabir Murjani,Mishri Bhavsar,Manish I. Patel,Jonti Talukdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 AlphaRoute，一种基于 SHAP 溢出分解和 LLM 语义策略优化的多目标自适应全局布线框架，在 ISPD 2025 基准测试中显著降低拥塞溢出，超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 VLSI 全局布线启发式算法依赖静态惩罚调度，难以应对复杂拥塞拓扑，需要动态优化系统来解决拆线重布（R&R）中的多目标权衡。

Method: 提出 AlphaRoute 框架：基于 SHAP 的溢出分解隔离每网拥塞，结合 3D Dijkstra 迷宫布线和自适应 PathFinder 策略进行目标子图提取；引入 LLM 作为语义策略优化器，在确定性知识图谱约束下解读拥塞指标并动态调整惩罚参数。

Result: 在 ISPD 2025 基准测试中，MEMPOOL 设计溢出减少 98.6%；ARIANE 设计溢出为 146,109，比 SOTA 降低 29.8 倍，惩罚得分 S_orig = 0.0538 远优于 SOTA 的 1.780。

Conclusion: 优越的算法搜索几何结构可以克服 Python 解释型实现的延劣势，基于 LLM 的语义策略优化结合 SHAP 分解为全局布线提供了有效的动态自适应机制。

Abstract: Very Large Scale Integration (VLSI) global routing is an NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem requiring signal net assignment across capacity-constrained 3D grids while minimizing congestion, wirelength, and via transitions. Because traditional heuristics rely on static penalty schedules that fail on complex congestion topologies, we present AlphaRoute: a multi-objective adaptive search framework reformulating rip-up and reroute (R&R) into a dynamic optimization system. We introduce SHAP-based overflow decomposition to isolate per-net congestion, driving targeted subgraph extraction via 3D Dijkstra maze routing and an adaptive PathFinder policy. Crucially, AlphaRoute employs Large Language Models (LLMs) as semantic policy optimizers. Bounded by a deterministic knowledge graph, the LLMs interpret congestion metrics to dynamically adjust penalty parameters. Evaluated on ISPD 2025 benchmarks, AlphaRoute reduces overflow by 98.6% on MEMPOOL. On the constrained ARIANE design, we achieve an overflow of 146,109 (a 29.8x reduction in overflow over the state of the art), yielding a penalized score of S_orig = 0.0538 versus the State-of-the-art (SOTA) 1.780. These results demonstrate that superior algorithmic search geometry can overcome the latency of interpreted Python implementations.

</details>


### [121] [Auto-Fill: Learning to Predict Missing Values Accurately with Specialist Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19847)
*Yurong Liu,Yeye He,Haoyu Dong,Junjie Xing,Shi Han,Dongmei Zhang,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Auto-Fill 通过训练三个专用小语言模型分别针对世界知识、文本推理和代码推理，并结合校准集成机制动态选择或拒绝预测，在 11 个基准的 2200 张真实表上以不到 1% 的成本超越了 o3-pro、Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1 等最先进推理模型的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进推理模型在表格缺失值预测中虽然能全局推理，但部署成本高、过度自信且易产生幻觉；论文观察到高精度预测需要世界知识、文本推理和代码推理三种能力的组合，因此提出专用化小模型与校准拒答机制来解决成本与可靠性问题。

Method: 1. 后训练三个专用小语言模型（SLM），分别针对世界知识、文本推理、代码推理进行优化；2. 设计校准集成机制，根据各专家模型的置信度动态选择最可靠的预测或直接拒绝预测，保证高精度。

Result: 在 11 个基准、2200 张跨领域真实表上的大量实验显示，Auto-Fill 准确率优于 o3-pro、Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1 等前沿推理模型，且推理成本仅为这些模型的 1% 以下。

Conclusion: 专用化小模型结合校准拒答机制能在表格缺失值预测这一重要任务上同时实现高精度、低成本和可靠性；Auto-Fill 已开源（https://github.com/lyrain2001/auto-fill）。

Abstract: Predicting missing cell values in tabular data is a fundamental problem in data cleaning. While state-of-the-art reasoning models show great promise in predicting missing values in tables, by reasoning holistically across rows and columns, they are costly to deploy at scale and tend to be overconfident, often generating hallucinated or false-positive predictions.
  In this paper, we observe that achieving high-precision missing-value prediction in tables requires a distinct combination of three capabilities: (1) world knowledge, (2) text-based reasoning, and (3) code-based reasoning. We systematically explore design choices for combining these capabilities, and propose an Auto-Fill approach that post-trains three specialist small language models (SLMs), each optimized for one capability. We develop a calibrated ensemble mechanism that either dynamically selects the most confident specialist or abstains, ensuring high accuracy.
  Extensive experiments on 11 benchmarks with 2200 real tables drawn from diverse domains show that Auto-Fill achieves superior accuracy compared to state-of-the-art reasoning models (e.g., o3-pro, Gemini 3 Pro, and DeepSeek R1), while operating at a fraction (less than 1%) of the cost of these frontier models. Our results highlight the effectiveness of specialization and calibrated abstention in the important domain of tabular data. Auto-Fill is publicly available at https://github.com/lyrain2001/auto-fill.

</details>


### [122] [Challenges of Explainability in Continual Learning for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19382)
*Quentin Besnard,Emmanuel Doumard,Nicolas Labroche,Nicolas Ragot,Nicolas Ringuet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了可解释性在持续学习自适应时间序列预测中的核心作用，结合经验回放策略和注意力采样机制，利用注意力展开和基于梯度的归因方法（Grad-CAM）分析预测行为和采样策略，在真实压力计时间序列上验证了可解释性对理解持续学习动态的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在时间序列预测中展现出强大潜力，但因非平稳动态和有限的可解释性，在实际环境监测部署中面临挑战，因此需要将可解释性作为理解持续学习的核心工具。

Method: 研究PatchMixer、PatchTST和DLinear等神经预测架构，增强基于注意力的采样机制以支持模型随时间适应；采用注意力展开和基于梯度的归因方法（Grad-CAM）分析预测行为与采样策略；在持续学习框架下结合经验回放策略，在真实压力计时间序列上进行实验。

Result: 实验表明，分析模型和采样行为能提供对持续学习框架动态的宝贵洞察；归因模式随时间演变，可指导数据选择和适应策略，在非平稳预测场景中揭示了可解释性的挑战与机遇。

Conclusion: 可解释性不仅有助于理解持续学习行为，还揭示了在非平稳预测场景中如何利用归因模式演变来指导数据选择和适应策略，突出了挑战与机遇。

Abstract: Deep learning models have shown strong potential for time series forecasting, yet their deployment in real-world environmental monitoring remains challenging due to non-stationary dynamics and limited explainability. In this work, we investigate explainability as a central tool for understanding continual learning in adaptive time series forecasting, with Experience Replay strategies. We study neural forecasting architectures such as PatchMixer, PatchTST and DLinear, augmented with attention-based sampling mechanisms to support model adaptation over time. Explainability is leveraged through attention rollout and gradient-based attribution methods (Grad-CAM) to analyze both predictive behavior and sampling strategies within a continual learning framework. Experiments conducted on real-world piezometric time series exhibiting heterogeneous patterns and regime shifts show that analyzing model and sampling behaviors provides valuable insights into the dynamics of the continual learning framework. Beyond predictive performance, our results highlight the challenges and opportunities of using explainability to understand continual learning behaviors, revealing how attribution patterns evolve over time and how they can inform data selection and adaptation strategies in non-stationary forecasting scenarios.

</details>


### [123] [An Isotropy-Preserving Spectral Cap for Muon: Theory and Three Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19771)
*Jiachun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个基于精确尺度不变性假设的统一框架，分析了 Muon 等矩阵符号优化器对权重矩阵内部几何的影响，发现 Muon 移除了 SGD 自带的 1/||W|| 制动，导致 Frobenius 范数和谱范数更快外漂；进一步提出轻量级的“谱上限”机制，仅投影掉奇异值顶方向的一阶增长，即可在不冻结训练的前提下控制输出协方差，并在三个小规模系统中验证了其能提高各向同性并防止具体失效（如专家坍缩、注意力头发散），同时验证损失基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 及相关矩阵符号优化器在大语言模型预训练中越来越常用，但其对单个权重矩阵内部几何结构的影响尚不清楚。作者希望通过一个理想化假设——损失对权重缩放的精确尺度不变性（在归一化密集网络中近似成立）——来统一分析 SGD 与 Muon 的更新动力学差异，并寻找一种轻量级方法控制谱范数外漂而不损害训练。

Method: 1. 假设损失函数对权重矩阵 W 的缩放具有精确尺度不变性。2. 推导 SGD 自带 1/||W|| 制动因子，而 Muon 的矩阵符号步骤移除该制动，导致 Frobenius 范数与谱范数分别按 t^{1/2} 与 t^{1/4} 速度外漂。3. 发现谱范数扰动含非负二阶项，设计“谱上限”：仅从每次更新中投影掉最大奇异方向的一阶增长分量。4. 将该上限与奇异值谱的最小熵（H-infinity）联系起来。5. 在三个系统上实验：nanoGPT 前馈投影、64 专家 MoE 路由器、bf16 FlashAttention 的 query/key 投影。

Result: 在三个实验系统中，谱上限均能增加权重矩阵的各向同性；在 MoE 路由器中防止了单专家坍缩，在注意力头中避免了近似发散，且验证集损失基本保持不变。说明该方法能在不冻结训练的情况下有效控制谱范数增长。

Conclusion: 基于尺度不变性假设，Muon 移除了 SGD 的隐式范数制动，导致范数更快外漂；提出的谱上限机制仅抑制顶奇异方向的一阶增长，即可在保持训练动态的同时控制输出协方差，防止具体失效模式。但作者强调尺度不变性假设较强，且实验规模较小，结果仅为初步报告，欢迎后续评论与验证。

Abstract: Muon and related matrix-sign optimizers are increasingly used to pre-train large language models, but their effect on the internal geometry of individual weight matrices is not well understood. This preliminary report proposes a unified framework built on a single idealizing assumption -- exact scale invariance of the loss under weight rescaling, which holds approximately in normalization-heavy networks. Under this assumption, plain SGD carries a built-in 1/||W|| brake on its update size, whereas Muon's matrix-sign step removes that brake, so both the Frobenius and spectral norms drift outward faster (t^{1/2} versus t^{1/4}). We further observe that the spectral-norm perturbation has a non-negative second-order term. This implies that a lightweight "spectral cap" -- which projects out only the first-order growth of the single top singular direction from each update -- can control the output covariance W K_X W^T without freezing training: the weight keeps learning through non-top directions, top-direction rotation, and top switching. We relate this cap to the min-entropy (H-infinity) of the singular-value spectrum. We then study three systems trained with Muon: a nanoGPT feed-forward projection, a 64-expert mixture-of-experts router, and the query/key projections of a bf16 FlashAttention block. In each case the cap increases isotropy and, at the margins -- a router collapsing to a single expert, and the near-divergence of one attention head -- prevents a concrete failure, while leaving validation loss essentially unchanged. We emphasize that the scale-invariance assumption is strong and that these small-scale results are preliminary; comments are welcome.

</details>


### [124] [Scale-Aware Learning of Chaotic Dynamics on Unstructured Meshes via Binned Spectral Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19387)
*Kanad Sen,Romit Maulik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于图拉普拉斯频带的分箱谱功率损失函数，将结构化网格上的傅里叶频带损失扩展到非结构化网格，并通过切比雪夫多项式图滤波器和多层图架构（GLEAM）实现可扩展近似，显著提升了非结构化网格上湍流预测的长时域滚动精度和统计不变量保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分箱谱功率损失依赖于结构化网格上的傅里叶基，无法直接应用于非结构化网格；非结构化网格缺乏标准的傅里叶基，需要基于图算子构建谱表示，且精确特征分解计算成本过高，限制了其在大规模非线性动力系统代理建模中的应用。

Method: 1) 用图拉普拉斯特征空间替代傅里叶频带，构建图类比的频带功率匹配；2) 提出基于切比雪夫多项式的稀疏图滤波器近似精确频带投影，避免显式特征分解；3) 引入多层图架构下的GLEAM（图拉普拉斯能量对齐），在图层级间施加保留子空间的尺度感知监督，正则化粗细表示。

Result: 在非结构化网格湍流预测任务中，提出的谱损失显著优于确定性基线：长时域滚动保真度提升，统计不变量（如能量谱、结构函数）得到更好保持；切比雪夫近似和GLEAM在精度与计算效率间实现良好权衡。

Conclusion: 基于图拉普拉斯频带的分箱谱损失及其可扩展近似（切比雪夫滤波器、GLEAM）为非结构化网格上的混沌动力系统代理建模提供了有效的物理约束监督机制，解决了非结构化网格缺乏标准频域表示的问题，实现了长时域预测精度和统计保真度的双重提升。

Abstract: Surrogate modeling for high-dimensional nonlinear dynamical systems that exhibit chaos requires mechanisms that preserve not only pointwise accuracy but also the scale-dependent structure of physical fields. Bandwise spectral power losses, such as the binned spectral loss function, provide such supervision on structured grids, where Fourier modes define a standard frequency decomposition. On irregular meshes, however, no canonical Fourier basis exists, and spectral representations must be constructed from graph operators induced by mesh connectivity and geometry. In this study, we extend the binned spectral power loss for application to unstructured-mesh surrogate modeling of nonlinear dynamical systems. This is obtained by replacing Fourier bands with graph-Laplacian frequency bands, and we provide scalable Chebyshev and multilevel approximations for improving long-horizon rollout fidelity. In its full-spectrum form, our approach uses graph Laplacian eigenspaces to provide a graph analogue of Fourier band-power matching, but incurs the high cost of spectral decomposition. As a scalable approximation, we replace exact band projectors with sparse Chebyshev polynomial graph filters, avoiding explicit eigendecomposition. When utilizing multilevel graph architectures, we introduce Graph Laplacian Energy Alignment for Meshes (GLEAM), which applies retained-subspace scale-aware supervision across graph hierarchies so that coarse and fine representations are regularized during autoregressive rollout. Our results show that the proposed spectral losses improve long-horizon rollout fidelity and preserve statistical invariants for the forecasting of turbulent flows on unstructured meshes, compared to deterministic baselines.

</details>


### [125] [OPIUM: Mitigating Steering Externalities and Over-Refusal via Dual Objective Latent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19806)
*Kavin Aravindan,Arihant Rastogi,Aadi Prasad,Krishak Aneja,Saiyam Jain,Vaishnavi Shivkumar,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的方法 OPIUM，通过表示匹配来净化引导向量，改善激活引导中的安全性与实用性的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导虽然轻量级，但引导向量可能产生意外副作用：实用性向量可能削弱安全行为，拒绝向量可能导致对良性提示的过度拒绝。

Method: OPIUM（Optimizing Protected Injections via Utility Manifolds）是一种无需训练的方法，通过表示匹配优化新的引导向量，在保留期望干预下游表示的同时，在原始向量失效的提示上匹配更安全的参考行为。

Result: 在引导外部性和过度拒绝设置中，OPIUM 相对于原始引导和方向消融改善了安全性-实用性权衡。

Conclusion: 激活引导的有害副作用通常可以直接在激活空间中缓解。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight mechanism for controlling large language models at inference time, but steering vectors can have unintended externalities: utility vectors may weaken safety behavior, while refusal vectors may induce over-refusal on benign prompts. We introduce OPIUM (Optimizing Protected Injections via Utility Manifolds), a training-free method for sanitizing steering vectors through representation matching. Given reference behaviors on two prompt sets, OPIUM optimizes a new steering vector that preserves the downstream representations induced by the desired intervention while matching a safer reference behavior on prompts where the original vector fails. Across steering-externality and over-refusal settings, OPIUM improves the safety--utility tradeoff relative to vanilla steering and directional ablation, suggesting that harmful side effects of activation steering can often be mitigated directly in activation space.

</details>


### [126] [Asymptotically Optimal Regret for Reinforcement Learning without Horizon Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19854)
*Runlong Zhou,Zihan Zhang,Maryam Fazel,Simon S. Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域时齐次表格型马尔可夫决策过程中的无视界遗憾最小化，提出新算法并证明遗憾上界为Ō(√(SAK) + S⁸A³)，完全消除了对时域H的依赖，达到渐近最优，匹配语境老虎机下界Ω(√(SAK))（对数因子内）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究中，Zhang等人(2021)的遗憾界包含log H因子，Zhang等人(2022)的无视界遗憾界为Ō(√(S⁹A³K))，S的依赖次数过高。核心难点在于：尽管转移核时齐次，但最优价值函数{Vₕ*}随步骤h变化（时不齐次），直接对所有价值函数做并集界会引入额外min{log H, S}因子。本文旨在彻底移除H依赖并大幅降低S的多项式次数。

Method: 提出新算法，结合三大技术创新：(1) 利用最优价值函数Vₕ*随h单调递增的性质，并将价值函数投影到S维网格上，避免并集界带来的额外因子；(2) 引入时域截断论证，实现基于奖励的探索，省去单独的无奖励探索阶段；(3) 设计切割奖励，同时保持乐观性和规划所需的单调性；(4) 证明时齐次MDP的总偏差新界，控制切割奖励中的截断方差项，使其仅依赖S的可调多项式次数，完全不依赖H。

Result: 获得遗憾上界Ō(√(SAK) + S⁸A³)（失败概率δ），其中K为回合数。该界无H依赖，渐近最优，匹配下界Ω(√(SAK))（对数因子内）。相比Zhang等人(2021)的Ō(√(SAK log H) + S²A log H)完全移除log H；相比Zhang等人(2022)的Ō(√(S⁹A³K))将S的指数从9降至8，A从3保持不变，显著改进渐近阶。

Conclusion: 本文通过单调性利用、网格投影、时域截断、切割奖励和总偏差新界等技术组合，实现了时齐次表格型MDP的渐近最优无视界遗憾，彻底解决了H依赖问题，并大幅优化了状态空间大小S的多项式依赖，为强化学习理论提供了更紧致的性能保证。

Abstract: We study horizon-free regret minimization for finite-horizon time-homogeneous tabular Markov decision processes with $S$ states, $A$ actions, horizon $H$, and per-trajectory total reward bounded by $1$.
  We propose a new algorithm and prove a regret upper bound \[\tilde O(\sqrt{SAK}+S^8A^3)\] with failure probability $δ$, where $K$ is the number of episodes and $\tilde O(\cdot)$ hides $\mathsf{poly}\log(S,A,K,1/δ)$.
  Thus, the regret is $H$-free and asymptotically optimal, matching the contextual-bandit lower bound $Ω(\sqrt{SAK})$ up to logarithmic factors. This completely removes the $\log H$ dependence from the previous $\tilde O(\sqrt{SAK\log H}+S^2A\log H)$ guarantee of Zhang et al. (2021), and drastically improves the prior best horizon-free regret $\tilde O(\sqrt{S^9A^3K})$ of Zhang et al. (2022) asymptotically.
  The main technical difficulty is that the optimal value functions $\{V_h^*\}_{h=1}^H$ are time-inhomogeneous even though the transition kernel is time-homogeneous. A direct union bound over all value functions typically incurs an additional $\min\{\log H,S\}$ factor. We avoid this factor by (i) exploiting the monotonicity of $V_h^*$ in $h$ and (ii) non-trivially projecting the value functions onto an $S$-dimensional grid.
  Our analysis relies on three additional ingredients. First, we introduce a horizon-truncation argument that enables reward-based exploration and removes the cost of a separate reward-free exploration phase. Second, we design a cutting bonus that preserves both optimism and the monotonicity needed for planning. Third, we prove a new bound on total deviation for time-homogeneous MDPs, which controls the clipped variance terms in the cutting bonus with adjustable polynomial dependence on $S$ and without any dependence on $H$. Together, these tools yield an asymptotically optimal horizon-free regret guarantee.

</details>


### [127] [Cross-Subject Semantic Decoding with Shared-Space Alignment for Generalized Neural Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19394)
*Ji-Hoon Heo,Aleksandra Joanna Wisniewska,Seo-Hyun Lee,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于共享响应模型(SRM)的跨受试者语义解码框架，将多受试者的皮层脑电(ECoG)语音感知神经响应对齐到共享潜在空间，并映射到语境语义嵌入空间，实现无需重新训练即可泛化到新受试者，显著提升跨受试者泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侵入式神经记录中跨受试者泛化面临挑战，因电极配置、解剖结构和神经信号模式在个体间差异巨大，需研究如何减少受试者特异性差异同时保留共享的刺激相关表示。

Method: 使用共享响应模型(SRM)将多受试者自然语言理解任务的ECoG数据投影到共享潜在空间；训练解码器从对齐后的神经表示预测语境语义嵌入；对新受试者估计其特定投影到预定义共享空间，直接应用预训练解码器无需微调。

Result: 该框架在各评估设置下持续优于基线方法，且从源受试者到保留受试者测试的性能下降减小，表明跨受试者泛化能力显著提升。

Conclusion: 将神经活动对齐到共享潜在空间并结合语义嵌入空间解码，通过减少受试者特异性差异同时有效捕捉共享刺激相关表示，为提升跨受试者泛化提供了有效策略。

Abstract: Generalizing across subjects remains challenging in invasive neural recordings because electrode configurations, anatomical structures, and neural signal patterns vary substantially across individuals. To investigate such inter-subject variability, we propose a cross-subject semantic decoding framework that aligns neural responses to speech perception from multiple subjects into a shared latent space and learns a mapping from the aligned neural representations to contextual embeddings. More specifically, using electrocorticography data collected during natural language comprehension, we estimate the shared space using the shared response model and train a decoder to predict contextual semantic embeddings from projected neural responses. For a held-out subject, we estimate a subject-specific projection into the predefined shared space, and directly apply the pretrained decoder without any retraining. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms baseline methods across evaluation settings and exhibits a reduced performance drop from source subject to held-out subject testing, indicating improved cross-subject generalization. These results suggest that aligning neural activity into a shared latent space, while decoding in a semantic embedding space, provides an effective strategy for improving cross-subject generalization by reducing subject-specific differences in neural responses while effectively capturing shared stimulus-related representations.

</details>


### [128] [Adversarial Frontiers: Minimum-Norm Attack Ensembles for Robustness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19855)
*Luca Scionis,Luca Melis,Maura Pintor,Fabio Brau,Ambra Demontis,Giorgio Fumera,Fabio Roli,Battista Biggio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种统一的对抗鲁棒性评估框架，解决了单一扰动预算评估不稳定、攻击最优性未知、攻击强度与评估成本权衡不可控等问题。通过构建跨多范数的最小范数攻击池，定义攻击前沿和防御前沿，将评估形式化为前沿逼近问题，并提出防御最优性指数进行模型排序。实验表明，该方法在可控查询预算下匹配或超越AutoAttack。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗鲁棒性评估依赖预定义攻击集合（如AutoAttack）在单一扰动预算ε和有限范数下进行，存在三大根本局限：1) 鲁棒性-扰动曲线可能相交或以不同速率衰减，导致单一ε排名不稳定；2) 当前攻击集合无法提供最优性证据，与最坏情况性能存在未知间隙；3) 固定攻击配置无法系统控制攻击强度与评估成本的权衡。

Method: 引入基于综合最小范数攻击池和跨ℓ0、ℓ1、ℓ2、ℓ∞范数的鲁棒性-扰动曲线的统一评估框架。定义攻击前沿为攻击池对模型产生的最坏情况鲁棒性估计。将评估形式化为前沿逼近问题，构建最小范数攻击集合（攻击池的优化子集），在可控查询预算下逼近前沿，预算越大估计越紧。定义防御前沿为模型集合在每个扰动大小下的最大鲁棒性。提出防御最优性指数（DOI），基于模型与防御前沿的间隙进行排序，无需选择参考ε。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上，提出的攻击集合在每个预算层级上对大多数防御模型均匹配或超越AutoAttack，且具有固定且可控的查询成本。该方法为从业者提供了一种基于查询控制、曲线化的评估替代方案，替代了固定ε评估。

Conclusion: 该工作从根本上重新定义了对抗鲁棒性评估，从单点评估转向全曲线评估，从固定攻击转向可控预算下的前沿逼近，并提供了无需参考ε的模型排序指标。这为鲁棒性评估提供了更严谨、可控且实用的范式。

Abstract: Adversarial robustness is commonly evaluated with predefined attack ensembles, such as AutoAttack, at a single perturbation budget $\varepsilon$ and on a selective choice of perturbation norms. We argue this formulation is fundamentally limited. First, robustness--perturbation curves may intersect or decay at different rates across models, making single-$\varepsilon$ rankings unstable. Second, current ensembles provide no evidence of optimality, leaving an unknown gap to worst-case performance. Third, fixed attack configurations provide no systematic control over the trade-off between attack strength and evaluation cost. To address these limitations, we introduce a unified evaluation framework based on a comprehensive pool of minimum-norm attacks and robustness--perturbation curves across $\ell_0$, $\ell_1$, $\ell_2$ and $\ell_\infty$ norms. We define the attack frontier as the worst-case robustness estimate the attack pool produces against a model. We then formalize evaluation as a frontier-approximation problem, constructing minimum-norm attack ensembles, optimized subsets of the comprehensive pool, that approach the frontier under a controllable query budget, with larger budgets monotonically tightening the estimate. Furthermore, we define the defense frontier as the maximum robustness across the model set at each perturbation size. We finally propose the Defense Optimality Index to rank defenses by their gap to the defense frontier, providing a ranking without selecting a reference $\varepsilon$. On CIFAR-10 and ImageNet, our ensembles match or exceed AutoAttack on most defenses at every budget tier, at fixed and controllable query cost, offering practitioners a query-controlled, curve-based alternative to fixed-$\varepsilon$ evaluation.

</details>


### [129] [The Blessing of Dimensionality: How Near-Orthogonality in High-Dimensional Spaces Explains Temporal Portability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20301)
*Abigail Woodring,Adrian Chan,Rana Muhammad Shahroz Khan,Sukwon Yun,Chau-Wai Wong,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 PortLLM 在持续预训练多步更新后的长期时间可移植性，通过实验和理论分析发现 LoRA 补丁的可移植性长期持久，且高维向量近正交性是关键理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PortLLM 仅验证短期可移植性，长期性能未探索；且其有效性缺乏理论解释。

Method: 在 Mistral、Gemma、Qwen 三个基础模型上进行 10 步持续预训练的实证研究，并提供两个理论分析：高维向量近正交性与损失景观几何视角。

Result: 实证发现可移植性在更长时间跨度内持久存在，无需重复微调；理论揭示近正交性是时间可移植性的关键，几何视角有助于对比不同适配方案。

Conclusion: PortLLM 的简单方法在长期持续预训练更新中仍具竞争力，理论分析为理解其机制提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Fine-tuning has been widely used to adapt large language models (LLMs) for domain-specific tasks. Parameter efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods such as low-rank adaptation (LoRA) are frequently used to reduce computational costs. PortLLM is a training-free and data-free scheme used to adapt LLMs after continual pretraining. Although the initial PortLLM results show that LoRA patches exhibit short-term temporal portability, the long-term performance of PortLLM across several updates of continual pretraining remains underexplored. Furthermore, the intriguing effectiveness of PortLLM is not well understood from a theoretical standpoint. We address these two open questions by (1) performing an extensive empirical study of the long-term temporal portability of PortLLM patches across 10 continual pretraining steps using base models Mistral, Gemma, and Qwen; and (2) offering two theoretical analyses to explain our observation that the simple PortLLM method achieves competitive performance. We find empirically that the portability persists across longer time duration, indicating that repeated fine-tuning is not required when the base model is periodically updated. We find theoretically that near-orthogonality of high-dimensional vectors is a key justification for temporal portability. Our analyses also demonstrate a geometric perspective of the loss landscape in facilitating the theoretical comparison of different adaptation options.

</details>


### [130] [From Trajectories to Prefixes: Reusing Teacher Trajectories via Replayed Prefixes and Online Continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19395)
*Yihan Wang,Zhong Guan,Haoran Sun,Jiale Huang,Likang Wu,Hongke Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Prefix-GRPO，一种强化学习框架，将教师轨迹分解为回放对齐的前缀查询和在线续写，使小语言模型在长时程交互环境中能同时优化历史前缀token和未来续写策略，显著优于蒸馏和标准RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型虽适合做交互式智能体骨干，但直接从强教师轨迹蒸馏会将丰富的多轮行为压缩为单步模仿目标，这在长时程环境中效率低下，因为早期决策会塑造后续状态和奖励。

Method: Prefix-GRPO将教师轨迹分解为回放对齐的前缀查询和在线续写。每个前缀在环境中回放以恢复有效中间状态，随后学生继续在线交互并获得任务奖励。与仅优化响应的GRPO不同，Prefix-GRPO还对回放前缀内的历史assistant token应用裁剪策略更新，利用策略蒸馏的SFT检查点估算其旧log-prob，将前缀学习和续写学习统一在同一策略优化形式中。

Result: 在TextCraft、BabyAI和ALFWorld三个基准上实验表明，Prefix-GRPO使小模型智能体优于蒸馏和标准RL基线；消融实验显示仅回放不足，必须显式优化前缀token。

Conclusion: Prefix-GRPO通过统一前缀token优化与续写策略优化，有效解决了直接蒸馏在长时程交互环境中的低效问题，为小模型交互式智能体提供了更优的训练范式。

Abstract: Small language models are attractive backbones for interactive agents, but direct distillation from strong teacher trajectories often turns rich multi-turn behavior into one-shot imitation targets. This is inefficient in long-horizon environments, where early decisions shape later states and rewards. We propose Prefix-GRPO, a reinforcement learning framework that decomposes teacher trajectories into replay-aligned prefix queries and online continuations. Each prefix is replayed in the environment to recover a valid intermediate state, after which the student continues online interaction and receives task reward. Unlike response-only GRPO, Prefix-GRPO also applies clipped policy updates to historical assistant tokens inside the replayed prefix, using a policy-distilled SFT checkpoint to estimate their old log-probabilities. This unifies prefix learning and continuation learning within the same policy-optimization form. Experiments on TextCraft, BabyAI, and ALFWorld show that Prefix-GRPO improves small-model agents over distillation and standard RL baselines, while ablations show that replay alone is insufficient without explicit prefix-token optimization. The implementation and reproduction scripts are available at https://github.com/HappynessI/Prefix_GRPO.

</details>


### [131] [Local Causal Structure Learning in the Presence of Latent Variables and Selection Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19866)
*Zheng Li,Hao Zhang,Ruxin Wang,Ruichu Cai,Kun Zhang,Feng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoCaLS算法，用于在存在隐变量和选择偏差的情况下，从观测数据中局部发现目标变量的直接因果关系，无需学习全局因果结构，显著降低计算成本并保持高准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法要么学习全局因果结构计算开销大，要么假设无隐变量和选择偏差，这在实际应用中常被违背。因此需要一种能处理隐变量和选择偏差的局部因果结构学习方法。

Method: 首先刻画一个局部区域，使得无需恢复全局结构即可进行目标特定的因果发现；建立从该局部区域观测分布学到的因果信息与全局因果结构中对应信息的理论联系；基于此提出LoCaLS算法，在标准假设下保证合理性和完备性，能识别出与全局因果发现方法相同的目标变量直接因果，同时允许隐变量和选择偏差存在。

Result: 在随机和真实结构上的大量实验表明，LoCaLS比现有局部方法具有更高的结构准确性，且计算量远低于最先进的全局方法。在两个真实基因表达数据集上的应用揭示了生物学上合理的目标特定因果结构，证明了其在大规模生物数据分析中的实用性。

Conclusion: LoCaLS为在存在隐变量和选择偏差的观测数据中进行目标变量局部因果发现提供了一种理论可靠、计算高效且实用有效的解决方案，适用于基因调控分析等大规模生物医学研究。

Abstract: Discovering the direct causes and effects of a target variable from observational data is a fundamental problem in causal discovery, with broad applications in domains such as gene regulatory analysis and biomedical research. Existing causal discovery methods either learn a global causal structure, which incurs substantial computational cost, or assume the absence of latent variables and selection bias, assumptions that are often violated in real-world settings. Motivated by these challenges, we study local causal structure learning in the presence of latent variables and selection bias. Specifically, we first characterize a local region that enables target-specific causal discovery without recovering the entire global structure. We then establish a theoretical bridge between causal information learned from the observed distribution induced on this local region and the corresponding information in the global causal structure. Building on these foundations, we propose LoCaLS, a local causal structure learning algorithm that is sound and complete under standard assumptions and identifies the same direct causes and effects of a target variable as those identifiable by global causal discovery methods, while allowing for latent variables and selection bias. Extensive experiments on random and real-world structures demonstrate that the proposed method consistently achieves higher structural accuracy than existing local methods while requiring substantially less computational effort than state-of-the-art global methods. Furthermore, applications to two real-world gene expression datasets reveal biologically plausible target-specific causal structures, demonstrating its practical applicability in large-scale biological data analysis.

</details>


### [132] [Leveraging Offline Supervision for Efficient and Generalizable Reinforcement Learning in Large-Scale Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19399)
*Dmitriy Poyarkov,Aleksei Staroverov,Aleksandr I. Panov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究混合离线-在线训练方法，将离线监督（离线数据或离线训练的参考策略）正则化引入强化学习（RL）训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略。实验表明，混合方法在保持强OOD（分布外）性能的同时，将训练预算减半，达到接近标准RL的性能，而非在速度与OOD性能间权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线RL训练的策略在OOD泛化上优于离线模仿学习方法，但RL训练成本高。现有工作已证实RL训练的VLA策略在OOD基准上优于监督微调（SFT）。本文旨在探究混合离线-在线训练能否结合两者优势：利用离线监督提升RL训练效率，同时保持强OOD能力。

Method: 提出基于离线监督正则化的RL方法：使用离线数据或离线训练的参考策略作为正则化项，指导在线RL训练。在OOD基准上评估该混合方法，并与纯离线训练和标准RL进行对比。

Result: 纯离线训练OOD性能有限；引入离线监督的RL方法在保持强OOD能力的同时，显著提升训练效率，仅需标准RL约一半训练预算即可达到相近性能。混合方法并未在速度与OOD性能间产生权衡，而是同时实现效率增益与强OOD泛化。

Conclusion: 混合离线-在线训练通过离线监督正则化RL，能以更低训练成本获得强OOD泛化能力的VLA策略，为高效机器人策略学习提供了实用范式。

Abstract: It is commonly observed that online reinforcement learning (RL) produces better-performing strategies than offline methods across a broad range of performance measures. In particular, RL-trained policies exhibit stronger out-of-distribution (OOD) behavior, where models trained only with imitation learning approaches often struggle. A recent study introduced an OOD-focused benchmark and reported that RL-trained vision-language-action (VLA) policies achieve noticeably better OOD performance and slightly better in-distribution (IND) performance than their counterparts trained with supervised fine-tuning (SFT). In this work, we investigate whether hybrid offline-online training can combine the advantages of both approaches. Specifically, we study RL methods regularized by offline supervision via either offline data or an offline-trained reference policy. We evaluate these approaches on the OOD benchmark and compare them with both offline-only training and standard RL. Our results show that although offline training achieves limited OOD performance by itself, incorporating offline supervision into RL preserves strong OOD capability while substantially improving training efficiency. In particular, the guided methods reach performance close to that of standard RL while requiring roughly half of the training budget. Rather than producing a trade-off between speed and OOD performance, the hybrid approach retains strong OOD capability while achieving this efficiency gain. Project page: https://alstar8.github.io/offline-supervision-vla-rl

</details>


### [133] [Nonlinear Bias-Compensated Adaptive Filter and Its Application for Time-Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19902)
*Yi Peng,Haiquan Zhao,Jinhui Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机傅里叶特征和广义自适应函数的偏差补偿滤波算法（RFFBCGA），解决了现有非线性自适应滤波算法忽略输入噪声且鲁棒性差的问题，在保持固定网络结构的同时有效抑制输入噪声并增强对非高斯输出噪声的鲁棒性，实验验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性自适应滤波算法仅考虑输出噪声，忽略了实际中普遍存在的输入噪声；虽然BCKLMS算法针对非线性误差变量模型中的输入噪声进行了偏差补偿，但固定字典限制了网络增长且无法充分捕捉输入信号特征，且作为基于LMS的算法，在非高斯输出噪声下鲁棒性较差。

Method: 提出随机傅里叶偏差补偿广义自适应滤波（RFFBCGA）算法，在随机傅里叶特征偏差补偿（RFFBC）框架下，保持固定网络结构，通过偏差补偿项减轻输入噪声干扰，利用广义自适应（GA）函数的灵活形式增强算法在各种噪声场景下的鲁棒性。

Result: 大量仿真实验包括真实世界时间序列预测任务表明，RFFBCGA算法在输入噪声和非高斯输出噪声环境下均优于现有方法，展示了其优越性。

Conclusion: RFFBCGA算法通过结合随机傅里叶特征和广义自适应函数，有效解决了非线性自适应滤波中输入噪声建模不足和非高斯噪声鲁棒性差的问题，为实际应用提供了更可靠的非线性自适应滤波方案。

Abstract: Most existing nonlinear adaptive filtering algorithms only account for output noise, neglecting the fact that input noise is also prevalent in practice. Although the recently proposed bias-compensated kernel least mean square (BCKLMS) algorithm addresses input noise in the nonlinear errors-in-variables (EIV) model, it still suffers from two major limitations. First, the use of a fixed-size dictionary restricts network growth but also prevents it from fully capturing the characteristics of the input signal. Second, as an least mean square (LMS) based algorithm, it exhibits poor robustness in the presence of non-Gaussian noise in the output signal. To overcome these issues, this paper proposes the random Fourier bias-compensated filter under general adaptive function (RFFBCGA) algorithm. Within the random Fourier feature based bias-compensated (RFFBC) framework, the proposed algorithm not only maintains a fixed network structure and effectively mitigates input noise interference through the BC term, but also achieves improved characterization of the input signal. Moreover, by leveraging the flexible form of the general adaptive (GA) function, the algorithm's robustness across various noise scenarios is further enhanced. Extensive simulations, including real-world time series prediction tasks, demonstrate the superiority of the proposed method.

</details>


### [134] [Recovering Clinical Utility Under Differential Privacy: Empirical Validation of Adaptive Federated Aggregation on Heterogeneous Cardiovascular Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19403)
*Rodrigo Tertulino,Laercio Alencar,Ricardo Almeida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在真实临床心血管数据集上验证了 FedCVR 联邦学习框架，证实其在差分隐私约束下仍保持自适应优势，F1-Score 79.2%、AUC 0.96，显著优于 FedAvg，隐私开销符合合成实验预测的平滑退化模式，证明了该框架在真实多中心临床场景中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作（Tertulino 和 Alencar, 2026）在合成六特征基准上证明服务端自适应优化可作为差分隐私噪声的时间去噪器，但明确指出需在真实临床数据上验证。本文旨在填补这一空白，评估 FedCVR 在真实异构多中心心血管数据集上的表现与隐私-效用权衡。

Method: 使用五个公开心血管数据集（Framingham、Cleveland、Hungarian、Switzerland、Long Beach VA），统一映射为 13 属性 UCI Heart Disease 模式，构建异构联邦场景，采用留一机构出交叉验证。在操作隐私预算（噪声乘数 0.8，ε≈4.2）下训练 FedCVR 与基线 FedAvg，通过配对 t 检验并经 Bonferroni 校正比较 F1、AUC 等指标。

Result: FedCVR 在真实数据上达到 F1-Score 79.2%、AUC 0.96，在所有评价指标上以 p≤0.003（Bonferroni 校正后仍显著）显著优于 FedAvg。实测隐私开销呈现合成实验中观察到的平滑退化模式，验证了自适应优化对 DP 噪声的去噪效果在真实异构数据中依然成立。

Conclusion: FedCVR 框架在真实多中心临床环境中表现出稳健的隐私保护与模型性能平衡，为联邦学习在临床决策支持系统中的落地提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Validating federated learning frameworks on real clinical data is an essential step between proof-of-concept demonstrations in controlled synthetic environments and deployment in real multicenter healthcare settings. A prior architectural study by the same authors (Tertulino and Alencar, 2026) demonstrated, on a synthetic six-feature benchmark, that server-side adaptive optimization acts as a temporal denoiser for Differential Privacy noise, answering an open challenge identified in the original pipeline work (Tertulino, 2025). That study used synthetically generated data and explicitly identified real-world validation as a priority future direction. The present work addresses this gap by validating the FedCVR framework on five publicly available real cardiovascular datasets (Framingham, Cleveland, Hungarian, Switzerland, and Long Beach VA), harmonized to the 13-attribute UCI Heart Disease schema and configured as a heterogeneous federated scenario with leave-one-institution-out cross-validation. Results demonstrate that FedCVR preserves its adaptive advantage on real data, achieving an F1-Score of 79.2% and AUC of 0.96 under the operational privacy budget (noise multiplier = 0.8, privacy budget epsilon approximately 4.2), while statistically outperforming standard FedAvg on all evaluated metrics (paired t-tests, all p <= 0.003, significant under the Bonferroni-corrected threshold). The measured privacy cost on real data confirms the graceful degradation pattern observed in the synthetic experiments, providing empirical evidence of the framework's clinical viability in genuine multicenter contexts.

</details>


### [135] [Time Series Network Utilization KPI Forecasting Using Advanced AI/ML Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19974)
*Niraj Gadhe,Kirti Bhardwaj,Moulik Jain,Shubhi Sharma,Vinay Saini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了多种时间序列预测模型（包括季节性分解、Prophet、随机森林、XGBoost、支持向量回归、双向LSTM和卷积LSTM）在带宽利用率预测上的表现，通过MAPE、NRMSE和R-square指标对比，为网络容量规划提供模型选择指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据密集型应用、云基础设施和IoT生态的快速扩张使得主动资源配置至关重要，但传统被动方法无法准确预测流量波动，导致过度配置、意外停机和服务质量下降，影响运营预算和业务连续性。准确预测带宽利用率是高效容量规划的关键。

Method: 研究使用通用接口数据集，评估季节性分解、Prophet、随机森林、XGBoost、支持向量回归、双向LSTM和卷积LSTM等多种模型，采用MAPE、NRMSE和R-square三个指标进行基准测试对比。

Result: 研究提供了模型准确性与计算效率之间权衡的可操作见解，量化了不同模型在带宽预测任务上的性能差异。

Conclusion: 这些发现使工程师、运营商和业务所有者能够根据其特定基础设施需求，选择最优的预测模型，从而实现更高效的网络容量规划。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of data-intensive applications, cloud infrastructure, and IoT ecosystems has made proactive resource provisioning critical for maintaining optimal network performance. However, network administrators face a constant battle against capacity constraints, where traditional reactive approaches fail to accurately anticipate traffic fluctuations. This inability to foresee demand leads to costly over-provisioning, unexpected downtime, and degraded quality of service directly impacting operational budgets and business continuity. To achieve efficient capacity planning, accurate forecasting of bandwidth utilization is essential. This study addresses the challenge by evaluating a diverse spectrum of models including seasonal decomposition, Prophet, Random Forest, XGBoost, Support Vector Regression, and advanced deep learning architectures like bidirectional and Convolutional LSTMs - using a common interface dataset benchmarked across MAPE, NRMSE, and R-square metrics. Ultimately, this research delivers actionable insights into the trade-offs between model accuracy and computational efficiency, empowering engineers, operators, and business owners to select the optimal forecasting model for their specific infrastructure needs.

</details>


### [136] [Structured Latent Space Modeling over Multi-Scale Temporal Patches for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19404)
*Xingsheng Chen,Deyu Yi,Siu-Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 M2Patch，一种基于 CNN 的多变量时间序列预测架构，通过多尺度打补丁、深度可分离卷积、尺度内平滑约束和尺度间对齐约束，将通道独立观测映射到结构化潜在空间，在十个真实基准上取得 57 个最优和 34 个次优结果，同时保持线性计算复杂度和对补丁级输入损坏的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测主干网络将学习到的表示视为预测的瞬态副产品，未充分利用多变量时间序列在多个时间尺度上展开的结构模式的组织几何特性。
