Method: M2Patch 采用多尺度打补丁将输入分解为重叠的时间粒度；使用带渐进膨胀的深度可分离卷积在线性时间内提取尺度特定特征；通过逐尺度学习投影压缩为紧凑潜在表示。潜在空间由尺度内平滑约束（强制相邻补丁间的时间连续性）和尺度间对齐约束（通过可学习跨尺度映射恢复跨粒度交互）组织，确保所有尺度编码相互一致的底层动力学表示。

Result: 在十个真实基准的 40 个预测设置中，M2Patch 获得 57 个最优和 34 个次优结果，匹配或超越代表性基线，同时保持线性计算复杂度和对补丁级输入损坏的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: M2Patch 通过结构化潜在空间和互补可微约束，有效利用多尺度时间模式的组织几何，实现了高精度、高效率且鲁棒的多变量时间序列预测。

Abstract: Multivariate time series encode structural patterns that unfold across multiple temporal scales, yet most forecasting backbones treat learned representations as transient byproducts of prediction, leaving the organizational geometry of these patterns underexploited. We introduce M2Patch, a CNN-based forecasting architecture that maps channel-independent multivariate observations into a structured latent space through two complementary differentiable constraints. Multi-scale patching decomposes the input into overlapping temporal granularities; depthwise separable convolutions with progressive dilation extract scale-specific features in linear time; and per-scale learned projections compress these features into a compact latent representation. The latent space is organized by an intra-scale smoothness constraint that enforces temporal continuity between adjacent patches, and an inter-scale alignment constraint, realized through learnable cross-scale mappings, that restores cross-granularity interaction within the channel-independent design, ensuring that all scales encode mutually consistent representations of the underlying dynamics. Experiments on ten real-world benchmarks show that M2Patch achieves 57 best and 34 second-best results across 40 forecasting settings, matching or exceeding representative baselines on most benchmarks while maintaining linear computational complexity and robustness to patch-level input corruption.

</details>


### [137] [Good Practice Guide for quantifying uncertainties for machine learning models applied to photoplethysmography signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19999)
*P. Harris,C. Bench,M. Rinkevičius,V. Marozas,L. Coquelin,A. Thompson,M. Nandi,U. Hackstein,P. J. Aston*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本指南来自QUMPHY项目，为基于光电容积脉搏波（PPG）信号的机器学习模型提供不确定性量化的最佳实践指导，涵盖模型选择、不确定性量化方法（模型相关与模型无关）、基准问题与数据集、软件工具及伦理考量，并给出总结建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对可穿戴设备PPG信号机器学习应用中缺乏标准化不确定性量化指导的问题，旨在建立统一的最佳实践框架，提升模型在临床与健康监测场景中的可靠性与可信度。

Method: 1. 综述适用于PPG信号的机器学习模型类型，对比回归与分类任务下的模型性能；2. 详述模型相关（如贝叶斯神经网络、集成方法）与模型无关（如置信度校准、一致性预测）的不确定性量化技术实现与验证流程；3. 定义六个基准问题并提供对应公开数据集指引；4. 介绍辅助实现的软件工具；5. 简要分析数据隐私、偏见公平性等伦理问题。

Result: 形成涵盖模型选型指南、不确定性量化方法实现与验证规范、标准化基准测试体系、开源软件支持及伦理合规建议的完整良好实践指南。

Conclusion: 建议实践者根据任务特性选择合适模型与不确定性量化方法，利用基准问题进行验证比较，集成推荐软件工具提升开发效率，并重视伦理合规要求，从而构建更可靠、可解释的PPG机器学习应用。

Abstract: This Good Practice Guide presents work done in the QUMPHY project (Uncertainty quantification for machine learning models applied to photoplethysmography signals) that considered both machine learning and uncertainty quantification for problems which used photoplethysmography (PPG) signals from wearable devices as input. It provides high-level guidance on what types of machine learning model might be used and how different models compare when applied to both regression and classification tasks. It provides guidance on the implementation of different methods for uncertainty quantification, covering both model-dependent and model-independent techniques, and on the validation of the results provided by those methods. It also describes six benchmark problems together with pointers to different benchmark datasets for each problem. Software is described that can assist practitioners in implementing the methods described herein and there is a brief consideration of ethical issues. It concludes with a summary and recommendations.

</details>


### [138] [Post-Training in Time Series Foundation Models: A Unifying Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20002)
*Shifeng Xie,Ambroise Odonnat,Zehao Xiao,Lei Zan,Malik Tiomoko,Lujia Pan,Themis Palpanas,Boris N. Oreshkin,Chenghao Liu,Keli Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为时间序列基础模型（TSFM）的后训练方法提供了一个统一框架，基于预测管道中的干预位置将其分为五大类，并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠预训练往往不足以实现 TSFM 的可靠下游部署，领域偏移、任务异构、有限监督和计算约束等问题促使需要后训练方法来适配、增强、组合、校准或专用化预训练模型。

Method: 作者基于预测管道中的干预位置分析 TSFM 后训练方法，划分为五类：参数适配、上下文增强、模型组合、输出处理与不确定性控制、压缩与专用化。在每类中研究代表性方法并讨论其局限。

Result: 建立了 TSFM 后训练领域的统一框架，识别出五个未来方向：受控适配、可靠上下文构建、不确定性感知模型组合、校准输出处理、部署感知专用化。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来研究提供导向，帮助在预训练 TSFM 与其可靠下游部署之间的设计空间中导航。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) have emerged as general-purpose models for time series analysis, but pretraining alone is often insufficient for reliable downstream deployment. Bridging this gap requires further intervention to handle domain shift, task heterogeneity, limited supervision, and computational constraints, which motivates post-training as a broad class of methods to adapt, augment, compose, calibrate, or specialize pretrained TSFMs for downstream tasks. In this work, we analyze TSFM post-training methods based on their locus of intervention in the prediction pipeline, yielding five categories: parameter adaptation, context augmentation, model composition, output processing and uncertainty control, and compression and specialization. Within each category, we study main representative methods and discuss their current limitations. We further identify future directions toward controlled adaptation, reliable context construction, uncertainty-aware model composition, calibrated output processing, and deployment-aware specialization. Overall, by providing a unifying framework for the emerging TSFM post-training landscape, this work aims to support future research to navigate the design space between a pretrained TSFM and its reliable downstream deployment.

</details>


### [139] [Generalized Kalman filter based temporal difference reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20010)
*Vasos Arnaoutis,Eric Lutters,Bojana Rosić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件期望理论的广义时序差分(TD)强化学习框架，将价值函数和动作价值函数视为不确定量，将估计问题形式化为随机推断问题。该方法不依赖线性高斯假设，自然扩展到非线性模型和非高斯分布，能递归估计价值函数的条件期望及其二阶概率矩，从而量化学习过程中的不确定性。通过多项式混沌展开或集成近似离散化随机问题，获得计算上可行的算法。在线性质量-弹簧-阻尼系统和非线性腔体热传导问题上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于卡尔曼滤波的时序差分学习依赖线性高斯假设，难以处理非线性系统和非高斯噪声。现有方法无法在学习过程中量化价值函数估计的不确定性。需要一种更通用的框架，能够处理更广泛的随机系统，同时提供不确定性量化。

Method: 基于条件期望理论建立广义TD框架，将价值函数和Q值函数视为随机变量，估计问题转化为随机推断。递归计算价值函数的条件期望（一阶矩）和二阶概率矩。为解决计算难题，采用多项式混沌展开或集成近似方法离散化随机变量，得到可计算的算法。

Result: 在线性质量-弹簧-阻尼系统和非线性腔体热传导最优控制问题上进行数值实验。结果表明：该方法能准确估计价值函数及其不确定性，成功将经典卡尔曼TD学习扩展到非线性和非高斯随机系统。

Conclusion: 提出的广义TD框架基于条件期望理论，摆脱了线性高斯限制，自然支持非线性模型和非高斯分布，并能在线量化价值函数估计的不确定性。通过多项式混沌展开或集成近似实现计算可行性，为随机最优控制和强化学习中的不确定性量化提供了新工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a generalized temporal-difference (TD) reinforcement learning framework based on the theory of conditional expectations. The value and action-value (Q-value) functions are treated as uncertain quantities, and their estimation is formulated as a stochastic inference problem. Unlike classical Kalman-based temporal-difference learning, which relies on linear-Gaussian assumptions, the proposed formulation is derived directly from the conditional expectation framework and naturally extends to nonlinear models and non-Gaussian probability distributions. The proposed method recursively estimates not only the conditional expectation of the value function but also its second probabilistic moment, thereby quantifying the uncertainty associated with the learned value function throughout the learning process. To obtain a computationally tractable algorithm, the stochastic problem is discretized using either polynomial chaos expansions or ensemble-based approximations, providing efficient representations of the underlying random variables. The proposed framework is demonstrated on two optimal control problems: a linear mass--spring--damper system and a nonlinear heat conduction problem in a closed cavity. The numerical examples illustrate the capability of the proposed method to accurately estimate both the value function and its associated uncertainty, while extending classical Kalman-based temporal-difference learning to a broader class of stochastic systems.

</details>


### [140] [Zero-Shot Heart Rate Variability Forecasting from Consumer Wearables Using Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20027)
*Luukas Peräkylä,Fahad Sohrab,Ville Hautamäki,Merja Heinäniemi,Sui Huang,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了三种时间序列基础模型（TimesFM、Chronos、MOIRAI）在真实可穿戴设备HRV数据上的短期预测性能，并提出了一种保留生理变异性的插补方法。结果显示，无需微调的TSFMs均优于传统基线，Chronos和TimesFM表现最佳，MASE在0.81-0.87之间，确立了TSFMs在真实碎片化数据上的基准，并指出领域微调是临床部署的有前景方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床需要短期HRV预测以提供自主神经功能障碍和心脏不良事件的可操作提前量，但消费级可穿戴设备产生的HRV信号碎片化且含噪声大，传统预测方法难以应对。

Method: 在49名健康个体的真实可穿戴HRV数据上，对比三种TSFMs（TimesFM、Chronos、MOIRAI）与三种传统基线（均值、指数平滑、指数加权移动平均）。针对数据碎片化，提出变异性保留插补法：在线性插值基础上叠加局部自适应随机噪声，保留生理动力学特征。预测时长达2小时，上下文长度32和64步。

Result: 所有TSFMs均在无微调情况下超越基线，平均MASE在0.81-0.87区间。Chronos和TimesFM为表现最佳模型，MOIRAI相对基线提升有限。结果确立了TSFMs在真实可穿戴数据上的预测基准。

Conclusion: TSFMs展现出在碎片化、含噪可穿戴HRV数据上进行短期预测的潜力，领域特定微调有望进一步提升性能以支持临床部署。

Abstract: Short-term Heart Rate Variability (HRV) forecasting could provide clinicians with actionable lead time for detecting autonomic dysfunction and adverse cardiac events. Consumer wearable devices generate fragmented, artifact-rich HRV signals that challenge conventional forecasting approaches. In this study, we evaluated the forecasting ability of three Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs), TimesFM, Chronos, and MOIRAI, against traditional baselines (Mean, Exponential Smoothing, and Exponentially Weighted Moving Average) on real-world wearable data collected from 49 healthy individuals. To address data fragmentation, we introduce a variability-preserving imputation method that augments linear interpolation with locally adaptive stochastic noise, retaining physiological dynamics essential for accurate forecasting. The results show that TSFMs outperformed all baselines without fine-tuning, achieving average Mean Absolute Scaled Error (MASE) between 0.81 and 0.87 across TSFMs and both context lengths (32 and 64 time steps), with Chronos and TimesFM as the top models, though MOIRAI showed limited gains over baselines. With up to a 2-hour forecast horizon, the results establish a baseline for TSFMs' performance on a real-world dataset, highlighting domain-specific fine-tuning as a promising direction for clinical deployment.

</details>


### [141] [Test Case Prioritization for DNNs via Neural Collapse Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20046)
*Chunyu Liu,Mingyuan Li,Yang Li,Wenmin Li,Fei Gao,Tengfei Tu,Su-Juan Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于神经崩溃启发的测试用例优先级框架(NCIP)，利用终端训练阶段跨检查点的预测变异性替代单一检查点置信度，通过等角性得分选择代表性检查点子集，并根据测试输入在选定检查点上的预测变异性进行优先级排序，显著提升了早期故障发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试用例优先级技术通常依赖单一检查点的输出概率置信度信号，但深度神经网络可能自信地犯错，且预测类别与竞争类别之间的置信度边际往往很小，削弱了早期故障发现的效果。

Method: NCIP框架包含两个关键组件：1) 利用分类器权重的等角性得分（类别权重向量两两余弦相似度的标准差）选择神经崩溃指导的代表性训练检查点子集；2) 根据测试输入在选定检查点上的预测变异性进行优先级排序，识别靠近决策边界且易失败的不稳定样本。

Result: 在多个数据集和架构上的大量实验表明，NCIP在早期故障发现方面表现优异，与竞争基线相比，RAUC-ALL提升1.5%至16.6%，RAUC-500提升4.9%至20.6%，并在所有数据集-模型对上实现了最佳平均性能。

Conclusion: NCIP通过利用终端训练阶段模型几何结构的高度有序性，用跨检查点预测变异性替代绝对置信度，有效解决了单一检查点置信度不可靠的问题，显著降低了有限测试预算下的模型验证成本。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of deep neural networks (DNNs) in safety-critical domains, reducing the cost of model validation under limited testing budgets has become increasingly important. Existing test case prioritization techniques often rely on single-checkpoint confidence signals derived from output probabilities. However, DNNs can be confidently wrong, and the confidence margin between the predicted and competing classes is frequently small, which weakens early fault discovery. To address this limitation, we propose a Neural-Collapse-Inspired Prioritization (NCIP) framework that replaces absolute confidence with cross-checkpoint prediction variability in the terminal training regime, where model geometry becomes highly structured. NCIP introduces two key components. First, it selects an NC-guided representative subset of training checkpoints using an equiangularity score of classifier weights, quantified as the standard deviation of pairwise cosine similarities among class weight vectors. Second, it prioritizes test inputs by their prediction variability across the selected checkpoints, surfacing boundary-adjacent and failure-prone samples that are unstable under checkpoint-induced decision boundary shifts. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets and architectures show that NCIP achieves strong performance in early fault discovery compared with competitive baselines, with 1.5 to 16.6 percent RAUC-ALL gains and 4.9 to 20.6 percent RAUC-500 gains under the same testing budget. NCIP further attains the best average performance across all dataset-model pairs.

</details>


### [142] [Evaluating and Mitigating Gender Bias in Pre-trained Embeddings for ML-based Recruitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20073)
*Farnaz Faramarzi Lighvan,Lynn Houthuys*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估预训练语言模型嵌入在简历筛选中的性别偏见泄露问题，发现显式性别清洗虽能大幅降低但无法完全消除性别泄露，对抗学习在原始文本上提升公平性效果较好，作为文本级去偏的补充策略而非替代，多目标帕累托前沿选择可平衡预测效用与公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机器学习的AI招聘系统依赖历史简历数据训练，存在延续和放大社会偏见的风险。核心挑战在于非结构化简历文本中，预训练语言模型嵌入可能在显式敏感属性（如性别）被移除后仍能推断出这些属性。

Method: 1) 在合成FairCVdb数据集上评估九种预训练嵌入模型，分析其对申请人评分的信息量及在原始和性别清洗后简历上的性别泄露敏感性。2) 采用带梯度反转的多任务对抗学习框架，预测申请人适配度同时抑制学习表征中的性别信息。3) 使用多目标帕累托前沿模型选择平衡预测效用与公平性。

Result: 显式性别清洗能大幅降低但无法完全消除性别泄露；对抗学习主要在原始简历上改善公平性，作为文本级去偏的补充策略而非替代方案。

Conclusion: 单纯依赖文本层面的性别清洗不足以消除嵌入中的偏见，表示层面的对抗去偏可作为有效补充，结合帕累托前沿选择可实现效用与公平的权衡，为构建更公平的AI招聘系统提供了实证指导。

Abstract: AI-based recruitment systems that rely on machine learning models trained on historical CV data, risk perpetuating and amplifying social biases. A key challenge arises in unstructured CV text, where pre-trained language model embeddings may infer sensitive attributes such as gender even after explicit indicators are removed. In this paper, we evaluate nine pre-trained embedding models on the synthetic FairCVdb dataset, analyzing the informativeness of their embeddings for applicant scoring and their susceptibility to gender leakage, on both original and gender-scrubbed biographies. We further use a multi-task adversarial learning framework with gradient reversal to predict applicant suitability while suppressing gender information from learned representations. Finally, we use a multi-objective Pareto-front-based model selection to balance predictive utility and fairness. Our experimental results show that explicit gender scrubbing substantially reduces but does not eliminate gender leakage, while adversarial learning improves fairness mainly on original biographies and acts as a complementary strategy rather than a substitute for text-level debiasing.

</details>


### [143] [Co-Evolving LLM Evaluators and Policies via DynamicRubric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20083)
*Beining Wang,Weihang Su,Hongtao Tian,Hao Kong,Tao Yang,Ting Yao,Qingyi Pan,Yueyue Wu,Qingyao Ai,Min Zhang,Yiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出评估器评分差距收敛导致策略优化信号减弱，提出DynamicRubric框架，通过动态生成加权二元评分项并聚合为响应级评分，实现评估器与策略协同进化，显著提升评估性能和策略监督质量，并在微信搜索大规模部署验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着策略改进，采样响应质量趋近，导致评估器相对评分差距崩塌，产生微弱或误导性的策略监督信号；理论分析表明相对评分差距正是指导策略更新的优化信号，因此需要评估器随策略协同进化以保持有效监督。

Method: 提出DynamicRubric框架，针对每个候选响应集动态生成加权二元评分项，聚合判断为响应级评分，实现评估器-策略协同进化。

Result: 在8B骨干模型上，DynamicRubric评估器性能优于70B奖励模型和235B静态评分生成器基线，提供更强策略监督；优化后策略在可验证推理和编码任务上提升；全量部署于微信搜索AI问答场景，日均服务数千万请求，关键在线指标改善。

Conclusion: 评估器应随其监督的策略协同进化，DynamicRubric验证了这一原则，为评估器引导的后训练提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Post-training with evaluator feedback on policy-induced samples serves as a major mechanism for improving large language models. As policies improve, these sampled responses become close in quality. These close candidates create a bottleneck for policy optimization: collapsed relative evaluator score gaps yield weak or misleading policy supervision. We theoretically characterize why these gaps matter through a probability allocation view, showing that the directional gain of shifting probability mass from one response to another is exactly the evaluator score gap between them. This identifies relative score gaps as the policy optimization signals that guide updates. Motivated by this view, we propose DynamicRubric, a response-set-conditioned evaluator--policy co-evolution framework that generates weighted binary rubric items for each candidate set and aggregates the resulting judgments into response-level scores. In our experiments with 8B backbones, DynamicRubric improves evaluator performance and provides stronger policy supervision than baselines using a 70B reward model or a 235B static rubric generator. DynamicRubric-optimized policies also show gains on verifiable reasoning and coding tasks. A DynamicRubric-optimized model is fully deployed in WeChat Search's AI answering scenario, where it serves all online traffic across tens of millions of requests per day and improves key online metrics. These results suggest a principle for evaluator-guided post-training: evaluators should evolve with the policies they supervise.

</details>


### [144] [Autonomous Collaborative Learning Among an Ensemble of Tsetlin Machines with Consensus-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20124)
*Yehuda Rudin,Osnat Keren,Michal Yemini,Alexander Fish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于共识推理的垂直特征分区去中心化协作学习范式，用于 Tsetlin Machine 集成，实现无原始数据交换的多模态感知融合，实验显示分类精度可媲美集中式模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究较少关注分布式和去中心化的 Tsetlin Machine 学习，尤其是垂直特征分区场景下的协作学习，需要进一步探索。

Method: 提出去中心化协作学习范式，每个代理维护私有 TM 模型，通过共识推理融合各代理预测，适用于异构代理（不同数据获取方式、本地数据分布、计算资源），支持垂直特征分区，无原始数据交换。

Result: 在二维网格和连通图网络拓扑上的实验表明，该方法达到的分类准确率与集中式模型相当。

Conclusion: 该范式实现了去中心化环境下 Tsetlin Machine 的有效协作学习，为多模态感知融合提供了可行方案，且性能接近集中式基准。

Abstract: Tsetlin Machine (TM) is a rule-based machine-learning algorithm comprising collectives of two-action Tsetlin Automata (TAs) that cooperatively form conjunctive logical clauses from Boolean inputs through stochastic feedback. Although few recent studies have examined TM Federated Learning, the broader area of distributed and decentralized TM learning has not received much attention in the existing literature and warrants further exploration. In this work, we propose a paradigm for decentralized collaborative learning under a vertical feature-partitioning setting among an ensemble of Tsetlin Machines using consensus-based inference. Within this decentralized paradigm, each agent maintains its own private TM model, and there is no exchange of raw data among agents. Inference combines individual agents model predictions into a global consensus. The paradigm accommodates heterogeneous TM-based agents with differing data acquisition means, local data distributions, or computational resources, thereby facilitating the integration and fusion of information in settings such as multi-modal sensing environments. Experiments conducted using two-dimensional grid and connected graph network topologies demonstrate that the classification accuracies achieved are comparable to those of centralized models.

</details>


### [145] [CURED: Creating, Understanding, and Repairing Errors Demonstrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20140)
*Nicholas Chandler,Sebastian Jäger,Philipp Jung,Felix Bießmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文展示了一个基于Web的演示器，结合机器学习方法用于表格数据的错误检测与清洗，支持用户上传数据、注入真实错误并使用现代ML算法进行清洗和错误机制分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中的错误检测与清洗是数据密集型软件应用的前提。机器学习与数据库管理系统交叉研究显示统计学习算法在错误检测和清洗方面的潜力，但理论进展与直观实践洞察之间存在差距。

Method: 开发了一个统一的Web演示器，允许用户上传表格数据，用真实的数据依赖错误扰动数据，并应用现代机器学习方法进行清洗和理解错误机制。该演示器整合了作者近期在基于ML的数据清洗和错误模型方面的工作。

Result: 演示器已部署并可在https://cured.demo.calgo-lab.de/访问，成功弥合了错误模型和数据清洗算法在理论进展与直观实践洞察之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该演示器为表格数据的错误检测和清洗提供了一个统一的实践平台，将机器学习方法与数据库技术相结合，使用户能够直观地理解和应用现代数据清洗算法。

Abstract: Detecting and cleaning errors in tabular data is a prerequisite for data intense software applications. Recent research at the intersection of Machine Learning (ML) and Database Management Systems (DBMS) highlights the potential of statistical learning algorithms for error detection and cleaning. This paper combines our recent work on ML-based data cleaning and error models in a unified demonstrator. The web application allows users to upload tabular data, perturb the data with realistic data dependent errors and use modern ML methods to clean and understand error mechanisms in data. Our demonstrator helps to bridge the gap between theoretical advancements and intuitive practical insights in the context of error models and data cleaning algorithms for tabular data. The demonstrator is available at https://cured.demo.calgo-lab.de/

</details>


### [146] [Active Inference as a Convex Markov Decision Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20152)
*Nikola Milosevic,Nicolás Hinrichs,Nico Scherf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将主动推理框架为策略优化问题，证明期望自由能最小化等价于凸马尔可夫决策过程，揭示认知价值作为策略依赖奖励的本质，推导镜像下降算法并建立与表演性强化学习的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将主动推理中的期望自由能最小化原理置于现代强化学习和优化理论框架中，解决其与标准强化学习的联系不明、缺乏收敛性分析和策略改进保证等理论空白。

Method: 1) 将闭环控制策略下的EFE最小化表述为凸MDP；2) 分解实用项与认知项，分析有限时域、折扣和平均奖励三种公式化；3) 推导镜像下降算法，在当前状态边际分布处局部线性化目标函数；4) 建立世界模型学习与策略优化耦合的表演性强化学习结构。

Result: 实用项等价于潜在MDP中的奖励最大化；认知项引入非线性成分，表现为策略依赖的表演性奖励；镜像下降算法产生兼容Actor-Critic和动态规划的策略依赖奖励；主动推理具备表演性RL结构，获得收敛性分析和原则性策略改进保证。

Conclusion: 主动推理可被严格奠基于现代强化学习与优化理论之中，其核心机制等价于带有策略依赖认知奖励的凸MDP求解，为该领域提供了统一的理论基础和算法工具箱。

Abstract: Active Inference (AIF) frames adaptive behavior as the minimization of expected free energy (EFE), combining epistemic and pragmatic objectives within a single variational principle. We frame AIF as policy optimization and show that, for closed-loop control policies, EFE minimization can be formulated as a convex Markov decision process (MDP). In this formulation, the pragmatic terms are linear in the predictive state marginals and therefore equivalent to reward maximization in a latent MDP, while the epistemic value introduces a nonlinear component that distinguishes EFE minimization from standard reinforcement learning. This perspective further reveals the epistemic drive of active inference as a policy-dependent (performative) reward. We analyze finite-horizon, discounted, and average-reward formulations of EFE and derive a mirror descent (MD) algorithm that locally linearizes the objective around the current state marginals, yielding a policy-dependent reward that is compatible with actor-critic methods and dynamic programming. Finally, we argue that coupling world-model learning with policy optimization gives active inference the structure of performative reinforcement learning, providing a route toward grounding active inference within modern reinforcement learning and optimization theory, including convergence analysis and principled policy improvement guarantees.

</details>


### [147] [Local Stability and Gaussian Smoothing of Quantized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20153)
*Sergey Salishev,Anton Makarov,Oleg Granichin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究高斯平均作为量化神经网络的平滑代理，推导了有界局部振荡下的局部维度相关误差界，计算了ReLU和sign激活函数的闭式高斯平均，并在高维二值感知机上演示了该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化神经网络存在不连续性，导致训练和推理困难；需要平滑代理来替代量化模型，以便进行梯度优化和稳定性分析。

Method: 采用高斯平均作为平滑代理，在有界局部振荡假设下推导|f-g|的局部维度相关界；计算ReLU和sign激活函数的闭式高斯平均；在高维二值感知机上验证，展示层预激活聚合在显式量化噪声代理下产生的高斯包络。

Result: 得到了连接高斯平滑与不连续网络稳定性分析的理论界；获得了ReLU和sign激活函数的闭式高斯平均表达式；在二值感知机实验中展示了推理端平滑和训练端平滑代理梯度的有效性。

Conclusion: 高斯平均为量化神经网络提供了有效的平滑代理，理论界建立了平滑度与稳定性的联系，闭式平均形式便于实现，在二值感知机上验证了其在推理和训练中的应用潜力。

Abstract: We study Gaussian averaging as a smooth surrogate for quantized neural models. Under bounded local oscillation, we derive a local dimension-dependent bound on |f-g|, linking Gaussian smoothing to the stability analysis of discontinuous networks. We compute closed-form Gaussian averages of the rectified linear unit (ReLU) and sign activation functions, and illustrate the mechanism on a high-dimensional binary perceptron, where layer-preactivation aggregation under an explicit quantization-noise surrogate yields the Gaussian envelope used in inference-side smoothing and training-side smooth surrogate gradients.

</details>


### [148] [Self-organizing Architecture of Receptron Units: a Hardware-Aware Framework for Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20162)
*Stefano Radice,Ludovico Casaccia,Riccaro Emanuele Beccalli,Bruno Paroli,Paolo Milani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于Receptron模型的神经形态启发分类器，该模型采用单单元架构即可实现非线性可分决策边界，无需多层网络，专为资源受限的中端MCU设计，支持持续设备端适应，在基准数据集上实现了与标准机器学习基线相当的交叉验证准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网边缘智能处理需求增长，但微控制器单元（MCU）严重的计算和内存限制使得传统深度学习方法难以实用化，因此需要一种轻量级、可部署于资源受限边缘设备且支持在线适应的分类器。

Method: 提出基于Receptron模型的神经形态启发分类器，采用单单元架构实现非线性可分决策边界，避免了多层网络的复杂性，设计用于直接部署在中端MCU上，并支持持续的设备端适应学习。

Result: 在基础数据集基准测试中，交叉验证准确率与标准机器学习方法基线相当，验证了Receptron作为资源受限神经形态边缘系统在动态非平稳环境下可行且可解释的替代方案。

Conclusion: Receptron模型为资源受限的神经形态边缘系统提供了一种可行、可解释的单单元分类器替代方案，能够在动态非平稳环境中实现持续的设备端适应，填补了轻量级边缘智能处理的空白。

Abstract: The growing demand for intelligent processing at the edge of IoT networks is constrained by the severe computational and memory limitations of microcontroller units, which render impractical conventional deep learning approaches. We propose a neuromorphicinspired classifier based on the Receptron model, a single-unit architecture capable of implementing non-linearly separable decision boundaries, without resorting to multi-layer networks. The model is designed for direct deployment on mid-range MCUs, while supporting continuous on-device adaptation. Experimental evaluation on basic dataset benchmarks yields cross-validated accuracies compatible with standard machine learning method baselines. These results position the Receptron as a viable and interpretable alternative for resource-constrained neuromorphic edge systems operating in dynamic, non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [149] [Instance Hardness-Based Relevance for Imbalanced Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20173)
*Vitor M. Leitao,Juscimara G. Avelino,George D. C. Cavalcanti,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于实例难度的相关性函数（InHaR）解决不平衡回归中稀有实例识别问题，结合学习难度而非仅依赖目标分布，在双峰分布场景下有效识别稀有区域，指导重采样策略显著提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统不平衡回归方法依赖仅基于目标值的固定相关性函数，在双峰等复杂分布下无法准确区分真正稀有与正常实例，导致稀有实例识别失效，需引入学习难度作为辅助判据。

Method: 提出InHaR相关性函数，融合实例硬度（学习难度）与目标分布信息，动态评估实例稀有度；将其应用于随机过采样（RO）和高斯噪声（GN）等重采样策略指导稀有样本生成。

Result: 实验表明InHaR在双峰分布下正确识别稀有区域，配合RO/GN重采样显著优于传统基于相关性的方法，提升预测性能。

Conclusion: InHaR通过引入实例难度解决了传统相关性函数在复杂分布下的局限性，为不平衡回归中的稀有实例识别提供了更鲁棒的解决方案，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Imbalanced regression problems arise when the target variable has an asymmetric distribution, resulting in underrepresented value ranges in the dataset. Traditional approaches for identifying rare instances rely on a relevance function that assigns higher importance to specific regions of the target distribution. However, the effectiveness of imbalance-aware learning methods depends strongly on how relevance is defined. In more complex scenarios, such as bimodal distributions, traditional relevance functions struggle to capture rarity, as they assign fixed relevance values based solely on target values, thereby compromising the distinction between truly rare and normal instances. To address these limitations, this study proposes an Instance Hardness-based relevance function (InHaR) for identifying rare instances in regression problems. Unlike traditional relevance functions, the proposed approach incorporates learning difficulty, allowing rarity to be inferred not only from the target distribution but also from the difficulty of instances for the learning algorithm. This property is particularly important in bimodal scenarios, where rarity cannot be accurately inferred from target values alone. Experimental results demonstrate that the InHaR correctly identifies rare regions under bimodal distributions and, when used to guide resampling strategies such as Random Oversampling (RO) and Gaussian Noise (GN), leads to significant improvements in predictive performance compared to traditional relevance-based approaches. The code, dataset, and further details about the proposed method are publicly available at https://github.com/VitorLeitao/instance-hardness-Imbalanced-regression.

</details>


### [150] [On Optimization Complexity of Second-Order Certified Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20192)
*Nikita Doikov,Anastasia Koloskova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从优化视角研究机器遗忘的算法复杂性，提出基于均匀凸正则化子的新型界，并开发了一种带有各向异性高斯机制的二阶遗忘算法，证明了其在准自协调损失线性模型上的快速收敛率，展示了二阶信息相对于一阶方法的可证优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是解决机器遗忘中认证遗忘与优化精度的同时达成问题，从优化角度分析算法复杂度，并寻求利用二阶信息提升遗忘效率的理论依据。

Method: 利用均匀凸正则化子推导初始模型与遗忘模型距离的新界，引入一种新的泛化误差替代量；设计基于各向异性高斯机制的二阶遗忘算法，针对准自协调损失的线性模型证明全局收敛速率。

Result: 理论证明：若被移除数据能被遗忘模型良好预测，则优化问题变得简单；提出的二阶算法在逻辑斯蒂回归和指数回归等任务上实现了认证遗忘的快速收敛率，并证明了二阶方法相对于一阶方法的可证优势。

Conclusion: 论文从优化理论建立了认证遗忘的新界，开发了具有最优全局收敛的二阶遗忘算法，为机器遗忘提供了更高效且有理论保证的解决方案。

Abstract: We study machine unlearning: the removal of memorized training data from a trained model. Specifically, we investigate the algorithmic complexity of certified unlearning from an optimization perspective. We formalize the goal of an unlearning algorithm as simultaneously achieving certified unlearning and optimization accuracy. Utilizing the notion of uniformly convex regularizers, we prove new bounds on the distance between initial and unlearned models using a novel substitute for generalization error. Thus we theoretically demonstrate that if the removed data is well-predicted by the unlearned model, the corresponding optimization problem is simple. Furthermore, we develop a new second-order unlearning algorithm with an anisotropic Gaussian mechanism and state-of-the-art global convergence. We prove fast rates for our method in achieving certified unlearning for linear models with quasi-self-concordant losses. As a direct application, our theory covers unlearning for logistic and exponential regressions and shows a provable benefit of utilizing second-order information compared to first-order unlearning methods.

</details>


### [151] [OLEDLM: A Unified Language Model for OLED Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20194)
*Fukang Wen,Yuchong Tang,Jingyuan Li,Beichen Wang,Yixuan Jiang,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yaxuan Liu,Shunyu Wang,Zuoqiang Shi,Yi Zhu,Yanan Zhu,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果语言模型的逆向分子设计框架，通过多阶段策略（基础化学语言模型预训练、属性预测器微调、强化学习优化、DFT验证）实现了针对OLED材料的高效生成，成功生成具有高结构有效性和优化光电性质的新型候选分子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OLED材料开发面临化学空间极大、量子化学约束严格、标注数据稀缺等复合挑战，现有针对该领域的有效生成模型很少，亟需一种能够直接根据目标光电性质生成满足约束的SMILES序列的方法。

Method: 采用多阶段策略：1) 使用LLaMA风格的Transformer架构建立基础化学语言模型，实现LLM在OLED领域的首次成功适配；2) 基于大规模OLED数据集预训练的BERT模型微调属性预测器；3) 利用属性预测器对微调模型进行强化学习，优化SMILES生成；4) 通过DFT计算验证生成分子的光电性质。

Result: 框架能高效探索OLED化学空间，生成的新候选分子具有高结构有效性和优化的光电性质（如激发能、振子强度等），通过DFT验证确认了生成分子的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了通用分子生成与光电材料严格结构要求之间的空白，代表了LLM在OLED领域的首次成功适配，为逆向分子设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: The development of organic light-emitting diode (OLED) materials faces the compounded challenges of an astronomically large chemical space, stringent quantum-chemical constraints, and a scarcity of labeled data. Although the question of OLED generation is important, few models have been trained effectively for this specific domain. We propose an inverse molecular design framework based on causal language models: given target optoelectronic properties (e.g., excitation energy, oscillator strength), our model directly generates OLED SMILES sequences satisfying the specified constraints. We employ a multi-stage strategy: first, we establish a foundational chemical language model using a LLaMA-style transformer architecture. To the best of our knowledge, this represents the first successful adaptation of LLMs specifically for the OLED domain, bridging the gap between generic molecular generation and the stringent structural requirements of optoelectronic materials. Second, we fine-tune property predictors based on a BERT model pre-trained on our large-scale OLED dataset. Then, we perform Reinforcement Learning on our fine-tuned model, leveraging our property predictor, for better SMILES generation. Finally, through DFT verification, we demonstrate that our framework can efficiently navigate the OLED chemical space, generating novel candidates with high structural validity and optimized optoelectronic properties.

</details>


### [152] [The Quadrilateral Loss: Additivity as a Measurable Behavior of Dense Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20201)
*Antonio Di Cecco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种可微分的“四边形损失”，将加性约束视为可测量的行为而非硬性架构约束，该损失能有效移除特征交互，在小数据集上同时提升精度和加性，并揭示了预正则化交互排序的不可靠性。对比多种实现精确加性的路径发现，行为约束优于结构约束，不同方法收敛到相同的形状函数，且记录了三种因前提条件被隐性违反而导致的静默失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加性模型通过禁止特征交互获得可解释性，但神经网络实例通常通过架构硬性强制加性。作者希望将加性视为一种可测量的行为，引入可调节的惩罚项，使加性成为一个可调的‘旋钮’和在线可观测的指标。

Method: 引入四边形损失：一种基于交换单个坐标的训练点对的二阶混合差分的可微分惩罚。该损失当且仅当坐标不携带交互时为零，对分段线性网络保持信息量，且在期望下等于干预 Shapley-GAM 的逐坐标交互质量。通过逐特征投降曲线在线监控加性。

Result: 大多数学到的交互几乎无成本可移除；小数据集上适度惩罚同时提升精度和加性。预正则化交互大小几乎无法预测正则化后保留的交互，削弱了事后交互排序的可靠性。对比结构掩码、行为惩罚、权重衰减、回拟、共享段模型和袋装提升树桩等路径：行为优先于结构的约束优于权重空间约束，数据 regime 下排名反转，收敛路径在形状函数上达成一致。发现三种静默失效模式，共同点是将保证引入了隐性违反其前提条件的设置中。

Conclusion: 四边形损失提供了一种灵活的、基于行为的加性约束方法，优于硬性结构约束，揭示了事后交互排序的局限性，并强调了理论保证前提条件被隐性违反时的风险。

Abstract: Additive models buy interpretability by forbidding feature interactions, a constraint that neural instantiations enforce architecturally. We introduce the quadrilateral loss, a differentiable penalty that treats additivity as a measurable behavior instead: a second-order mixed difference on pairs of training points swapping one coordinate, which vanishes if and only if the coordinate carries no interaction, remains informative for piecewise-linear networks, and equals in expectation the per-coordinate interaction mass of the interventional Shapley-GAM. The loss turns additivity into a dial - most learned interactions prove removable almost for free, and on small datasets a moderate penalty improves accuracy and additivity simultaneously - and into an online observable: its per-feature surrender curves show, across seeds and datasets, that pre-regularization interaction magnitude barely predicts what a regularized model retains, undermining post-hoc interaction rankings. Against this instrument we compare routes to exact additivity, spanning structural masks, behavioral penalties (optionally crystallized into exact structure), weight decay, backfitting, the shared-section model, and bagged boosted stumps: constraining behavior before structure dominates weight-space constraints, rankings reverse between data regimes, and converging routes agree on the shape functions themselves. Three silent failure modes we document share one anatomy: guarantees imported into settings that quietly void their preconditions.

</details>


### [153] [ELSAA: Efficient Low-Rank and Sparse Attention Approximation for Training Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20214)
*Mahdi Heidari,Mohammad Mahdi Rahimi,Jaekyun Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELSAA是一种高效的低秩稀疏注意力近似方法，通过在Q,K,V稠密投影后直接近似注意力分数算子，结合稀疏分支捕捉高相似度交互和低秩分支总结全局扩散交互，并引入分母感知融合项平衡两分支，实现了不显性化二次分数矩阵的长上下文训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的二次注意力分数矩阵是扩展长序列的核心瓶颈。现有高效注意力方法要么施加稀疏性，要么使用低秩/核草图压缩全局交互，但缺乏将两者结合且不分解投影矩阵的统一框架。

Method: ELSAA在稠密投影生成Q,K,V后，直接近似注意力分数算子：稀疏分支捕获选定的高相似度交互，低秩分支总结扩散的全局交互。引入分母感知融合项，根据稀疏分支相对于低秩分支的估计注意力质量缩放稀疏分支，实现两分支的有效融合。

Result: 提出了一种构建低秩稀疏注意力输出的实用框架，无需显性化完整的二次分数矩阵，在保持锐利的token级交互和广泛上下文混合的同时，支持更长上下文的训练。

Conclusion: ELSAA通过创新的分母感知融合机制，成功将稀疏注意力和低秩注意力结合，为长序列Transformer提供了兼顾局部精细交互和全局上下文建模的高效解决方案。

Abstract: The quadratic $N\times N$ attention score matrix remains a central obstacle to extending Transformers to longer input lengths. Existing efficient attention methods usually reduce this bottleneck by either imposing sparsity, so that each query attends to only a small subset of keys, or by using low-rank/kernel sketches, so that global interactions are compressed into a lower-dimensional representation. We propose \emph{ELSAA}, an efficient low-rank and sparse approximation of attention. Importantly, ELSAA does \emph{not} decompose the learned projection or output matrices of the Transformer into sparse and low-rank factors. Instead, after dense projections produce $Q,K,V$, ELSAA approximates the induced attention score operator itself: a sparse branch captures selected high-similarity interactions, while a low-rank branch summarizes diffuse global interactions. Since the two branches can be normalized over supports with very different denominator mass, ELSAA introduces a denominator-aware fusion term that scales the sparse branch according to its estimated attention mass relative to the low-rank branch. This gives a practical framework for constructing low-rank and sparse attention outputs without materializing the full quadratic score matrix, aiming to enable longer-context training while preserving both sharp token-level interactions and broad contextual mixing.

</details>


### [154] [User-Centric Modeling of Transactional Sequences with Explainable State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20228)
*Ivan Palagin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合方法，将对比学习(CoLES)与选择性状态空间模型(Mamba)结合，用于用户中心的交易事件序列建模。通过两种融合策略(初始化隐藏状态或前置前缀token)，模型从第一步就获得了信息丰富的用户先验。在三个公开数据集上，混合模型持续优于单独的Mamba和CoLES+线性分类器基线，收敛速度提升2-3倍，并通过可解释性分析揭示了选择性事件过滤和关键交易特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户序列建模方法面临困境：对比学习(CoLES)虽能产出高质量用户表示，但其编码器(RNN/Transformer)分别存在梯度消失和二次复杂度问题；Mamba作为选择性SSM虽能高效处理长距离依赖，但在个性化用户分析领域尚未充分探索。因此需要一种融合方案，兼顾表示质量与建模效率。

Method: 提出两种CoLES与Mamba融合策略：(1) 用CoLES嵌入初始化Mamba隐藏状态；(2) 将投影后的CoLES嵌入作为前缀token拼接到输入序列前端。两种方式均使模型从首步获得信息丰富的用户先验。

Result: 在Age(年龄组多分类)、MBD(多标签产品获取)、Taobao(二分类购买预测)三个数据集上，混合模型持续超越单独Mamba和CoLES+线性分类器基线，收敛速度加快2-3倍。可解释性分析(离散化步长图、Integrated Gradients)显示：在行为丰富数据集上表现出选择性事件过滤，并识别出最具信息量的交易特征。

Conclusion: CoLES与Mamba的混合架构有效结合了对比学习的表示能力与选择性SSM的长序列建模效率，通过简单的先验注入机制显著提升预测性能与训练速度，同时提供了可解释的用户行为洞察。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid approach for user-centric modeling of transactional event sequences that combines contrastive representation learning (CoLES) with State Space Models (SSMs). While contrastive methods yield high-quality compressed user representations, existing encoders -- RNNs and Transformers -- suffer from vanishing gradients or quadratic complexity, respectively. Mamba, a selective SSM, efficiently handles long-range dependencies but remains underexplored for personalized user analysis. We investigate two integration strategies: (1)~initializing the Mamba hidden state with a CoLES embedding, and (2)~prepending the projected CoLES embedding as a prefix token to the input sequence. Both approaches supply the model with an informative user prior from the first step. Experiments on three public datasets -- Age (multiclass age-group prediction), MBD (multi-label product acquisition), and Taobao (binary purchase prediction) -- demonstrate consistent improvements over standalone Mamba and CoLES with a linear classifier, with the hybrid models converging 2--3$\times$ faster than the plain SSM baseline. Explainability analysis via discretization-step maps and Integrated Gradients reveals selective event filtering on behavior-rich datasets and identifies the most informative transaction features.

</details>


### [155] [PIER: Physics-Informed Environmental Retrieval for Time-Series Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20230)
*Shiyuan Luo,Runlong Yu,Chonghao Qiu,Yue Qin,Rahul Ghosh,Robert Ladwig,Paul C. Hanson,Yiqun Xie,Xiaowei Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PIER是一个模型无关的框架，通过物理感知流增强基于嵌入的检索，利用通量-响应一致性评分和自适应权重调整，在湖泊水温和溶解氧预测任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境系统建模面临观测数据有限和物理动力学跨系统差异大的挑战，标准基于嵌入的检索无法保证底层物理过程的一致性，相似嵌入可能源于不同物理机制。

Method: 提出Physics-Informed Environmental Retrieval (PIER)框架：1) 增加物理感知流，用局部验证器基于物理衍生的通量特征对候选进行通量-响应一致性评分；2) 引入权重调整机制，基于诊断特征自适应平衡嵌入检索流和物理检索流。

Result: 在中西部美国356个湖泊、跨越41年的实验中，PIER在水温和溶解氧预测上持续优于基线方法，并作为通用增强策略适用于多样化主干网络。

Conclusion: PIER通过物理知情检索确保物理过程一致性，有效解决了环境系统建模中跨系统知识迁移的物理一致性问题，为环境科学提供了可泛化的检索增强范式。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of environmental systems is fundamental to scientific understanding and decision-making, yet remains challenging because observations are limited and physical dynamics vary across systems. Retrieval-augmented approaches offer a natural path to transfer knowledge across systems, but standard embedding-based retrieval does not guarantee consistency of underlying physical processes, since scenarios with similar embeddings may arise from different underlying mechanisms. We propose Physics-Informed Environmental Retrieval (PIER), a model-agnostic framework that augments embedding-based retrieval with a physics-aware stream that scores candidates by flux-response consistency with the target, using local verifiers trained on physics-derived flux features. A weight adjustment mechanism then learns per-scenario weights that adaptively balance the two retrieval streams based on diagnostic features summarizing physics-stream reliability. Experiments on 356 lakes across the Midwestern United States spanning 41 years show that PIER consistently outperforms baselines for water temperature and dissolved oxygen prediction, and serves as a general augmentation strategy across diverse backbones.

</details>


### [156] [PhaseAware: Interpretable Human-in-the-Loop Rehabilitation Scoring with Boundary Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20237)
*Yankai Zheng,Yuhe Liu,Yuxin Ma,Tianci Xue,Jiayuan Tian,Yu Fu,Yuxuan Hu,Jianing Wang,Zichun Xiao,Junya Mu,Shaohui Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PhaseAware是一个紧凑的持续康复质量评估框架，结合时序主干与阶段/身体组描述符，通过主干条件门控残差路径实现，在UI-PRMD深蹲协议上实现RMSE 0.0230（较基线降低88.9%），并在KIMORE数据集上泛化良好，还能生成可解释的阶段/身体级敏感性提示，支持临床医生审查和人工介入分诊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 康复评分系统在临床工作流中需要可被审查和解释的输出，现有方法可能缺乏可解释性、跨协议泛化能力或资源受限环境下的部署能力。

Method: 提出PhaseAware框架，包含时序主干网络、阶段描述符和身体组描述符，通过主干条件门控残差通路融合，生成连续评分并输出阶段/身体级敏感性提示，架构轻量化适合资源受限设备。

Result: 在UI-PRMD深蹲协议上RMSE 0.0230，较基线降低88.9%；在KIMORE深蹲子集上保持良好性能，验证跨协议迁移能力；生成的结构化提示能高亮关键运动阶段和身体区域。

Conclusion: PhaseAware提供了一种实用且可解释的康复评分方法，可将自动化评估集成到信息系统中，同时保留临床医生的监督角色，适合人工介入的分诊和边界情况监控。

Abstract: Rehabilitation scoring systems are most useful when their outputs can be reviewed and interpreted within clinical workflows. This study presents PhaseAware, a compact framework for continuous rehabilitation quality assessment that combines a temporal backbone with phase- and body-group descriptors through a backbone-conditioned gated residual pathway. The model was evaluated on the UI-PRMD deep-squat protocol and further tested on the KIMORE squatting subset. On UI-PRMD, PhaseAware achieved an RMSE of 0.0230, corresponding to an 88.9% reduction relative to the accepted baseline. It also maintained favorable performance on KIMORE, suggesting that the phase-aware design transfers across related squatting protocols. In addition to score prediction, PhaseAware generates structured review cues based on phase- and body-level sensitivity, highlighting the movement stages and body regions most relevant to each prediction. The architecture employs a backbone-conditioned gated residual mechanism to stabilize feature representation, supporting use in resource-constrained settings. These cues are intended to support clinician review, boundary-case monitoring, and human-in-the-loop triage rather than autonomous decision-making. Overall, PhaseAware offers a practical and interpretable approach to rehabilitation scoring that may help integrate automated assessment into information systems while preserving clinician oversight.

</details>


### [157] [Breaking the $T^{3/4}$ Barrier for Regret Minimization With Bi-Dimensional CDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20258)
*Matteo Castiglioni,Anna Lunghi,Alberto Marchesi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对CDF相关目标函数的遗憾最小化问题，提出算法实现Õ(T^{7/10})遗憾界，优于先前的Õ(T^{3/4})，部分缓解维数灾难，但与Ω(T^{2/3})下界仍有差距；应用于固定价格双边贸易利润最大化也得同样界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在未知分布D下，针对形式为g(x)·P(X≤x)的CDF相关目标函数的遗憾最小化，目标是改进现有遗憾界并探索维数灾难的缓解程度。

Method: 设计基于二分反馈的学习算法，利用Lipschitz函数g的性质和CDF估计技术，实现Õ(T^{7/10})遗憾界。

Result: 算法达到Õ(T^{7/10})遗憾，优于先前的Õ(T^{3/4})；在重复双边贸易固定价格利润最大化应用中同样取得该界。

Conclusion: 该工作部分缓解了该类目标的维数灾难，但与理论下界Ω(T^{2/3})仍有差距，未来工作可进一步缩小差距。

Abstract: We study regret minimization for learning CDF-related objectives of the form \[ g(x)\cdot\mathbb{P}_{X\sim\mathcal{D}}(X\le x), \] over $[0,1]^2$, where $g$ is a known Lipschitz function and $\mathcal{D}$ is an unknown distribution. At each round $t$, the learner selects a point $x_t$ and observes the binary feedback $\mathbb{I}(X_t\le x_t)$, where $X_t\sim\mathcal{D}$. We design an algorithm achieving regret $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})$, improving over the previous best-known bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{3/4})$ and showing that the curse of dimensionality can be at least partially lifted for this class of objectives, though a gap remains with the $Ω(T^{2/3})$ lower bound. As an application, our techniques yield the same $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})$ regret bound for profit maximization in repeated bilateral trade with fixed prices.

</details>


### [158] [Interpretable Fuzzy Rule-Based Regression Extension for Ex-Fuzzy Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20277)
*Cayan Deniz Kucuktopana,Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Richard Pitts,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Ex-Fuzzy库提出了一个可解释回归扩展，实现了基于Mamdani模糊推理的标量后件直接从数据学习，并引入了基于模糊C均值聚类的目标感知划分初始化策略。在10个KEEL回归数据集上实验表明，高斯划分优于梯形划分，平均决定系数约0.86，生成10-15条人类可读规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在回归任务中虽具高预测精度，但在安全关键和受监管领域部署需要可解释性。模糊规则系统提供透明、语言显式的可解释模型，但Mamdani风格模糊回归在现代机器学习软件库中仍显不足。

Method: 提出Ex-Fuzzy库的可解释回归扩展，支持带标量后件的Mamdani模糊推理。引入基于模糊C均值聚类的目标感知划分初始化策略，从增广输入-输出空间导出语言变量以强调输出相关特征区域。在10个KEEL回归数据集上对比高斯与梯形划分策略，基线包括线性回归、多层感知机和随机森林。

Result: 高斯划分始终优于均匀梯形划分，平均决定系数约0.86，同时产生10-15条紧凑的人类可读规则库。

Conclusion: 该实现提供了一个透明且具竞争力的黑盒回归模型替代方案，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时支持实用的可解释性。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve high predictive accuracy in regression tasks, but their deployment in safety-critical and regulated domains requires interpretability. While fuzzy rule-based systems offer transparent, linguistically explicit interpretable models, Mamdani-style fuzzy regression remains underrepresented in modern machine learning software libraries. This paper presents an interpretable regression extension for the Ex-Fuzzy library, enabling Mamdani fuzzy inference with scalar consequents learned directly from data. For this, a target-aware partition initialisation strategy based on Fuzzy C-Means clustering is introduced, in which linguistic variables are derived from an augmented input-output space to emphasise output-relevant regions of the feature space. The proposed extension is evaluated on ten regression datasets from the KEEL repository, comparing Gaussian and trapezoidal partition strategies against standard baselines including linear regression, multilayer perceptron, and random forests. Experimental results show that Gaussian partitions consistently outperform uniform trapezoidal partitions, achieving a mean coefficient of determination of approximately 0.86 while producing compact rule bases of 10-15 human-readable rules. The proposed implementation provides a transparent and competitive alternative to black-box regression models, supporting practical interpretability with competitive predictive performance.

</details>


### [159] [Classical Hardware Acceleration of Quantum Autoencoders for Real-Time Anomaly Detection in Collider Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20302)
*Ivan Ge,Sagar Addepalli,Abhilasha Dave,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了变分量子自编码器模型在高能物理实验实时异常检测触发器中的应用，并实现了其在FPGA上的综合部署，性能媲美最先进的经典方法，满足未来对撞机触发系统的资源和时序约束，为量子机器学习在高能物理触发系统中的工程化落地提供了首批FPGA实现案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习（QML）能以更少参数高效表示高维对撞机数据中的长程高阶相关性，具有良好的扩展性。将其部署于实时对撞机触发系统，需要在经典硬件上模拟编译量子电路，并将量子门综合到低延迟的FPGA加速器上，以适应现有数据采集流程并推进量子就绪基础设施建设。

Method: 构建变分量子自编码器模型用于实时异常检测触发；在经典计算机上模拟和编译量子电路，随后将生成的量子门综合映射到FPGA硬件加速器上，验证资源占用和时序性能。

Result: 模型性能达到最先进经典方法的可比水平；FPGA综合后满足未来对撞机触发应用的资源使用和时序约束。

Conclusion: 该工作为高能物理触发系统提供了首批量子机器学习模型的FPGA实现，使更高能力的模型能在当前经典数据采集管线中运行，同时推动了对撞机实验基础设施的量子就绪进程。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) algorithms in high energy physics (HEP) can efficiently represent and leverage long-range, high-order correlations in high-dimensional collider data, potentially with fewer parameters and favorable scaling relative to classical models. Deployment of QML in real-time collider applications such as trigger systems requires the ability to emulate and compile quantum circuits classically, then synthesize the resulting quantum gates onto low-latency hardware accelerators, namely field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs). We present a study of variational quantum autoencoder models for real-time anomaly detection triggers in modern collider experiments. The models achieve performance comparable to state-of-the-art classical approaches and, after FPGA synthesis, satisfy resource usage and timing constraints consistent with trigger applications in future colliders. This work provides one of the first FPGA implementations of QML models for HEP triggers, enabling higher-capability models in today's classical data acquisition pipelines while advancing quantum readiness of collider experiment infrastructure.

</details>


### [160] [Multi-modal transformer for signal classification in nanopore blockade experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20323)
*Sandro Kuppel,Julian Hoßbach,Samuel Tovey,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种多模态深度学习架构，结合原始时间序列、小波图像和静态特征向量，显著提升了纳米孔传感器对肽段和氨基酸的识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米孔传感器检测信号高度复杂，难以可靠地将信号归因于特定分子，现有方法在分子识别准确率上受限。

Method: 提出多模态深度学习架构，联合处理原始时间序列数据、基于小波变换的图像表示和静态特征向量，通过注意力机制整合多模态互补信息。

Result: 在42种肽段基准测试中准确率超越现有方法10个百分点以上；迁移至20种氨基酸数据集实现近乎完美的识别准确率；注意力分析揭示时间序列与小波图像输入捕捉同一事件的不同特征。

Conclusion: 多模态深度学习能够实现纳米孔传感器稳健、高精度的分子识别，展示了机器学习在单分子感知诊断中的巨大应用潜力。

Abstract: Nanopore devices have emerged as powerful tools for single-molecule sensing, with potential for rapid, portable diagnostics. They detect changes in ionic current as analytes enter nanometer-scale pores, providing a means of identifying diverse biomarkers from their characteristic signal patterns. However, these signals are highly complex, and reliably assigning them to specific molecules remains a major challenge. Here, we address this by introducing a multi-modal deep learning architecture that jointly processes multiple signal representations, including raw time-series data, wavelet-based images, and static feature vectors. Our approach surpasses existing methods by more than 10 percentage points on a 42-peptide benchmark and transfers to a 20-amino-acid dataset with near-perfect accuracy. The model integrates complementary information from these representations, with attention analysis showing that the time-series and wavelet-image inputs emphasize different features of the same event. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of machine learning to enable robust, high-accuracy molecular identification with nanopore sensors.

</details>


### [161] [Interval and fuzzy physics-augmented neural networks (iPANN and fPANN) for uncertainty quantification and propagation in constitutive modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20339)
*Somesh Pratap Singh,Govinda Anantha Padmanabha,Jingye Tan,Steven Yang,Reese E. Jones,D. Thomas Seidl,Nikolaos Bouklas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出区间和模糊物理增强神经网络（iPANNs 和 fPANNs），用于在稀疏、噪声和异质应力-变形数据下实现不确定性感知的超弹性本构建模。iPANNs 学习稀疏的下界、均值和上界自由能量密度分支，通过自动微分得到的应力包围噪声观测；fPANNs 通过 α-截集插值将 iPANNs 分支嵌入模糊集表示，生成嵌套的可容许响应族。两者均编码力学约束（客观性、一致性、多凸性）并采用平滑 L0 正则化促进可解释能量表达。采用两阶段迁移学习训练：先学习稀疏均值本构响应，再微调为上下界能量分支。在合成各向同性超弹性数据（含异方差噪声、不同随机实现、偏移噪声均值、变化噪声幅度）上验证，结果表明学习到的界包围噪声应力观测并泛化至测试集；有限元仿序中进一步考察了均值、上下界预测的不确定性传播。该框架为超弹性本构建模及其下游有限元仿真提供了紧凑、物理一致的非分布假设偶然不确定性量化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本构建模中的不确定性量化仍是可靠力学仿真的核心挑战，尤其在可用应力-变形数据稀疏、含噪或异质时。现有方法往往依赖分布假设，难以在物理约束下直接从噪声数据中学习确定性界或模糊集表示，且缺乏可解释的能量表达与不确定性传播至下游有限元仿真的能力。

Method: 提出 iPANNs 和 fPANNs。iPANNs 学习稀疏的下界、均值、上界自由能量密度分支，应力通过自动微分获得，确保包围噪声观测。fPANNs 利用 α-截集插值将 iPANNs 分支嵌入模糊集，形成嵌套可容许响应族。两者均强制满足客观性、一致性，并通过平滑 L0 正则化促进多凸性和可解释能量表达。训练采用两阶段迁移学习：第一阶段学习稀疏均值本构响应，第二阶段微调为上下界能量分支。

Result: 在合成各向同性超弹性数据（含异方差噪声、多种随机实现、偏移噪声均值、变化噪声幅度）上验证：学习到的上下界成功包围噪声应力观测，并泛化至测试集。有限元仿真中展示了均值、上下界预测的不确定性传播效果。框架实现了非分布假设的偶然不确定性量化，且保持物理一致性和可解释性。

Conclusion: iPANNs/fPANNs 为超弹性本构建模提供了紧凑、物理一致的不确定性感知建模框架，能够从稀疏噪声数据中直接学习确定性区间界和模糊集表示，并支持不确定性在下游有限元仿真中的传播，为无分布假设的偶然不确定性量化提供了新途径。

Abstract: Constitutive modeling under uncertainty remains a central challenge for reliable mechanics simulations, particularly when the available stress-deformation data are sparse, noisy, or heterogeneous. We propose interval and fuzzy physics-augmented neural networks (iPANNs and fPANNs) for uncertainty-aware hyperelastic constitutive modeling. iPANNs learn sparse lower, mean, and upper free energy density branches whose stresses, obtained by automatic differentiation, ultimately enclose noisy stress observations. In contrast to this deterministic interval description, fPANNs embed the learned iPANN branches into a fuzzy-set representation through alpha-cut interpolation, yielding a nested family of admissible responses. iPANNs and fPANNs encode mechanistic constraints - preserving objectivity, consistency and promoting polyconvexity - and smoothed L0 regularization promotes interpretable energy representations. The bound models are trained through a two-stage transfer-learning procedure in which a sparse mean constitutive response is learned first and then fine-tuned into lower and upper energy branches. We evaluate the framework on synthetic isotropic hyperelastic data with heteroscedastic noise, varying random realizations, shifted noise means, and varying noise magnitudes. The results show that the learned bounds enclose noisy stress observations while generalizing to the test set. Further, we examine the propagation of uncertainty through the mean, upper and lower bound predictions of the learned iPANN models in a finite element setting. The proposed framework provides a compact, physics-consistent route for distribution-free aleatoric uncertainty quantification in hyperelastic constitutive modeling, and propagation in downstream finite element simulations.

</details>


### [162] [Variance-reduced Domain Adaptation using Paired Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20367)
*Andrea Napoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对无监督领域自适应（UDA）中相关性对齐和最大均值差异损失在小批量优化时的高方差问题，提出了一种名为 PSDA（成对采样领域自适应）的新型随机方差缩减技术，通过域内及跨域配对形成四元组同步采样，最小化期望梯度方差，并在三个领域偏移数据集上验证了目标域准确率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 UDA 分布匹配框架（如相关性对齐和最大均值差异）在小批量优化设置下存在高方差问题，且缺乏有限和结构，无法直接应用经典的随机方差缩减（SVR）方法，导致训练效果受限。

Method: 提出 PSDA 方法：将观测样本在域内和跨域进行配对，形成四元组并始终一起采样；配对设计旨在最小化期望梯度方差，归结为求解一组线性分配问题。

Result: 仿真实验显示 PSDA 相比相关方法显著降低了方差；在三个领域偏移数据集上的实验表明，PSDA 能够提升目标域的分类准确率。

Conclusion: PSDA 通过成对采样策略有效解决了 UDA 损失函数的高方差问题，兼容小批量优化，为无监督领域自适应提供了一个实用且理论合理的方差缩减方案。

Abstract: Correlation alignment and the maximum mean discrepancy are two widely used distribution-matching frameworks for unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA). However, high variance in these losses has been shown to undermine their effectiveness in minibatch optimisation settings. Furthermore, the losses lack finite-sum structure, which renders them incompatible with classical stochastic variance reduction (SVR) methods. This paper proposes Paired Sampling for Domain Adaptation (PSDA), a novel SVR technique tailored to such objectives. PSDA pairs observations both within and across domains, to form quadruplets that are always sampled together during training. The pairings are designed to minimise expected gradient variance, and reduce to solving a set of linear assignment problems. Our simulations demonstrate reduced variance compared to related methods, and experiments on three domain shift datasets show improved target domain accuracy.

</details>


### [163] [Online Variance Reduction for Domain Adaptation on Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20374)
*Andrea Napoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ARROW（自适应方差缩减在线重加权）算法，这是首个针对最大均值差异（MMD）和相关性对齐（CORAL）损失函数的在线随机方差缩减（SVR）方法，适用于流式数据场景。该方法通过维护对齐统计量的移动平均参考，并自适应重加权传入的小批量数据以实现统计对齐，同时提出松弛重加权方案使优化问题可解。实验表明ARROW在运行时、方差缩减程度和目标域准确率方面与离线算法具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的针对MMD和CORAL损失的离线SVR算法不兼容在线、分布式或增量学习设置，缺乏能够处理流式数据的在线方差缩减方法。

Method: ARROW算法：1）维护对齐统计量（MMD/CORAL）的移动平均参考；2）自适应重加权传入的小批量数据，使小批量统计量与参考统计量对齐；3）提出松弛重加权方案，将重加权优化问题转化为可解的凸优化问题。

Result: 在实验和仿真中，ARROW在运行时效率、方差缩减程度和目标域分类准确率三方面均与离线SVR算法表现相当或更优，验证了其在流式数据场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: ARROW填补了MMD和CORAL损失在线随机方差缩减的空白，为流式数据下的域适应和分布对齐提供了首个可行的在线SVR解决方案，且保持了与离线方法相当的性能。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of stochastic variance reduction (SVR) for the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) and correlation alignment (CORAL) loss functions. Although various offline SVR algorithms for these losses have been proposed, these are incompatible with online, distributed, or incremental learning settings. This paper presents Adaptive vaRiance Reduction via Online reWeighting (ARROW), the first online SVR algorithm for the MMD and CORAL for streamed data. The method maintains moving average references of the alignment statistics, and adaptively reweights incoming minibatches so that the minibatch and reference statistics are aligned. Further, we propose a relaxed reweighting scheme so that the ensuing weight-optimisation problem is tractable. In experiments and simulations, we show that ARROW performs competitively with offline algorithms in terms of runtime, degree of variance reduction achieved, and target domain accuracy.

</details>


### [164] [PG-KINN: A Physics-Informed Petrov-Galerkin Kolmogorov-Arnold Network for Solving Forward and Inverse PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20378)
*Amirhossein Sadr,Nima Soltani,Vahideh Moghtadaiee,Aida Pakniyat,Dara Rahmati,Saeid Gorgin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PG-KINN 结合 Petrov-Galerkin 方法与 KAN 试验空间，通过分段多项式检验函数降低求导阶数，实现对非自伴、非线性及逆问题的高精度求解，在多个力学基准测试中超越 MLP 与现有 KAN 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理信息神经网络主要基于 MLP，存在谱偏置和参数冗余问题；KAN 虽缓解这些问题，但损失函数构造方式（强形式、能量形式、边界积分形式）各有局限，如高阶导数需求、仅适用自伴算子、参数识别退化、需已知基本解等。

Method: 提出 PG-KINN，采用 Petrov-Galerkin 变分形式：试验空间用 KAN，检验空间用独立、紧支撑、分段多项式基函数，配合 Gauss-Legendre 数值积分。通过分部积分降低求导阶数，将整体残差转化为单元级弱残差，改善条件数。

Result: 在裂尖奇异性、应力集中、Neo-Hookean 超弹性、非均匀介质逆参数识别、复杂几何等基准测试中，PG-KINN 持续优于 MLP 基线及现有基于 KAN 的强形式/能量形式/逆问题方法（PIKAN）。

Conclusion: 基于 KAN 试验空间与多项式检验空间的 Petrov-Galerkin 耦合，为 AI 驱动的计算力学提供了一条鲁棒且高精度的新路径。

Abstract: Physics-informed learning of partial differential equations (PDEs) has been dominated by multilayer perceptrons (MLPs), whose spectral bias and dense parameterization limit both accuracy and interpretability. Kolmogorov Arnold Networks (KANs) mitigate these limitations because their learnable spline activations are structurally aligned with the piecewise-polynomial bases of classical discretizations. However, the way a PDE is cast into a loss functional is as decisive as the choice of approximator: strong-form residual minimization requires high-order derivatives and heavily weighted losses, the energy (Bubnov-Galerkin) form is restricted to self-adjoint operators and, as we show, collapses to a trivial solution for parameter-identification problems, and boundary integral forms require a known fundamental solution. We propose PG-KINN, a physics-informed KAN built on a Petrov-Galerkin formulation in which the trial space is a KAN and the test space is an independent, compactly supported, piecewise-polynomial space evaluated with Gauss-Legendre quadrature. Integration by parts lowers the differentiation order while retaining applicability to general non-self-adjoint, nonlinear, and inverse problems; the localized test functions turn the global residual into a set of element-wise weak residuals with favorable conditioning. On a suite of benchmarks spanning crack singularities, stress concentration, Neo-Hookean hyperelasticity, inverse parameter identification in heterogeneous media, and complex geometries, PG-KINN consistently outperforms legacy MLP baselines and state-of-the-art KAN-based strong/energy/inverse formulations (PIKAN). These results position the Petrov-Galerkin coupling of KAN trial spaces and polynomial test spaces as a robust and accurate route for AI-based computational mechanics.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Personalized Recommendation Tool Learning via Autonomous Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19739)
*Mingdai Yang,Zhiwei Liu,Weizhi Zhang,Yibo Wang,Hao Peng,Philip Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PRTA框架，让LLM作为中央规划师与多个推荐模型作为工具交互，通过架构设计而非修改LLM本身，解决LLM在全排序推荐任务中的幻觉和上下文长度限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的推荐代理存在幻觉和上下文长度限制，不适合全排序推荐任务。作者希望通过架构设计而非修改LLM本身来规避这些限制。

Method: 提出PRTA（基于记忆的个性化推荐工具学习）框架：LLM作为中央规划师负责高层推理和个性化工具选择，传统推荐模型作为工具执行全排序打分；设计反思机制，基于用户画像和候选排序列表评估和比较工具，支持个性化工具选择。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，PRTA在提升全排序推荐性能方面优于传统推荐方法和基于LLM的基线方法。

Conclusion: 结合LLM规划能力与传统推荐模型可扩展性的架构设计，有效解决了LLM在全排序推荐任务中的局限性，实现了更优的推荐性能。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) have recently gained traction in recommender systems due to their strong reasoning capabilities and extensive world knowledge, previous LLM-based agents suffer from hallucination and context-length limitations, and thus are not suitable for full-ranking recommendation tasks. To circumvent these limitations through architectural design rather than modifying the LLM itself, we propose an agent-based recommendation framework, memory-based $\textbf{P}$ersonalized $\textbf{R}$ecommendation $\textbf{T}$ool learning via autonomous language $\textbf{A}$gents (PRTA), in which an LLM acts as a central planner interacting with multiple recommendation models as tools. The LLM-based agent is responsible for high-level reasoning and personalized tool selection, while traditional recommendation models perform full-ranking scoring, leveraging their scalability in modeling behavioral patterns. To support personalized tool selection, we design reflection mechanisms that enable the agent to evaluate and compare tools for each user based on user profiles and candidate ranked lists. Extensive experiments across three public datasets demonstrate the superiority of \modelname over traditional recommendation and LLM-based baselines in improving full-ranking recommendation performance.

</details>


### [166] [Zero-Observation User Reactivation with Gap-Driven Dimensional Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19802)
*Jiandong Ding,Tianying Liu,Fuyuan Liu,Huijie Qin,Tiandeng Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出DeltaGate，一种轻量级输出层插件，解决序列推荐中用户长期不活跃后回归的“零观察重激活”问题。通过冻结主干网络，引入基于时间间隙Δt和个性化表示的门控机制，在个性化历史与全局先验间路由表示维度。在三个Amazon数据集上，DeltaGate显著提升长间隙（>365天）下的Hit@10，参数量仅增加2-4%，且保持主干零漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐模型假设用户行为连续观测，但实际中用户可能数月或数年无交互（宏观间隙Δt），导致模型性能随间隙增长单调下降。论文定义Zero-Observation Reactivation设置，旨在利用间隙前历史和间隙时长预测回归用户偏好，而不依赖间隙期间的行为信号。

Method: 提出DeltaGate插件：冻结主干网络（如SASRec、BERT4Rec），在输出层引入门控机制。门控以Δt和个性化表示为条件，将每个表示维度在个性化历史嵌入和零初始化的全局先验之间路由。门控网络轻量（约66K参数），支持诊断性实验（固定个性化表示，仅变Δt）。

Result: 在Video Games、CDs & Vinyl、Movies & TV三个数据集上，Hit@10随间隙桶单调下降。在>365天桶中，DG-SASRec达0.047 vs SASRec 0.031；DG-BERT4Rec达0.046 vs BERT4Rec 0.025。端到端重训虽绝对精度更高，但改变主干嵌入；冻结插件保持零主干漂移，参数量减少约40倍，且保留可观测的维度级路由。

Conclusion: DeltaGate为长间隙用户重激活提供高效、可解释的解决方案：无需微调主干，仅通过轻量门控利用Δt信息融合个性化历史与全局先验，显著提升长尾间隙下的推荐性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation (SR) models capture continuously observed behavior, but a returning user may have no interactions for months or years. We define this setting as Zero-Observation Reactivation: the user has a pre-gap history, while the platform observes no behavioral signals during a macro-gap Delta t. Under a chronologically aligned Gap-Synthesize Protocol on three Amazon datasets (Video Games, CDs & Vinyl, and Movies & TV), Hit@10 decreases monotonically across the evaluated gap buckets and reaches its lowest level beyond one year. The pattern appears across recurrent, unidirectional, and bidirectional SR backbones.
  We propose DeltaGate, a lightweight output-layer plugin that keeps the backbone frozen and routes each representation dimension between the personalized history and a learned, zero-initialized global prior. The gate is conditioned jointly on Delta t and the personalized representation. In a controlled diagnostic, we hold the personalized representation fixed and vary Delta t to isolate the trained gate's response to the gap input. In the >365d Video Games bucket, DG-SASRec reaches 0.047 Hit@10 versus 0.031 for SASRec, while DG-BERT4Rec reaches 0.046 versus 0.025 for BERT4Rec, with 66K trainable parameters (2--4% overhead). End-to-end retraining attains higher absolute accuracy but changes the backbone embeddings; the frozen plugin preserves zero backbone drift, uses about 40x fewer trainable parameters, and retains observable dimension-wise routing. The source code is available at https://github.com/jdding/DeltaGate.

</details>


### [167] [UniRank: Benchmarking Ranking Models for Unified Sequential Modeling and Feature Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19987)
*Honghao Li,Xianquan Wang,Zibin Zhang,Yi Zhang,Kangyi Lin,Yiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UniRank 是一个开源的统一排序模型基准，解决了学术界缺乏可复现、大规模、统一序列建模与特征交互的排序模型评测平台的问题。它提供了标准化的评测流程、高效的 PyTorch 工具包，并在 5 个大规模公开数据集上对 15 个代表性模型进行了基准测试，最大数据集超 7 亿样本，最长序列超 10^5 交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代排序模型越来越多地融合序列建模与特征交互，但多数进展依赖专有数据、闭源实现和大规模工业基础设施，导致学术界难以进行可复现的对比、缩放定律研究、长序列建模和多任务排序研究。

Method: 提出 UniRank 基准：采用时间顺序的逐点自回归监督，跨反馈任务标准化评测；提供支持分布式数据并行、算子优化、混合精度训练、注意力优化等效率技术的 PyTorch 工具包，降低硬件需求。在短视频、广告、电商 5 个大规模公开数据集上基准测试 15 个代表性统一排序模型。

Result: 最大数据集含 7 亿+ 样本，最长行为序列超 10^5 交互。UniRank 为统一排序模型的可复现对比、有限算力下的缩放定律研究、缩小学术与工业排序研究差距提供了基础。代码和数据已开源。

Conclusion: UniRank 通过可复现实验、面向生产的评测和易用的实现，惠及研究者、从业者和初学者，推动了统一排序模型的学术研究向工业级规模和标准靠拢。

Abstract: Ranking is a core stage in online advertising and recommender systems. Modern ranking models increasingly unify sequential modeling and feature interaction, yet many advances rely on proprietary data, closed implementations, and large-scale industrial infrastructure. This setting limits reproducible comparison and hinders academic study of scaling laws, long-sequence modeling, and multi-task ranking. To address these limitations, this paper proposes UniRank, an open benchmark for ranking models that unify sequential modeling and feature interaction. UniRank uses chronological pointwise autoregressive supervision, standardizes evaluation across feedback tasks, and provides a PyTorch toolkit with Distributed Data Parallel training, operator optimization, mixed-precision training, attention optimization, and other efficiency techniques that reduce hardware requirements. We benchmark 15 representative unified ranking models on five large-scale public datasets from short-video, advertising, and e-commerce platforms, with the largest dataset containing over 700 million instances and the longest behavior sequence exceeding 10^5 interactions. UniRank provides a reproducible basis for comparing unified ranking models, studying scaling laws under limited compute, and narrowing the gap between academic and industrial ranking research. We believe UniRank benefits researchers, practitioners, and beginners through reproducible experiments, production-oriented evaluation, and accessible implementations. Code and data are available at https://github.com/salmon1802/UniRank.

</details>
